




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

khuddakanikāyai

naittippakaraṇa-aṭṭhakathā

ganthārambhakathā

mahākāruṇikaṃ  nāthaṃ, ñaiyyasāgarapāraguṃ|

vandai nipuṇagambhīra-vicitranayadaisanaṃ||

vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nā, yaina niyyanti laukatau|

vandai tamuttamaṃ dhammaṃ, sammāsambuddhapūjitaṃ||

sīlādiguṇasampan‍nau, ṭhitau maggaphalaisu yau|

vandai ariyasaṅghaṃ taṃ, puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ anuttaraṃ||

vandanājanitaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, iti yaṃ ratanattayai|

hatantarāyau sabbattha, hutvāhaṃ tassa taijasā||

ṭhitiṃ  ākaṅkhamānaina, ciraṃ saddhammanaittiyā|

dhammarakkhitanāmaina, thairaina abhiyācitau||

padumuttaranāthassa, pādamūlai pavattitaṃ|

passatā abhinīhāraṃ, sampattaṃ yassa matthakaṃ||

saṃkhittaṃ vibhajantānaṃ, aisau aggauti tādinā|

ṭhapitau aitadaggasmiṃ, yau mahāsāvakuttamau||

chaḷabhiñ‍ñau vasippattau, pabhin‍napaṭisambhidau|

mahākac‍cāyanau thairau, sambuddhaina pasaṃsitau||

taina  yā bhāsitā naitti, satthārā anumauditā|

sāsanassa sadāyattā, navaṅgassatthavaṇṇanā||

tassā gambhīrañāṇaihi, augāhaitabbabhāvatau|

kiñ‍cāpi duk‍karā kātuṃ, atthasaṃvaṇṇanā mayā||

saha saṃvaṇṇanaṃ yasmā, dharatai satthusāsanaṃ|

pubbācariyasīhānaṃ, tiṭṭhataiva vinicchayau||

tasmā tamupanissāya, augāhaitvāna pañ‍capi|

nikāyai paiṭakaināpi, saṃsanditvā yathābalaṃ||

suvisuddhamasaṃkiṇṇaṃ, nipuṇatthavinicchayaṃ|

mahāvihāravāsīnaṃ, samayaṃ avilaumayaṃ||

pamādalaikhaṃ vaj‍jaitvā, pāḷiṃ sammā niyaujayaṃ|

upadaisaṃ vibhāvaintau, karissāmatthavaṇṇanaṃ||

iti  atthaṃ asaṅkiṇṇaṃ, naittippakaraṇassa mai|

vibhajantassa sak‍kac‍caṃ, nisāmayatha sādhavauti||

tattha kainaṭṭhaina naitti? saddhammanayanaṭṭhaina naitti| yathā hi taṇhā sattai kāmādibhavaṃ nayatīti ‘‘bhavanaittī’’ti vuc‍cati, aivamayampi vainaiyyasattai ariyadhammaṃ nayatīti saddhammanayanaṭṭhaina ‘‘naittī’’ti vuc‍cati| atha vā nayanti tāyāti naitti| naittippakaraṇaina hi karaṇabhūtaina dhammakathikā vainaiyyasattai dassanamaggaṃ nayanti sampāpaintīti, nīyanti vā aittha aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai adhiṭṭhānabhūtai patiṭṭhāpaitvā vainaiyyā nibbānaṃ sampāpiyantīti naitti| na hi naittiupadaisasan‍nissayaina vinā aviparītasuttatthāvabaudhau sambhavati| tathā hi vuttaṃ – ‘‘tasmā nibbāyitukāmainā’’tiādi| sabbāpi hi suttassa atthasaṃvaṇṇanā naittiupadaisāyattā, naitti ca suttappabhavā, suttaṃ sammāsambuddhappabhavanti|

sā panāyaṃ naitti pakaraṇaparicchaidatau tippabhaidā hāranayapaṭṭhānānaṃ vasaina| paṭhamañhi hāravicārau, tatau nayavicārau, pacchā paṭṭhānavicārauti| pāḷivavatthānatau pana saṅgahavāravibhāgavāravasaina duvidhā| sabbāpi hi naitti saṅgahavārau vibhāgavārauti vāradvayamaiva hauti|

tattha  saṅgahavārau āditau pañ‍cahi gāthāhi paricchin‍nau| sabbau hi pakaraṇatthau ‘‘yaṃ laukau pūjayatai’’tiādīhi pañ‍cahi gāthāhi apariggahitau nāma natthi| nanu caittha paṭṭhānaṃ asaṅgahitanti? nayidamaivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, mūlapadaggahaṇaina paṭṭhānassa saṅgahitattā| tathā hi vakkhati – ‘‘aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā kuhiṃ daṭṭhabbā sāsanapaṭṭhānai’’ti| mūlapadapaṭṭhānāni hi atthanayasaṅkhārattikā viya añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ saṅgahitāni|

vibhāgavārau pana uddaisaniddaisapaṭiniddaisavasaina tividhau| taisu ‘‘tattha katamai sauḷasa hārā’’ti ārabhitvā yāva ‘‘bhavanti aṭṭhārasa padānī’’ti ayaṃ uddaisavārau| ‘‘assādādīnavatā’’ti ārabhitvā yāva ‘‘taittiṃsā aittikā naittī’’ti ayaṃ niddaisavārau| paṭiniddaisavārau pana hāravibhaṅgavārau hārasampātavārau nayasamuṭṭhānavārau sāsanapaṭṭhānavārauti catubbidhau| taisu ‘‘tattha katamau daisanāhārau’’ti ārabhitvā yāva ‘‘ayaṃ pahānaina samāraupanā’’ti ayaṃ hāravibhaṅgavārau| tattha ‘‘katamau daisanāhārasampātau’’ti ārabhitvā yāva ‘‘anupādisaisā ca nibbānadhātū’’ti ayaṃ hārasampātavārau| aitthāha – hāravibhaṅgahārasampātavārānaṃ kiṃ nānākaraṇanti? vuc‍catai – yattha anaikaihipi udāharaṇasuttaihi aikau  hārau niddisīyati, ayaṃ hāravibhaṅgavārau| yattha pana aikasmiṃ suttai anaikai hārā sampatanti, ayaṃ hārasampātavārau| vuttañhaitaṃ paiṭakai –

‘‘yattha ca sabbai hārā, sampatamānā nayanti suttatthaṃ|

byañ‍janavidhiputhuttā, sā bhūmī hārasampātau’’ti||

nayasamuṭṭhānasāsanapaṭṭhānavāravibhāgau pākaṭau aiva| sāsanapaṭṭhānavārau pana saṅgahavārai viya uddaisaniddaisavāraisupi na sarūpatau uddhaṭauti| aitthāha – ‘‘idaṃ naittippakaraṇaṃ mahāsāvakabhāsitaṃ, bhagavatā anumaudita’’nti ca kathamaitaṃ viñ‍ñāyatīti? pāḷitau aiva| na hi pāḷitau añ‍ñaṃ pamāṇataraṃ atthi| yā hi catūhi mahāpadaisaihi aviruddhā pāḷi, sā pamāṇaṃ| tathā hi agarahitāya ācariyaparamparāya paiṭakaupadaisau viya idaṃ naittippakaraṇaṃ ābhataṃ| yadi aivaṃ kasmāssa nidānaṃ na vuttaṃ| sāvakabhāsitānampi hi subhasutta- (dī॰ ni॰ 1.444 ādayau) anaṅgaṇasutta- (ma॰ ni॰ 1.57 ādayau) kac‍cāyanasaṃyuttādīnaṃ  nidānaṃ bhāsitanti? nayidaṃ aikantikaṃ| sāvakabhāsitānaṃ buddhabhāsitānampi hi aikac‍cānaṃ paṭisambhidāmagganiddaisādīnaṃ dhammapadabuddhavaṃsādīnañ‍ca nidānaṃ na bhāsitaṃ, na ca tāvatā tāni appamāṇaṃ, aivamidhāpi daṭṭhabbaṃ|

nidānañ‍ca nāma suttavinayānaṃ dhammabhaṇḍāgārikaupālitthairādīhi mahāsāvakaihaiva bhāsitaṃ, idañ‍ca mahāsāvakabhāsitaṃ, thairaṃ muñ‍citvā anañ‍ñavisayattā imissā vicāraṇāyāti kimaitaina nidānagavaisanaina, atthauyaivaittha gavaisitabbau, yau pāḷiyā aviruddhauti| atha vā pāḷiyā atthasaṃvaṇṇanābhāvatau na imassa pakaraṇassa visuṃ nidānavacanakic‍caṃ atthi, paṭisambhidāmagganiddaisādīnaṃ viyāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

idāni aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai nānappakārakausal‍latthaṃ ayaṃ vibhāgau vaiditabbau – sabbamaiva caitaṃ pakaraṇaṃ sāsanapariyaiṭṭhibhāvatau aikavidhaṃ, tathā ariyamaggasampādanatau vimuttirasatau ca| byañ‍janatthavicārabhāvatau duvidhaṃ, tathā saṅgahavibhāgabhāvatau dhammavinayatthasaṃvaṇṇanatau laukiyalaukuttaratthasaṅgahaṇatau rūpārūpadhammapariggāhakatau lakkhaṇalakkhiyabhāvatau pavattinivattivacanatau sabhāgavisabhāganiddaisatau sādhāraṇāsādhāraṇadhammavibhāgatau ca|

tividhaṃ puggalattayaniddaisatau tividhakalyāṇavibhāgatau pariñ‍ñattayakathanatau pahānattayūpadaisatau  sikkhattayasaṅgahaṇatau tividhasaṃkilaisavisaudhanatau mūlagītianugītisaṅgītibhaidatau piṭakattayatthasaṃvaṇṇanatau hāranayapaṭṭhānappabhaidatau ca|

catubbidhaṃ catuppaṭisambhidāvisayatau catunayadaisanatau dhammatthadaisanāpaṭivaidhagambhīrabhāvatau ca| pañ‍cavidhaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyādidhammavibhāgatau pañ‍cakkhandhaniddaisatau pañ‍cagatiparicchaidatau pañ‍canikāyatthavivaraṇatau ca| chabbidhaṃ chaḷārammaṇavibhāgatau chaajjhattikabāhirāyatanavibhāgatau ca| sattavidhaṃ sattaviñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiparicchaidatau| navavidhaṃ suttādinavaṅganiddaisatau| cuddasavidhaṃ suttādhiṭṭhānavibhāgatau| sauḷasavidhaṃ aṭṭhavīsatividhañ‍ca sāsanapaṭṭhānappabhaidatau| caturāsītisahassavidhaṃ caturāsītisahassadhammakkhandhavicārabhāvatautiādinā nayaina pakaraṇavibhāgau vaiditabbau|

tattha sāsanapariyaiṭṭhibhāvatauti sakalaṃ naittippakaraṇaṃ sikkhattayasaṅgahassa navaṅgassa satthusāsanassa atthasaṃvaṇṇanābhāvatau| ariyamaggasampādanatauti  dassanabhūmibhāvanābhūmisampādanatau| vimuttirasatauti sāsanassa amatapariyausānattā vuttaṃ| byañ‍janatthavicārabhāvatauti hārabyañ‍janapadakammanayānaṃ byañ‍janavicārattā atthapadaatthanayānaṃ atthavicārattā vuttaṃ| saṅgahavibhāgabhāvau paratau āvi bhavissati| dhammavinayatthasaṃvaṇṇanatauti sakalassāpi pariyattisāsanassa dhammavinayabhāvatau vuttaṃ| lakkhaṇalakkhiyabhāvatauti naittivacanassa lakkhaṇattā udāharaṇasuttānañ‍ca lakkhiyattā vuttaṃ| sabhāgavisabhāganiddaisatauti samānajātiyā dhammā sabhāgā, paṭipakkhā visabhāgā, taṃvicārabhāvatauti atthau| sādhāraṇāsādhāraṇadhammavibhāgatauti pahānaikaṭṭhasahajaikaṭṭhatādisāmañ‍ñaina yai dhammā yaisaṃ dhammānaṃ nāmavatthādinā sādhāraṇā tabbidhuratāya asādhāraṇā ca, taṃvibhāgatau duvidhanti atthau|

puggalattayaniddaisatauti ugghaṭitañ‍ñuādi puggalattayaniddaisatau| tividhakalyāṇavibhāgatauti ādikalyāṇādivibhāgatau| mūlagītianugītisaṅgītibhaidatauti paṭhamaṃ vacanaṃ mūlagīti, vuttassaiva atthassa saṅgahagāthā anugīti, taṃtaṃsuttatthayaujanavasaina vippakiṇṇassa pakaraṇassa saṅgāyanaṃ saṅgīti, sā thairassa paratau pavattitāti vaiditabbā, aitāsaṃ tissan‍naṃ bhaidatau tividhanti atthau| pañ‍cakkhandhaniddaisatauti rūpādipañ‍cakkhandhasīlādipañ‍cadhammakkhandhaniddaisatau pañ‍cavidhanti atthau| suttādhiṭṭhānavibhāgatauti laubhadausamauhānaṃ alaubhādausāmauhānaṃ kāyavacīmanaukammānaṃ saddhādipañ‍cindriyānañ‍ca vasaina cuddasavidhassa suttādhiṭṭhānassa vibhāgavacanatau cuddasavidhanti atthau| saisaṃ suviñ‍ñaiyyanti na papañ‍citaṃ|

1. saṅgahavāravaṇṇanā

aivaṃ  anaikabhaidavibhattai naittippakaraṇai yadidaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘saṅgahavibhāgavāravasaina duvidha’’nti, tattha saṅgahavārau ādi| tassāpi ‘‘yaṃ laukau pūjayatai’’ti ayaṃ gāthā ādi| tattha yanti aniyamatau upayauganiddaisau, tassa ‘‘tassā’’ti iminā niyamanaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| laukauti kattuniddaisau| pūjayataiti kiriyāniddaisau| salaukapālauti kattuvisaisanaṃ| sadāti kālaniddaisau| namassati cāti upacayaina kiriyāniddaisau| tassāti sāminiddaisau| aitanti pac‍cattaniddaisau| sāsanavaranti pac‍cattaniddaisaina niddiṭṭhadhammanidassanaṃ| vidūhīti karaṇavacanaina kattuniddaisau| ñaiyyanti kammavācakakiriyāniddaisau| naravarassāti ‘‘tassā’’ti niyamaitvā dassitassa sarūpatau dassanaṃ|

tattha laukiyanti aittha puñ‍ñāpuñ‍ñāni tabbipākau cāti laukau, pajā, sattanikāyauti atthau| lauka-saddau hi jātisaddatāya sāmañ‍ñavasaina niravasaisatau sattai saṅgaṇhāti| kiñ‍cāpi hi laukasaddau saṅkhārabhājanaisupi diṭṭhappayaugau, pūjanakiriyāyaugyabhūtatāvasaina pana sattalaukavacanau aiva idha gahitauti daṭṭhabbaṃ| pūjayataiti mānayati, apacāyatīti atthau|

laukaṃ pālaintīti laukapālā, cattārau mahārājānau| laukiyā pana indayamavaruṇakuvairā laukapālāti vadanti| saha laukapālaihīti salaukapālau, ‘‘laukau’’ti iminā tulyādhikaraṇaṃ| atha vā issariyādhipac‍caina taṃtaṃsattalaukassa pālanatau rakkhaṇatau khattiyacatumahārājasak‍kasuyāmasantusitasunimmitaparanimmitavasavattimahābrahmādayau laukapālā| taihi saha taṃtaṃsattanikāyau salaukapālau laukauti vuttau| atha vā ‘‘dvaimai, bhikkhavai, suk‍kā dhammā laukaṃ pālaintī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 2.9; itivu॰ 42) vacanatau hirauttappadhammā laukapālā| taihi saman‍nāgatau laukau salaukapālau| hirauttappasampan‍nā hi pāpagarahinau sappurisā dhammacchandavantatāya bhagavati pūjānamak‍kāraparā hauntīti|

sadāti  sabbakālaṃ rattiñ‍caiva divā ca, sadāti vā bhagavatau dharamānakālai tatau parañ‍ca| atha vā sadāti abhinīhāratau paṭṭhāya yāva sāsanantaradhānā, tatau parampi vā| mahābhinīhāratau paṭṭhāya hi mahābaudhisattā baudhiyā niyatatāya buddhaṅkurabhūtā sadaivakassa laukassa pūjanīyā caiva vandanīyā ca haunti| yathāha bhagavā sumaidhabhūtau –

‘‘dīpaṅkarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

mama kammaṃ pakittaitvā, dakkhiṇaṃ pādamuddhari||

‘‘yai  tatthāsuṃ jinaputtā, padakkhiṇamakaṃsu maṃ|

daivā manussā asurā ca, abhivādaitvāna pak‍kamu’’nti|| (bu॰ vaṃ॰ 2.75-76)|

namassati cāti kaici kaisañ‍ci pūjāsak‍kārādīni karauntāpi taisaṃ apākaṭaguṇatāya namak‍kāraṃ na karaunti, na aivaṃ bhagavatau, yathābhūtaabbhuggatakittisaddatāya pana bhagavantaṃ sadaivakau laukau pūjayati caiva namassati cāti atthau| ‘‘sadā naramanussau’’ti kaici paṭhanti, taṃ na sundaraṃ| tassāti yaṃ sadaivakau laukau pūjayati caiva namassati ca, tassa| aitanti idāni vattabbaṃ buddhiyaṃ viparivattamānaṃ sāmañ‍ñaina dassaiti| sāsanavaranti taṃ sarūpatau dassaiti| tattha diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikaparamatthaihi yathārahaṃ sattai sāsati vinaiti aitaināti sāsanaṃ, tadaiva aikantaniyyānaṭṭhaina anañ‍ñasādhāraṇaguṇatāya ca uttamaṭṭhaina taṃtaṃabhipatthitasamiddhihaitutāya paṇḍitaihi varitabbatau vā varaṃ, sāsanamaiva varanti sāsanavaraṃ| vidūhīti yathāsabhāvatau kammakammaphalāni kusalādibhaidai ca dhammai vidantīti vidū, paṇḍitamanussā, taihi| ñātabbaṃ, ñāṇamarahatīti vā ñaiyyaṃ| naravarassāti purisavarassa, aggapuggalassāti atthau|

idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yau anañ‍ñasādhāraṇamahākaruṇāsabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇādiguṇavisaisayaugaina sadaivakaina laukaina pūjanīyau namassanīyau ca bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, tassa laukai uttamapuggalassa aitaṃ idāni amhaihi vibhajitabbahāranayapaṭṭhānavicāraṇavisayabhūtaṃ sāsanaṃ ādikalyāṇatādiguṇasampattiyā varaṃ aggaṃ uttamaṃ nipuṇañāṇagaucaratāya paṇḍitavaidanīyamaivāti| bhagavatau hi vacanaṃ aikagāthāmattampi sac‍capaṭic‍casamuppādakhandhāyatanadhātindriyasatipaṭṭhānādisabhāvadhammaniddhāraṇakkhamatāya  sauḷasahārapañ‍canayasauḷasaaṭṭhavīsatividhapaṭṭhānavicārayaugyabhāvaina ca paramagambhīraṃ atthatau agādhapāraṃ saṇhasukhumañāṇavisayamaivāti| tainaivāha – ‘‘pañ‍ñavantassāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau duppañ‍ñassā’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 3.358; a॰ ni॰ 8.30)| atha vā bhagavatau sāsanaṃ pariñ‍ñāk‍kamaina lakkhaṇāvabaudhappaṭipattiyā suñ‍ñatamukhādīhi augāhitabbattā aviñ‍ñūnaṃ supinantainapi na visayau hautīti āha – ‘‘vidūhi ñaiyya’’nti| tathā ca vuttaṃ – ‘‘aitu viñ‍ñū purisau’’tiādi|

aparai  pana ‘‘taṃ tassa sāsanavara’’nti paṭhanti, taisaṃ mataina yaṃ-saddau sāsana-saddaina samānādhikaraṇauti daṭṭhabbau| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti yaṃ sāsanavaraṃ salaukapālau laukau pūjayati namassati ca, taṃ sāsanavaraṃ vidūhi ñātabbanti| imasmiñ‍ca nayai laukapāla-saddaina bhagavāpi vuc‍cati| bhagavā hi laukaggatāyakattā nippariyāyaina laukapālau, tasmā ‘‘tassā’’ti laukapālassa satthunauti atthau| salaukapālauti caittha laukapāla-saddau guṇībhūtaupi satthuvisayattā sāsana-saddāpaikkhatāya sāmibhāvaina sambandhīvisaisabhūtau padhānabhūtau viya paṭiniddaisaṃ arahatīti|

kathaṃ pana sayaṃ dhammassāmī bhagavā dhammaṃ pūjayatīti? nāyaṃ viraudhau| dhammagarunau hi buddhā bhagavantau, tai sabbakālaṃ dhammaṃ apacāyamānāva viharantīti| vuttañhaitaṃ – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ yvāyaṃ dhammau mayā abhisambuddhau, tamaiva dhammaṃ sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyya’’nti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.173; a॰ ni॰ 4.21)|

api ca bhagavatau dhammapūjanā sattasattāhappaṭipattiādīhi dīpaitabbā| dhammassāmīti ca dhammaina sadaivakassa laukassa sāmīti atthau, na dhammassa sāmīti| aivampi namassatīti vacanaṃ na yuj‍jati| na hi bhagavā kañ‍ci namassatīti, aisaupi niddausau| na hi namassatīti padassa namak‍kāraṃ karautīti ayamaiva atthau, atha khau garukaraṇaina tan‍nin‍nau tappauṇau tappabbhārauti ayampi atthau labbhati| bhagavā ca dhammagarutāya sabbakālaṃ dhammanin‍napauṇapabbhārabhāvaina viharatīti| ayañ‍ca atthau ‘‘yaina sudaṃ svāhaṃ nic‍cakappaṃ viharāmī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.387) aivamādīhi suttapadaihi dīpaitabbau| ‘‘vidūhi naiyya’’ntipi  pāṭhau, tassa paṇḍitaihi saparasantānaisu naitabbaṃ pāpaitabbanti atthau| tattha attasantānai pāpanaṃ bujjhanaṃ, parasantānai baudhananti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

aivaṃ bhagavatau sadaivakassa laukassa pūjanīyavandanīyabhāvau aggapuggalabhāvau ca vuc‍camānau guṇavisiṭṭhataṃ dīpaiti, sā ca guṇavisiṭṭhatā mahābaudhiyā vaiditabbā| āsavakkhayañāṇapadaṭṭhānañhi sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇapadaṭṭhānañ‍ca āsavakkhayañāṇaṃ ‘‘mahābaudhī’’ti vuc‍cati| sā aviparītadhammadaisanatau tathāgatai suppatiṭṭhitāti viñ‍ñāyati| na hi savāsananiravasaisakilaisappahānaṃ anāvaraṇañāṇañ‍ca vinā tādisī dhammadaisanā sambhavati| ic‍cassa catuvaisāraj‍jayaugau| taina dasabalachaasādhāraṇañāṇaaṭṭhārasāvaiṇikabuddhadhammādisakalasabbañ‍ñuguṇapāripūrī pakāsitā hauti| aitādisī ca guṇavibhūti mahākaruṇāpubbaṅgamaṃ abhinīhārasampattiṃ purassaraṃ katvā sampāditaṃ samattiṃsapāramisaṅkhātaṃ puñ‍ñañāṇasambhāramantaraina na upalabbhatīti haitusampadāpi atthatau vibhāvitā hautīti aivaṃ bhagavatau tīsupi avatthāsu sabbasattānaṃ aikantahitappaṭilābhahaitubhūtā ādimajjhapariyausānakalyāṇā niravasaisā buddhaguṇā imāya gāthāya pakāsitāti vaiditabbaṃ|

dutiyanayai pana yasmā sikkhattayasaṅgahaṃ saphalaṃ ariyamaggasāsanaṃ tassa ārammaṇabhūtañ‍ca amatadhātuṃ tadadhigamūpāyañ‍ca pubbabhāgapaṭipattisāsanaṃ tadatthaparidīpanañ‍ca pariyattisāsanaṃ yathārahaṃ sac‍cābhisamayavasaina abhisamaintau svākkhātatādiguṇavisaisayuttataṃ manasikarauntau sak‍kac‍caṃ savanadhāraṇaparipucchādīhi paricayaṃ karauntau ca sadaivakau laukau pūjayati nāma| laukanāthau ca sammāsambaudhippattiyā vainaiyyānaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ dhammadaisanaina ‘‘ariyaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, sammāsamādhiṃ daisaissāmi’’ (ma॰ ni॰ 3.136; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.28; paiṭakau॰ 24), ‘‘maggānaṭṭhaṅgikau saiṭṭhau’’ (dha॰ pa॰ 273; kathā॰ 872; naitti॰ 125; paiṭakau॰ 30), ‘‘yāvatā, bhikkhavai, dhammā saṅkhatā vā asaṅkhatā vā, virāgau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati’’ (itivu॰ 90; a॰ ni॰ 4.34), ‘‘khayaṃ virāgaṃ amataṃ paṇītaṃ’’ (khu॰ pā॰ 6.4; su॰ ni॰ 227), ‘‘aikāyanau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā’’ (dī॰ ni॰ 2.373; ma॰ ni॰ 1.106; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.367), ‘‘dhammaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi ādikalyāṇa’’ntiādīhi (ma॰ ni॰ 3.420; naitti॰ 5) vacanaihi thaumanaina ca pūjayati nāma| tasmā sāsanavarassa pūjanīyabhāvau idha vuc‍camānau anavasaisatau dhammaguṇai dīpaitīti yai ariyabhāvādayau niyyānādayau khayavirāgādayau madanimmadanādayau asaṅkhatādayau svākkhātatādayau  ādikalyāṇatādayau ca anaikaihi suttapadaihi pavaiditā anaikai dhammaguṇā, tai niravasaisatau imāya gāthāya pakāsitāti vaiditabbā|

yasmā  pana ariyasac‍cappaṭivaidhaina samugghāṭitasammauhāyaiva paramatthatau paṇḍitā bālyādisamatik‍kamanatau, tasmā bhāvitalaukuttaramaggā sacchikatasāmañ‍ñaphalā ca ariyapuggalā visaisatau vidūti vuc‍canti| tai hi yathāvuttasāsanavaraṃ aviparītatau ñātuṃ naituñ‍ca saparasantānai sak‍kuṇantīti aṭṭhaariyapuggalasamūhassa paramatthasaṅghassāpi idha gahitattā yai suppaṭipan‍natādayau anaikaihi suttapadaihi saṃvaṇṇitā ariyasaṅghaguṇā, taipi niravasaisatau idha pakāsitāti vaiditabbā|

aivaṃ paṭhamagāthāya sātisayaṃ ratanattayaguṇaparidīpanaṃ katvā idāni –

‘‘sabbapāpassa akaraṇaṃ, kusalassa upasampadā|

sacittapariyaudapanaṃ, aitaṃ buddhāna sāsana’’nti|| (dī॰ ni॰ 2.90; dha॰ pa॰ 183; naitti॰ 30, 50, 116, 124) –

vacanatau saṅkhaipatau sikkhattayasaṅgahaṃ sāsanaṃ, taṃ pana sikkhattayaṃ ñāṇavisaisavisayabhāvabhaidatau avatthābhaidatau ca tividhaṃ hauti| kathaṃ? sutamayañāṇagaucarau ca yau ‘‘pariyattisaddhammau’’ti vuc‍cati| cintāmayañāṇagaucarau ca yau ākāraparivitak‍kadiṭṭhinijjhānakkhantīhi gahaitabbākārau vimuttāyatanavisaisau ‘‘paṭipattisaddhammau’’ti vuc‍cati| vipassanāñāṇādisahagatau bhāvanāmayañāṇagaucarau ca yau ‘‘paṭivaidhasaddhammau’’ti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ tividhampi sāsanaṃ sāsanavaranti padaina saṅgaṇhitvā tattha yaṃ paṭhamaṃ, taṃ itaraisaṃ adhigamūpāyauti sabbasāsanamūlabhūtaṃ attanau pakaraṇassa ca visayabhūtaṃ pariyattisāsanamaiva tāva saṅkhaipatau vibhajantau ‘‘dvādasa padānī’’ti gāthamāha|

tattha dvādasāti gaṇanaparicchaidau| padānīti paricchin‍nadhammanidassanaṃ| taisu byañ‍janapadāni paj‍jati atthau aitaihīti padāni| atthapadāni pana paj‍janti ñāyantīti padāni| ubhayampi vā ubhayathā yaujaitabbaṃ byañ‍janapadānampi aviparītaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabbattā, atthapadānaṃ uttarivisaisādhigamassa  kāraṇabhāvatau, tāni padāni paratau pāḷiyañ‍ñaiva āvi bhavissantīti tatthaiva vaṇṇayissāma| atthasūcanādiatthatau suttaṃ| vuttañhaitaṃ saṅgahaisu –

‘‘atthānaṃ sūcanatau, suvuttatau savanatautha sūdanatau|

suttāṇā suttasabhāgatau ca, ‘sutta’nti akkhāta’’nti|| (pārā॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikathā; dī॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikathā; dha॰ sa॰ aṭṭha॰ nidānakathā)|

tadaitaṃ  tattha suttapiṭakavasaina āgataṃ, idha pana piṭakattayavasaina yaujaitabbaṃ| ‘‘dvādasa padāni sutta’’nti vuttaṃ, yaṃ pariyattisāsananti atthau| taṃ sabbanti taṃ ‘‘sutta’’nti vuttaṃ sakalaṃ buddhavacanaṃ| byañ‍janañ‍ca atthau cāti byañ‍janañ‍caiva tadatthau ca| yatau ‘‘dvādasa padāni sutta’’nti vuttaṃ| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – atthasūcanāditau suttaṃ pariyattidhammau, tañ‍ca sabbaṃ atthatau dvādasa padāni cha byañ‍janapadāni caiva cha atthapadāni cāti| atha vā yadaitaṃ ‘‘sāsanavara’’nti vuttaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ suttaṃ, pariyattisāsanassa adhippaitattā| atthatau pana dvādasa padāni, byañ‍janatthapadasamudāyabhāvatau| yathāha – ‘‘byañ‍janañ‍ca atthau cā’’ti| taṃ viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ ubhayanti yasmiṃ byañ‍janai atthai ca vacanavacanīyabhāvaina sambandhai suttavauhārau, tadubhayaṃ sarūpatau viñ‍ñātabbaṃ tattha katamaṃ byañ‍janaṃ katamau atthauti? tainaivāha – ‘‘kau atthau byañ‍janaṃ katama’’nti|

aivaṃ ‘‘sāsanavara’’nti vuttassa suttassa pariyattibhāvaṃ tassa ca atthabyañ‍janapadabhāvaina vaiditabbataṃ dassaitvā idāni tassa pavicayupāyaṃ naittippakaraṇaṃ padatthavibhāgaina dassaituṃ ‘‘sauḷasahārā’’ti gāthamāha|

tattha sauḷasa hārā aitissāti sauḷasahārā| pañ‍canayā aṭṭhārasamūlapadāti aitthāpi aisaiva nayau| atha vā sauḷasa hārā sauḷasahārā| aivaṃ itaratthāpi| hāranayamūlapadāni aiva hi saṅkhaipatau vitthāratau ca bhāsitāni naittīti| sāsanassa pariyaiṭṭhīti sāsanassa atthapariyaisanā, pariyattisāsanassa atthasaṃvaṇṇanāti atthau, sakalassaiva vā sāsanassa atthavicāraṇāti atthau| paṭipattipaṭivaidhaipi hi naittinayānusāraina adhigacchantīti| mahakac‍cānaināti kac‍cauti purātanau isi, tassa vaṃsālaṅkārabhūtauyaṃ mahāthairau ‘‘kac‍cānau’’ti vuc‍cati| mahakac‍cānauti pana  pūjāvacanaṃ, yathā mahāmauggal‍lānauti, ‘‘kac‍cāyanagauttaniddiṭṭhā’’tipi pāṭhau| ayañ‍ca gāthā naittiṃ saṅgāyantaihi pakaraṇatthasaṅgaṇhanavasaina ṭhapitāti daṭṭhabbā| yathā cāyaṃ, aivaṃ hāravibhaṅgavārai taṃtaṃhāraniddaisanigamanai ‘‘taināha āyasmā’’tiādivacanaṃ, hārādisamudāyabhūtāyaṃ naittiyaṃ byañ‍janatthasamudāyai ca suttai kiṃ kaina viciyatīti vicāraṇāyaṃ āha – ‘‘hārā byañ‍janavicayau’’tiādi|

tattha  sauḷasapi hārā mūlapadaniddhāraṇamantaraina byañ‍janamukhainaiva suttassa saṃvaṇṇanā haunti, na nayā viya mūlapadasaṅkhātasabhāvadhammaniddhāraṇamukhaināti tai ‘‘byañ‍janavicayau suttassā’’ti vuttā| atthanayā pana yathāvuttaatthamukhainaiva suttassa atthasampaṭipattiyā hauntīti āha – ‘‘nayā tayau ca suttatthau’’ti| ayañ‍ca vicāraṇā parataupi āgamissati| kaici ‘‘nayau cā’’ti paṭhanti, taṃ na sundaraṃ| ubhayaṃ pariggahītanti hārā nayā cāti aitaṃ ubhayaṃ suttassa atthaniddhāraṇavasaina parisamantatau gahitaṃ sabbathā suttai yaujitaṃ| vuc‍cati suttaṃ vadati saṃvaṇṇaiti| kathaṃ? yathāsuttaṃ suttānurūpaṃ, yaṃ suttaṃ yathā saṃvaṇṇaitabbaṃ, tathā saṃvaṇṇaitīti atthau| yaṃ yaṃ suttanti vā yathāsuttaṃ, sabbaṃ suttanti atthau| naittinayaina hi saṃvaṇṇaituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyaṃ nāma suttaṃ natthīti|

idāni yaṃ vuttaṃ – ‘‘sāsanavaraṃ vidūhi ñaiyya’’nti, tattha naittisaṃvaṇṇanāya visayabhūtaṃ pariyattidhammamaiva pakārantaraina niyamaitvā dassaituṃ ‘‘yā caivā’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

tattha atthaisu kataparicchaidau byañ‍janappabandhau daisanā, yau pāṭhauti vuc‍cati| tadatthau daisitaṃ tāya daisanāya pabaudhitattā| tadubhayañ‍ca vimuttāyatanasīsaina paricayaṃ karauntānaṃ anupādāparinibbānapariyausānānaṃ sampattīnaṃ haitubhāvatau aikantaina viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, tadubhayavinimuttassa vā ñaiyyassa abhāvatau tadaiva dvayaṃ viñ‍ñaiyyanti imamatthaṃ dassaiti yā caiva…pai॰… viñ‍ñaiyyanti| tatrāti tasmiṃ vijānanai sādhaitabbai, nipphādaitabbai caitaṃ bhummaṃ| ayamānupubbīti ayaṃ vakkhamānā anupubbi hāranayānaṃ anuk‍kamau, anuk‍kamaina vakkhamānā hāranayāti atthau| navavidhasuttantapariyaiṭṭhīti suttādivasaina navaṅgassa sāsanassa pariyaisanā, atthavicāraṇāti atthau| sāmiatthai vā aitaṃ pac‍cattaṃ navavidhasuttantapariyaiṭṭhiyā anupubbīti| atha vā anupubbīti karaṇatthai pac‍cattaṃ| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yathāvuttavijānanai sādhaitabbai vakkhamānāya hāranayānupubbiyā ayaṃ navavidhasuttantassa atthapariyaisanāti|

aitthāha  – kathaṃ panaittha gaiyyaṅgādīnaṃ suttabhāvau, suttabhāvai ca taisaṃ kathaṃ sāsanassa navaṅgabhāvau| yañ‍ca saṅgahaisu vuc‍cati ‘‘sagāthakaṃ suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ, niggāthakaṃ suttaṃ vaiyyākaraṇa’’nti, tathā ca sati suttaṅgamaiva na  siyā| athāpi visuṃ suttaṅgaṃ siyā, maṅgalasuttādīnaṃ (khu॰ pā॰ 5.1 ādayau; su॰ ni॰ 261 ādayau) suttaṅgasaṅgahau na siyā, gāthābhāvatau dhammapadādīnaṃ viya, gaiyyaṅgasaṅgahau vā siyā, sagāthakattā sagāthāvaggassa viya, tathā ubhatauvibhaṅgādīsu sagāthakappadaisānanti| vuc‍catai –

suttanti sāmañ‍ñavidhi, visaisavidhayau parai|

sanimittā niruḷhattā, sahatāñ‍ñaina nāñ‍ñatau||

sabbassāpi hi buddhavacanassa suttanti ayaṃ sāmañ‍ñavidhi| tathā hi ‘‘aittakaṃ tassa bhagavatau suttāgataṃ suttapariyāpan‍naṃ (pāci॰ 1242), sāvatthiyā suttavibhaṅgai, sakavādai pañ‍ca suttasatānī’’tiādivacanatau vinayābhidhammapariyattivisaisaipi suttavauhārau dissati| tadaikadaisaisu pana gaiyyādayau visaisavidhayau taina taina nimittaina patiṭṭhitā| tathā hi gaiyyassa sagāthakattaṃ tabbhāvanimittaṃ| laukaipi hi sasilaukaṃ sagāthakaṃ cuṇṇiyaganthaṃ gaiyya’’nti vadanti| gāthāvirahai pana sati pucchitvā vissaj‍janabhāvau vaiyyākaraṇassa| pucchāvissaj‍janañhi ‘‘byākaraṇa’’nti vuc‍cati| byākaraṇamaiva vaiyyākaraṇanti| aivaṃ santai sagāthakādīnampi pañhāvissaj‍janavasaina pavattānaṃ vaiyyākaraṇabhāvau āpaj‍jatīti? nāpaj‍jati, gaiyyādisañ‍ñānaṃ anaukāsabhāvatau ‘‘gāthāvirahai satī’’ti visaisitattā ca| tathā hi dhammapadādīsu kaivalaṃ gāthābandhaisu sagāthakattaipi saumanassañāṇamayikagāthāyuttaisu ‘‘vuttañhaita’’ntiādivacanasambandhaisu abbhutadhammapaṭisaṃyuttaisu ca suttavisaisaisu yathāk‍kamaṃ gāthāudānaitivuttakaabbhutadhammasañ‍ñā patiṭṭhitā, tathā satipi gāthābandhabhāvai bhagavatau atītāsu jātīsu cariyānubhāvappakāsakaisu jātakasañ‍ñā| satipi pañhāvissaj‍janabhāvai sagāthakattai ca kaisuci suttantaisu vaidassa labhāpanatau vaidal‍lasañ‍ñā patiṭṭhitāti aivaṃ taina taina sagāthakattādinā nimittaina taisu taisu suttavisaisaisu gaiyyaṅgādisañ‍ñā patiṭṭhitāti visaisavidhayau suttaṅgatau parai gaiyyādayau|

yaṃ panaittha gaiyyaṅgādinimittarahitaṃ suttaṃ, taṃ suttaṅgaṃ visaisasañ‍ñāparihāraina sāmañ‍ñasañ‍ñāya pavattanatauti| nanu ca sagāthakaṃ  suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ, niggāthakaṃ suttaṃ vaiyyākaraṇanti suttaṅgaṃ na sambhavatīti caudanā tadavatthā aivāti? na tadavatthā, saudhitattā| saudhitañhi  pubbai gāthāvirahai sati pucchāvissaj‍janabhāvau vaiyyākaraṇassa tabbhāvanimittanti| yañ‍ca vuttaṃ – ‘‘gāthābhāvatau maṅgalasuttādīnaṃ suttaṅgasaṅgahau na siyā’’ti, tampi na, niruḷhattā| niruḷhau hi maṅgalasuttādīsu suttabhāvau, na hi tāni dhammapadabuddhavaṃsādayau viya gāthābhāvaina pañ‍ñātāni, kintu suttabhāvainaiva| tainaiva hi aṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘suttanāmaka’’nti nāmaggahaṇaṃ kataṃ|

yaṃ pana vuttaṃ ‘‘sagāthakattā gaiyyaṅgasaṅgahau vā siyā’’ti, tadapi natthi, yasmā sahatāñ‍ñaina| saha gāthāhīti hi sagāthakaṃ| sahabhāvau ca nāma atthatau añ‍ñaina hauti, na ca maṅgalasuttādīsu gāthāvinimuttau kauci suttappadaisau atthi| yau saha gāthāhīti vuc‍caiyya, na ca samudāyau nāma kauci atthi| yadapi vuttaṃ – ‘‘ubhatauvibhaṅgādīsu sagāthakappadaisānaṃ gaiyyaṅgasaṅgahau siyā’’ti, tadapi na añ‍ñatau| añ‍ñā aiva hi tā gāthā, jātakādipariyāpan‍nattā| atau na tāhi ubhatauvibhaṅgādīnaṃ gaiyyaṅgabhāvauti aivaṃ suttādīnaṃ aṅgānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñasaṅkarābhāvau vaiditabbau| yasmā pana sabbampi buddhavacanaṃ yathāvuttanayaina atthānaṃ sūcanādiatthaina suttantvaiva vuc‍cati, tasmā vuttaṃ – ‘‘navavidhasuttantapariyaiṭṭhī’’ti|

saṅgahavāravaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. uddaisavāravaṇṇanā

1. aivaṃ  saṅgahavāraina saṅkhaipatau dassitai hārādayau idāni vibhāgaina dassaituṃ ‘‘tattha katamai sauḷasa hārā’’tiādidaisanā āraddhā| tattha tatthāti yaṃ vuttaṃ – ‘‘sauḷasahārā naittī’’ti, tasmiṃ vacanai, tissaṃ vā gāthāyaṃ, yāni hāranayamūlapadāni uddhaṭāni, taisūti atthau| katamaiti pucchāvacanaṃ| pucchā ca nāmaisā pañ‍cavidhā adiṭṭhajautanāpucchā diṭṭhasaṃsandanāpucchā vimaticchaidanāpucchā anumatipucchā kathaitukamyatāpucchāti| tāsu ayaṃ kathaitukamyatāpucchā| sauḷasāti gaṇanavasaina paricchaidau| taina naisaṃ na tatau uddhaṃ adhau cāti aitaparamataṃ  dassaiti| sā caitaparamatā paratau āvi bhavissati| hārāti gaṇanavasaina paricchin‍nānaṃ sāmañ‍ñatau dassanaṃ| daisanā vicayautiādi sarūpadassanaṃ|

tattha kainaṭṭhaina hārā? harīyanti aitaihi, aittha vā suttagaiyyādivisayā añ‍ñāṇasaṃsayavipal‍lāsāti hārā, haranti vā sayaṃ tāni, haraṇamattamaiva vāti hārā phalūpacāraina| atha vā harīyanti vauharīyanti dhammasaṃvaṇṇakadhammapaṭiggāhakaihi dhammassa dānaggahaṇavasaināti hārā| atha vā hārā viyāti hārā| yathā hi anaikaratanāvalisamūhau hārasaṅkhātau attanau avayavabhūtaratanasamphassaihi samuppaj‍janīyamānahilādasukhau hutvā tadupabhaugījanasarīrasantāpaṃ nidāghapariḷāhupajanitaṃ vūpasamaiti, aivamaitaipi nānāvidhaparamattharatanappabandhā saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisā attanau avayavabhūtaparamattharatanādhigamaina samuppādiyamānanibbutisukhā dhammapaṭiggāhakajanahadayaparitāpaṃ kāmarāgādikilaisahaitukaṃ vūpasamaintīti| atha vā hārayanti añ‍ñāṇādīnaṃ hāraṃ apagamaṃ karaunti ācikkhantīti vā hārā| atha vā sautujanacittassa haraṇatau ramaṇatau ca hārā niruttinayaina, yathā – ‘‘bhavaisu vantagamanau bhagavā’’ti (visuddhi॰ 1.144; pārā॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.1 vairañ‍jakaṇḍavaṇṇanā)| ayaṃ tāva hārānaṃ sādhāraṇatau atthau|

asādhāraṇatau pana daisīyati saṃvaṇṇīyati aitāya suttatthauti daisanā, daisanāsahacaraṇatau vā  daisanā| nanu ca añ‍ñaipi hārā daisanāsaṅkhātassa suttassa atthasaṃvaṇṇanatau daisanāsahacārinauvāti? sac‍camaitaṃ, ayaṃ pana hārau yaibhuyyaina yathārutavasainaiva viñ‍ñāyamānau daisanāya saha caratīti vattabbataṃ arahati, na tathā parai| na hi assādādīnavanissaraṇādisandassanarahitā suttadaisanā atthi| assādādisandassanavibhāvanalakkhaṇau cāyaṃ hārauti|

viciyanti aitaina, aittha vā padapañhādayau, viciti aiva vā taisanti vicayau| pāḷiyaṃ pana vicinatīti vicayauti ayamatthau dassitau|

yuttīti upapattisādhanayutti, idha pana yuttivicāraṇā yutti uttarapadalaupaina ‘‘rūpabhavau rūpa’’nti yathā, yuttisahacaraṇatau vā| idhāpi daisanāhārai vuttanayaina atthau vitthāraitabbau|

padaṭṭhānanti  āsan‍nakāraṇaṃ, idhāpi padaṭṭhānavicāraṇātiādi vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbaṃ|

lakkhīyanti aitaina, aittha vā aikalakkhaṇā dhammā avuttāpi aikavacanaināti lakkhaṇaṃ|

viyūhīyanti vibhāgaina piṇḍīyanti aitaina, aittha vāti byūhau| nibbacanādīnaṃ suttai dassiyamānānaṃ catun‍naṃ byūhauti catubyūhau, catun‍naṃ vā byūhau aitthāti catubyūhau|

āvaṭṭīyanti aitaina, aittha vā sabhāgā visabhāgā ca dhammā, taisaṃ vā āvaṭṭananti āvaṭṭau|

vibhajīyanti aitaina, aittha vā sādhāraṇāsādhāraṇānaṃ saṃkilaisavaudānadhammānaṃ bhūmiyauti vibhatti, vibhajanaṃ vā aitaisaṃ bhūmiyāti vibhatti|

paṭipakkhavasaina parivattīyanti iminā, aittha vā suttai vuttadhammā, parivattanaṃ vā taisanti parivattanau|

vividhaṃ  vacanaṃ aikassaivatthassa vācakamaitthāti vivacanaṃ, vivacanamaiva vaivacanaṃ, vividhaṃ vuc‍cati aitaina atthauti vā vivacanaṃ| saisaṃ vuttanayamaiva|

pakāraihi pabhaidatau vā ñāpīyanti iminā, aittha vā atthāti pañ‍ñatti|

autārīyanti anuppavaisīyanti aitaina, aittha vā suttāgatā dhammā paṭic‍casamuppādādīsūti autaraṇau|

saudhīyanti samādhīyanti aitaina, aittha vā suttai padapadatthapañhārambhāti saudhanau|

adhiṭṭhīyanti anupavattīyanti aitaina, aittha vā sāmañ‍ñavisaisabhūtā dhammā vinā vikappaināti adhiṭṭhānau|

parikarauti abhisaṅkharauti phalanti parikkhārau, haitu pac‍cayau ca, parikkhāraṃ ācikkhatīti parikkhārau, hārau, parikkhāravisayattā parikkhārasahacaraṇatau vā parikkhārau|

samāraupīyanti aitaina, aittha vā padaṭṭhānādimukhaina dhammāti samāraupanau| sabbattha ca bhāvasādhanavasaināpi atthau sambhavatīti tassāpi vasaina yaujaitabbaṃ|

tassāti  yathāvuttassa hāruddaisassa| anugītīti vuttassaivatthassa sukhaggahaṇatthaṃ anupacchā gāyanagāthā, tāsu ausānagāthāya atthatau asaṃkiṇṇāti padatthaina saṅkararahitā, taina yadipi kaici hārā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ avisiṭṭhā viya dissanti, tathāpi taisaṃ atthatau saṅkarau natthīti dassaiti| sau ca naisaṃ asaṅkarau lakkhaṇaniddaisai supākaṭau hauti| aitaisañ‍caivāti aitaisaṃ sauḷasan‍naṃ hārānaṃ| yathā asaṅkarau, tathā caiva bhavati| kiṃ bhavati? vitthāratayā vitthāraina| nayavibhatti nayaina upāyaina ñāyaina vibhāgau| aitaina taṃ aiva asaṅkiṇṇataṃ vibhāvaiti| kaici ‘‘vitthāranayā’’ti paṭhanti, taṃ na sundaraṃ, ayañ‍ca gāthā kaisuci pautthakaisu natthi|

2. aivaṃ hārai uddisitvā idāni nayai uddisituṃ ‘‘tattha katamai’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha nayanti saṃkilaisai vaudānāni ca vibhāgatau ñāpaintīti nayā, nīyanti vā tāni aitaihi, aittha vāti nayā, nayanamattamaiva vāti nayā, nīyanti vā sayaṃ dhammakathikaihi upanīyanti suttassa atthapavicayatthanti nayā| atha vā nayā viyāti nayā| yathā hi aikattādayau nayā sammā paṭivijjhiyamānā pac‍cayapac‍cayuppan‍nadhammānaṃ yathāk‍kamaṃ sambandhavibhāgabyāpāravirahānurūpaphalabhāvadassanaina asaṅkaratau sammutisac‍caparamatthasac‍cānaṃ sabhāvaṃ pavaidayantā paramatthasac‍cappaṭivaidhāya saṃvattanti, aivamaitaipi kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgadhammavibhāgadassanaina aviparītasuttatthāvabaudhāya abhisambhuṇantā vainaiyyānaṃ catusac‍cappaṭivaidhāya saṃvattanti| atha vā pariyattiatthassa nayanatau saṃkilaisatau yamanatau ca nayā niruttinayaina|

nandiyāvaṭṭautiādīsu nandiyāvaṭṭassa viya āvaṭṭau aitassāti nandiyāvaṭṭau, yathā hi nandiyāvaṭṭau antauṭhitaina padhānāvayavaina bahiddhā āvaṭṭati, aivamayampi nayauti atthau| atha vā nandiyā taṇhāya pamaudassa vā āvaṭṭau aitthāti nandiyāvaṭṭau| tīhi avayavaihi laubhādīhi saṃkilaisapakkhai alaubhādīhi ca vaudānapakkhai pukkhalau saubhanauti tipukkhalau| asantāsanajavaparak‍kamādivisaisayaugaina sīhau bhagavā, tassa vik‍kīḷitaṃ daisanāvacīkammabhūtau vihārauti katvā vipal‍lāsatappaṭipakkhaparidīpanatau sīhassa vik‍kīḷitaṃ aitthāti sīhavik‍kīḷitau,  nayau| balavisaisayaugadīpanatau vā sīhavik‍kīḷitasadisattā nayau sīhavik‍kīḷitau| balavisaisau caittha saddhādibalaṃ, dasabalāni aiva vā| atthanayattayadisābhāvaina kusalādidhammānaṃ ālaucanaṃ disālaucanaṃ| tathā ālaucitānaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ atthanayattayayaujanai samānayanatau aṅkusau viya aṅkusau| gāthāsu lañ‍jaiti pakāsaiti suttatthanti lañ‍jakau, nayau ca sau lañ‍jakau cāti nayalañ‍jakau| gatāti ñātā, matāti atthau| sau aiva vā pāṭhau| saisaṃ vuttanayaina vaiditabbaṃ|

3. aivaṃ nayaipi uddisitvā idāni mūlapadāni uddisituṃ ‘‘tattha katamānī’’tiādi āraddhaṃ| tattha mūlāni ca tāni nayānaṃ paṭṭhānabhāgānañ‍ca patiṭṭhābhāvatau padāni ca adhigamūpāyabhāvatau kauṭṭhāsabhāvatau cāti mūlapadāni| kausal‍lasambhūtaṭṭhaina, kucchitānaṃ vā pāpadhammānaṃ salanatau viddhaṃsanatau, kusānaṃ vā rāgādīnaṃ lavanatau, kusā viya vā lavanatau, kusaina vā ñāṇaina lātabbatau pavattaitabbatau kusalāni, tappaṭipakkhatau akusalānīti padatthau vaiditabbau|

aivaṃ gaṇanaparicchaidatau jātibhaidatau ca mūlapadāni dassaitvā idāni sarūpatau dassaintau saṃkilaisapakkhaṃyaiva  paṭhamaṃ uddisati ‘‘taṇhā’’tiādinā| tattha tasati paritasatīti taṇhā| avindiyaṃ vindati, vindiyaṃ na vindatīti avij‍jā, vij‍jāpaṭipakkhāti vā avij‍jā| lubbhanti taina, sayaṃ vā lubbhati, lubbhanamattamaiva vā sauti laubhau| dausamauhaisupi aisaiva nayau| asubhai ‘‘subha’’nti pavattā sañ‍ñā subhasañ‍ñā| sukhasañ‍ñādīsupi imināva nayaina atthau vaiditabbau| saṅgahanti gaṇanaṃ| samausaraṇanti samauraupanaṃ|

pac‍canīkadhammai samaitīti samathau| anic‍cādīhi vividhaihi ākāraihi passatīti vipassanā| alaubhādayau laubhādipaṭipakkhatau vaiditabbā| asubhai ‘‘asubha’’nti pavattā sañ‍ñā asubhasañ‍ñā, kāyānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānaṃ| sañ‍ñāsīsaina hi daisanā| dukkhasañ‍ñādīsupi aisaiva nayau|

idaṃ uddānanti idaṃ vuttassaiva atthassa vippakiṇṇabhāvaina nassituṃ adatvā uddhaṃ dānaṃ rakkhaṇaṃ uddānaṃ, saṅgahavacananti atthau| ‘‘cattārau vipal‍lāsā’’tipi  pāṭhau| kilaisabhūmīti saṃkilaisabhūmi sabbaisaṃ akusaladhammānaṃ samausaraṇaṭṭhānattā| kusalānaṃ yāni tīṇi mūlāni| ‘‘kusalānī’’tipi paṭhanti| satipaṭṭhānāti asubhasañ‍ñādayau sandhāyāha| indriyabhūmīti saddhādīnaṃ vimuttiparipācanindriyānaṃ samausaraṇaṭṭhānattā vuttaṃ| yuj‍jantīti yaujīyanti| khauti padapūraṇai, avadhāraṇatthai vā nipātau| taina aitai aivāti dassaiti| aṭṭhārasaivāti vā| mūlapadāti mūlapadāni, liṅgavipal‍lāsau vā|

uddaisavāravaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

3. niddaisavāravaṇṇanā

4. aivaṃ  uddiṭṭhai hārādayau niddisituṃ ‘‘tattha saṅkhaipatau’’tiādi āraddhaṃ| tattha tatthāti tasmiṃ uddaisapāṭhai| saṅkhaipatau naitti kittitāti samāsatau naittippakaraṇaṃ kathitaṃ| hāranayamūlapadānañhi sarūpadassanaṃ uddaisapāṭhaina katanti| aittha ca hāranayānaṃ –

sāmañ‍ñatau visaisaina, padatthau lakkhaṇaṃ kamau|

aittāvatā ca haitvādī, vaiditabbā hi viñ‍ñunā||

taisu avisaisatau visaisatau ca hāranayānaṃ atthau dassitau| lakkhaṇādīsu pana avisaisatau sabbaipi hārā nayā ca yathāk‍kamaṃ byañ‍janatthamukhaina navaṅgassa sāsanassa atthasaṃvaṇṇanalakkhaṇā| visaisatau pana tassa tassa hārassa nayassa ca lakkhaṇaṃ niddaisai aiva kathayissāma| kamādīni ca yasmā naisaṃ lakkhaṇaisu ñātaisu viñ‍ñaiyyāni haunti, tasmā tānipi niddaisatau paratau pakāsayissāma|

hārasaṅkhaipau

1. yā pana assādādīnavatātiādikā niddaisagāthā, tāsu assādādīnavatāti assādau ādīnavatāti padavibhāgau| ādīnavatāti ca ādīnavau aiva| kaici ‘‘assādādīnavatau’’ti paṭhanti, taṃ na sundaraṃ| tattha assādīyatīti assādau, sukhaṃ saumanassañ‍ca| vuttañhaitaṃ – ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu  assādau’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.166; saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.26)| yathā caitaṃ sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, aivaṃ iṭṭhārammaṇampi| vuttampi caitaṃ – ‘‘sau tadassādaiti taṃ nikāmaitī’’ti ‘‘rūpaṃ assādaiti abhinandati, taṃ ārabbha rāgau uppaj‍jatī’’ti (paṭṭhā॰ 1.1.424), ‘‘saṃyaujanīyaisu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu assādānupassinau’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.53) ca| assādaiti aitāyāti vā assādau, taṇhā| taṇhāya hi kāraṇabhūtāya puggalau sukhampi sukhārammaṇampi assādaiti| yathā ca taṇhā, aivaṃ vipal‍lāsāpi| vipal‍lāsavasaina hi sattā aniṭṭhampi ārammaṇaṃ iṭṭhākāraina assādainti, aivaṃ vaidanāya sabbaisaṃ taibhūmakasaṅkhārānaṃ taṇhāya vipal‍lāsānañ‍ca assādavicārau vaiditabbau|

kathaṃ pana dukkhādukkhamasukhavaidanānaṃ assādanīyatāti? vipal‍lāsatau sukhapariyāyasabbhāvatau ca| tathā hi vuttaṃ – ‘‘sukhā khau, āvusau visākha, vaidanā ṭhitisukhā vipariṇāmadukkhā| dukkhā vaidanā ṭhitidukkhā vipariṇāmasukhā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā ñāṇasukhā añ‍ñāṇadukkhā’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.465)| tattha vaidanāya aṭṭhasatapariyāyavasaina, taibhūmakasaṅkhārānaṃ nikkhaipakaṇḍarūpakaṇḍavasaina, taṇhāya saṃkilaisavatthuvibhaṅgai nikkhaipakaṇḍai ca taṇhāniddaisavasaina, vipal‍lāsānaṃ sukhasañ‍ñādivasaina dvāsaṭṭhidiṭṭhigatavasaina ca vibhāgau vaiditabbau|

ādīnavau dukkhā vaidanā tissaupi vā dukkhatā| atha vā sabbaipi taibhūmakā saṅkhārā ādīnavau| ādīnaṃ ativiya kapaṇaṃ vāti pavattatīti hi ādīnavau, kapaṇamanussau, aivaṃsabhāvā ca taibhūmakā dhammā anic‍catādiyaugaina| yatau tattha ādīnavānupassanā āraddhavipassakānaṃ yathābhūtanayauti vuc‍cati| tathā ca vuttaṃ – ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā, ayaṃ pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu ādīnavau’’ti| tasmā ādīnavau dukkhasac‍caniddaisabhūtānaṃ jātiyādīnaṃ anic‍catādīnaṃ dvācattālīsāya ākārānañ‍ca vasaina vibhajitvā niddisitabbau|

nissarati aitaināti nissaraṇaṃ, ariyamaggau| nissaratīti vā nissaraṇaṃ nibbānaṃ| ubhayampi sāmañ‍ñaniddaisaina aikasaisaina vā ‘‘nissaraṇa’’nti  vuttaṃ| pi-saddau purimānaṃ pacchimānañ‍ca sampiṇḍanatthau| tattha ariyamaggapakkhai satipaṭṭhānādīnaṃ sattattiṃsabaudhipakkhiyadhammānaṃ kāyānupassanādīnañ‍ca tadantaugadhabhaidānaṃ vasaina nissaraṇaṃ vibhajitvā niddisitabbaṃ|

nibbānapakkhai pana kiñ‍cāpi asaṅkhatāya dhātuyā nippariyāyaina vibhāgau natthi| pariyāyaina pana saupādisaisanirupādisaisabhaidaina| yatau vā taṃ nissaṭaṃ, taisaṃ paṭisambhidāmaggai (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.3) dassitappabhaidānaṃ cakkhādīnaṃ chan‍naṃ dvārānaṃ rūpādīnaṃ chan‍naṃ ārammaṇānaṃ taṃtaṃdvārappavattānaṃ  chan‍naṃ chan‍naṃ viñ‍ñāṇaphassavaidanāsañ‍ñācaitanātaṇhāvitak‍kavicārānaṃ pathavīdhātuādīnaṃ chan‍naṃ dhātūnaṃ dasan‍naṃ kasiṇāyatanānaṃ asubhānaṃ kaisādīnaṃ dvattiṃsāya ākārānaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ dvādasan‍naṃ āyatanānaṃ aṭṭhārasan‍naṃ dhātūnaṃ laukiyānaṃ indriyānaṃ kāmadhātuādīnaṃ tissan‍naṃ dhātūnaṃ kāmabhavādīnaṃ tiṇṇaṃ tiṇṇaṃ bhavānaṃ catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ appamañ‍ñānaṃ āruppānaṃ dvādasan‍naṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaṅgānañ‍cāti aivamādīnaṃ saṅkhatadhammānaṃ nissaraṇabhāvaina ca vibhajitvā niddisitabbaṃ|

phalanti daisanāphalaṃ| kiṃ pana tanti? yaṃ daisanāya nipphādīyati| nanu ca nibbānādhigamau bhagavatau daisanāya nipphādīyati| nibbānañ‍ca ‘‘nissaraṇa’’nti iminā vuttamaivāti? sac‍camaitaṃ, tañ‍ca khau paramparāya| idha pana pac‍cakkhatau daisanāphalaṃ adhippaitaṃ| taṃ pana sutamayañāṇaṃ| atthadhammavaidādiariyamaggassa pubbabhāgappaṭipattibhūtā chabbisuddhiyau| yañ‍ca tasmiṃ khaṇai maggaṃ anabhisambhuṇantassa kālantarai tadadhigamakāraṇabhūtaṃ sampattibhavahaitu ca siyā| tathā hi vakkhati – ‘‘attānudiṭṭhiṃ ūhac‍ca, aivaṃ mac‍cutarau siyāti (su॰ ni॰ 1125; kathā॰ 226; cūḷani॰ maugharājamāṇavapucchā 144, maugharājamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 88; naitti॰ 5; paiṭakau॰ 22) idaṃ phala’’nti, ‘‘dhammau havai rakkhati dhammacārinti idaṃ phala’’nti (jā॰ 1.10.102-103; 1.15.385) ca| aitaina nayaina daivaisu ca mānusaisu ca āyuvaṇṇabalasukhayasaparivāraādhipataiyyasampattiyau upadhisampattiyau cak‍kavattisirī daivaraj‍jasirī cattāri sampatticak‍kāni sīlasampadā samādhisampadā tissau vij‍jā cha abhiñ‍ñā catassau paṭisambhidā sāvakabaudhi pac‍caikabaudhi sammāsambaudhīti sabbāpi sampattiyau puñ‍ñasambhārahaitukā bhagavatau daisanāya sādhaitabbatāya phalanti vaiditabbā|

upāyauti  ariyamaggapadaṭṭhānabhūtā pubbabhāgappaṭipadā| sā hi purimā purimā pacchimāya pacchimāya adhigamūpāyabhāvatau paramparāya magganibbānādhigamassa ca haitubhāvatau upāyau| yā ca pubbai vuttaphalādhigamassa upāyapaṭipatti| kaici pana ‘‘saha vipassanāya maggau upāyau’’ti vadanti, taisaṃ mataina nissaraṇanti nibbānamaiva vuttaṃ siyā| phalaṃ viya upāyaupi pubbabhāgauti vuttaṃ siyā, yaṃ pana vakkhati ‘‘sabbai dhammā…pai॰… visuddhiyāti (dha॰ pa॰ 279; thairagā॰ 678) ayaṃ upāyau’’ti| aitthāpi pubbabhāgappaṭipadā aiva udāhaṭāti sak‍kā viñ‍ñātuṃ| yasmā pana ‘‘tai pahāya tarai aughanti idaṃ nissaraṇa’’nti ariyamaggassa nissaraṇabhāvaṃ vakkhati| ariyamaggau hi aughataraṇanti|

āṇattīti  āṇārahassa bhagavatau vainaiyyajanassa hitasiddhiyā ‘‘aivaṃ paṭipaj‍jāhī’’ti vidhānaṃ| tathā hi vakkhati ‘‘suñ‍ñatau laukaṃ avaikkhassu, maugharājāti (su॰ ni॰ 1125; kathā॰ 226; naitti॰ 5; paiṭakau॰ 22; cūḷani॰ maugharājamāṇavapucchā 144, maugharājamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 88) āṇattī’’ti|

yaugīnanti catusac‍cakammaṭṭhānabhāvanāya yuttappayuttānaṃ vainaiyyānaṃ, atthāyāti vacanasaisau| daisanāhārauti aitaisaṃ yathāvuttānaṃ assādādīnaṃ vibhajanalakkhaṇau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau daisanāhārau nāmāti atthau| aitthāha – kiṃ panaitaisaṃ assādādīnaṃ anavasaisānaṃ vacanaṃ daisanāhārau, udāhu aikac‍cānanti? niravasaisānaṃyaiva| yasmiñhi suttai assādādīnavanissaraṇāni sarūpatau āgatāni, tattha vattabbamaiva natthi| yattha pana aikadaisaina āgatāni, na vā sarūpaina| tattha anāgataṃ atthavasaina niddhāraitvā hārau yaujaitabbau| ayañ‍ca atthau daisanāhāravibhaṅgai āgamissatīti idha na papañ‍citau|

2. yaṃ pucchitanti yā pucchā, viciyamānāti vacanasaisau| vissaj‍jitaṃ anugītīti aitthāpi aisaiva nayau| tattha vissaj‍jitanti vissaj‍janā, sā aikaṃsabyākaraṇādivasaina catubbidhaṃ byākaraṇaṃ| ca-saddau sampiṇḍanatthau, taina gāthāyaṃ avuttaṃ padādiṃ saṅgaṇhāti| tā pana pucchāvissaj‍janā kassāti āha ‘‘suttassā’’ti| aitaina suttai āgataṃ pucchāvissaj‍janaṃ vicaitabbanti dassaiti| yā ca anugītiti vuttassaiva atthassa yā anu pacchā gīti anugīti, saṅgahagāthā, pucchāya vā anurūpā gīti| aitaina pubbāparaṃ  gahitaṃ| byākaraṇassa hi pucchānurūpatā idha pubbāparaṃ adhippaitaṃ| yā ‘‘pucchānusandhī’’ti vuc‍cati| purimaṃ ‘‘suttassā’’ti padaṃ pubbāpaikkhanti puna ‘‘suttassā’’ti vuttaṃ| taina suttassa nissayabhūtai assādādikai pariggaṇhāti| aittāvatā vicayahārassa visayau niravasaisaina dassitau hauti| tathā ca vakkhati vicayahāravibhaṅgai ‘‘padaṃ vicinati…pai॰… anugītiṃ vicinatī’’ti|

tattha suttai sabbaisaṃ padānaṃ anupubbaina atthasau byañ‍janasau ca vicayau padavicayau| ‘‘ayaṃ pucchā adiṭṭhajautanā diṭṭhasaṃsandanā vimaticchaidanā anumatipucchā kathaitukamyatāpucchā sattādhiṭṭhānā dhammādhiṭṭhānā aikādhiṭṭhānā anaikādhiṭṭhānā sammutivisayā paramatthavisayā atītavisayā anāgatavisayā pac‍cuppan‍navisayā’’tiādinā pucchāvicayau vaiditabbau| ‘‘idaṃ vissaj‍janaṃ  aikaṃsabyākaraṇaṃ vibhaj‍jabyākaraṇaṃ paṭipucchābyākaraṇaṃ ṭhapanaṃ sāvasaisaṃ niravasaisaṃ sauttaraṃ anuttaraṃ laukiyaṃ laukuttara’’ntiādinā vissaj‍janavicayau|

‘‘ayaṃ pucchā iminā samaiti, aitaina na samaitī’’ti pucchitatthaṃ ānaitvā vicayau pubbaināparaṃ saṃsanditvā ca vicayau pubbāparavicayau| ‘‘ayaṃ anugīti vuttatthasaṅgahā avuttatthasaṅgahā tadubhayatthasaṅgahā kusalatthasaṅgahā akusalatthasaṅgahā’’tiādinā anugītivicayau| assādādīsu sukhavaidanāya ‘‘iṭṭhārammaṇānubhavanalakkhaṇā’’tiādinā, taṇhāya ‘‘ārammaṇaggahaṇalakkhaṇā’’tiādinā, vipal‍lāsānaṃ ‘‘viparītaggahaṇalakkhaṇā’’tiādinā, avasiṭṭhānaṃ taibhūmakadhammānaṃ ‘‘yathāsakalakkhaṇā’’tiādinā sabbaisañ‍ca dvāvīsatiyā tikaisu dvācattālīsādhikai ca dukasatai labbhamānapadavasaina taṃtaṃassādatthavisaisaniddhāraṇaṃ assādavicayau|

dukkhavaidanāya ‘‘aniṭṭhānubhavanalakkhaṇā’’tiādinā, dukkhasac‍cānaṃ ‘‘paṭisandhilakkhaṇā’’tiādinā, anic‍catādīnaṃ ādiantavantatāya anic‍cantikatāya ca ‘‘anic‍cā’’tiādinā sabbaisañ‍ca laukiyadhammānaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyahānabhāgiyatādivasaina ādīnavavuttiyā aukāraniddhāraṇaina ādīnavavicayau| nissaraṇapadai ariyamaggassa āgamanatau  kāyānupassanādipubbabhāgappaṭipadāvibhāgavisaisaniddhāraṇavasaina nibbānassa yathāvuttapariyāyavibhāgavisaisaniddhāraṇavasaināti aivaṃ nissaraṇavicayau| phalādīnaṃ taṃtaṃsuttadaisanāya sādhaitabbaphalassa tadupāyassa tattha tattha suttavidhivacanassa ca vibhāganiddhāraṇavasaina vicayau vaiditabbau| aivaṃ padapucchāvissaj‍janapubbāparānugītīnaṃ assādādīnañ‍ca visaisaniddhāraṇavasainaiva vicayalakkhaṇau ‘‘vicayau hārau’’ti vaiditabbau|

3. sabbaisanti sauḷasan‍naṃ| bhūmīti byañ‍janaṃ sandhāyāha| byañ‍janañhi mūlapadāni viya nayānaṃ hārānaṃ bhūmi pavattiṭṭhānaṃ, taisaṃ byañ‍janavicārabhāvatau| vuttañhi – ‘‘hārā byañ‍janavicayau’’ti, paiṭakaipi hi vuttaṃ – ‘‘yattha ca sabbai hārā, sampatamānā nayanti suttatthaṃ| byañ‍janavidhiputhuttā’’ti| gaucarauti suttatthau| suttassa hi padatthuddhāraṇamukhaina hārayaujanā| taisaṃ byañ‍janatthānaṃ| yuttāyuttaparikkhāti yuttassa ca ayuttassa ca upaparikkhā| ‘‘yuttāyuttiparikkhā’’tipi pāṭhau, yuttiayuttīnaṃ vicāraṇāti atthau| kathaṃ pana taisaṃ yuttāyuttajānanā? catūhi mahāpadaisaihi avirujjhanaina| tattha byañ‍janassa tāva sabhāvaniruttibhāvau adhippaitatthavācakabhāvau ca yuttabhāvau| atthassa pana suttavinayadhammatāhi avilaumanaṃ| ayamaittha saṅkhaipau| vitthārau pana paratau āvi bhavissati| hārau yuttīti niddiṭṭhauti aivaṃ suttai byañ‍janatthānaṃ yuttāyuttabhāvavibhāvanalakkhaṇau yuttihārauti vaiditabbau|

4. dhammanti yaṃ kiñ‍ci suttāgataṃ kusalādidhammamāha| tassa dhammassāti tassa yathāvuttassa kusalādidhammassa| yaṃ padaṭṭhānanti yaṃ kāraṇaṃ, yaunisaumanasikārādi suttai āgataṃ vā anāgataṃ vā sambhavatau niddhāraitvā kathaitabbanti adhippāyau| itīti aivaṃ, vuttanayaināti atthau| yāva sabbadhammāti yattakā tasmiṃ suttai āgatā dhammā, taisaṃ sabbaisampi yathānurūpaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ niddhāraitvā kathaitabbanti adhippāyau| atha vā yāva sabbadhammāti suttāgatassa dhammassa yaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, tassapi yaṃ padaṭṭhānanti sambhavatau yāva sabbadhammā padaṭṭhānavicāraṇā kātabbāti atthau| aisau hārau padaṭṭhānauti aivaṃ suttai āgatadhammānaṃ padaṭṭhānabhūtā dhammā taisañ‍ca padaṭṭhānabhūtāti sambhavatau padaṭṭhānabhūtadhammaniddhāraṇalakkhaṇau padaṭṭhānau nāma hārauti atthau|

5. vuttamhi  aikadhammaiti kusalādīsu khandhādīsu vā yasmiṃ kismiñ‍ci aikadhammai, suttai sarūpatau niddhāraṇavasaina vā kathitai| yai dhammā aikalakkhaṇā kaicīti yai kaici dhammā kusalādibhāvaina rūpakkhandhādibhāvaina vā taina dhammaina samānalakkhaṇā| vuttā bhavanti sabbaiti tai sabbaipi kusalādisabhāvā, khandhādisabhāvā vā dhammā suttai avuttāpi tāya samānalakkhaṇatāya vuttā bhavanti ānaitvā saṃvaṇṇanāvasaināti adhippāyau| aittha ca aikalakkhaṇāti samānalakkhaṇā vuttā| taina sahacāritā samānakic‍catā samānahaitutā samānaphalatā samānārammaṇatāti aivamādīhi avuttānampi vuttānaṃ viya niddhāraṇaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| sau hārau lakkhaṇau nāmāti aivaṃ suttai anāgataipi dhammai vuttappakāraina āgatai viya niddhāraitvā yā saṃvaṇṇanā, sau lakkhaṇau nāma hārauti atthau|

6. nairuttanti niruttaṃ, padanibbacananti atthau| adhippāyauti buddhānaṃ sāvakānaṃ vā tassa suttassa daisakānaṃ adhippāyau| byañ‍jananti byañ‍janaina, karaṇai hi aitaṃ pac‍cattaṃ| kāmañ‍ca sabbai hārā byañ‍janavicayā, ayaṃ pana visaisatau byañ‍janadvārainaiva atthapariyaisanāti katvā ‘‘byañ‍jana’’nti vuttaṃ| tathā hi vakkhati – ‘‘byañ‍janaina suttassa nairuttañ‍ca adhippāyau ca nidānañ‍ca pubbāparānusandhi ca gavaisitabbā’’ti| athāti padapūraṇamattaṃ| daisanānidānanti nidadāti phalanti nidānaṃ, kāraṇaṃ, yaina kāraṇaina daisanā pavattā, taṃ daisanāya pavattinimittanti atthau| pubbāparānusandhīti pubbaina ca aparaina ca anusandhi| ‘‘pubbāparaina sandhī’’tipi  pāṭhau, suttassa pubbabhāgaina aparabhāgaṃ saṃsanditvā kathananti atthau| saṅgītivasaina vā pubbāparabhūtaihi suttantaraihi saṃvaṇṇiyamānassa suttassa saṃsandanaṃ pubbāparānusandhi| yañ‍ca pubbapadaina parapadassa sambandhanaṃ, ayampi pubbāparānusandhi| aisau hārau catubyūhauti aivaṃ nibbacanādhippāyādīnaṃ catun‍naṃ vibhāvanalakkhaṇau catubyūhau hārau nāmāti atthau|

7. aikamhi padaṭṭhānaiti aikasmiṃ ārambhadhātuādikai parak‍kamadhātuādīnaṃ padaṭṭhānabhūtai dhammai daisanāruḷhai sati| pariyaisati saisakaṃ padaṭṭhānanti tassa visabhāgatāya aggahaṇaina vā saisakaṃ pamādādīnaṃ āsan‍nakāraṇattā padaṭṭhānabhūtaṃ kausaj‍jādikaṃ dhammantaraṃ pariyaisati pañ‍ñāya gavaisati, pariyaisitvā ca saṃvaṇṇanāya yaujaintau daisanaṃ  āvaṭṭati paṭipakkhaiti vīriyārambhādimukhaina āraddhasuttaṃ vuttanayaina pamādādivasaina niddisantau daisanaṃ paṭipakkhatau āvaṭṭaiti nāma| āvaṭṭau nāma sau hārauti daisanāya gahitadhammānaṃ sabhāgavisabhāgadhammavasaina āvaṭṭanalakkhaṇau āvaṭṭau hārau nāmāti atthau|

8. dhammanti sabhāvadhammaṃ, taṃ kusalādivasaina anaikavidhaṃ| padaṭṭhānanti yasmiṃ patiṭṭhitai uttari guṇavisaisai adhigacchati, taṃ visaisādhigamanakāraṇaṃ| bhūminti puthuj‍janabhūmi dassanabhūmīti aivamādikaṃ bhūmiṃ| vibhaj‍jataiti vibhāgaina kathaiti| sādhāraṇaiti dassanapahātabbādināmavasaina vā puthuj‍janasautāpan‍nādivatthuvasaina vā sādhāraṇai avisiṭṭhai samānaiti atthau| vuttavipariyāyaina asādhāraṇā vaiditabbā| naiyyau vibhattīti yathāvuttadhammādīnaṃ vibhajanau ayaṃ hārau vibhattīti ñātabbauti atthau| tasmā saṃkilaisadhammai vaudānadhammai ca sādhāraṇāsādhāraṇatau padaṭṭhānatau bhūmitau ca vibhajanalakkhaṇau ‘‘vibhattihārau’’ti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

9. niddiṭṭhaiti kathitai suttai āgatai, saṃvaṇṇitai vā| bhāvitaiti yathā uppan‍nasadisā uppan‍nāti vuc‍canti, aivaṃ bhāvitasadisai bhāvaitabbaiti atthau| pahīnaiti aitthāpi aisaiva nayau| parivattati paṭipakkhaiti vuttānaṃ dhammānaṃ yai paṭipakkhā, taisaṃ vasaina parivattaitīti atthau| aivaṃ niddiṭṭhānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭipakkhatau parivattanalakkhaṇau ‘‘parivattanau hārau’’ti vaiditabbau|

10. vividhāni aikasmiṃyaiva atthai vacanāni vivacanāni, vivacanāni aiva vaivacanāni, pariyāyasaddāti atthau| tāni vaivacanāni| bahūnīti anaikāni| tu-saddau avadhāraṇai| taina bahū aiva pariyāyasaddā vaivacanahārayaujanāyaṃ kathaitabbā, na katipayāti dassaiti| suttai vuttānīti navavidhasuttantasaṅkhātai taipiṭakai buddhavacanai bhāsitāni| aitthāpi tu-saddassa atthau ānaitvā yaujaitabbau, taina pāḷiyaṃ āgatāniyaiva vaivacanāni gahaitabbānīti vuttaṃ hauti| aikadhammassāti aikassa padatthassa| yau jānāti suttavidūti yathā ‘‘sappissa jānāhī’’ti vuttai ‘‘sappinā vicāraihi, sappiṃ daihi, daithā’’ti vā āṇāpaitīti atthau, aivaṃ yau suttakauvidau dhammakathikau aikassa  atthassa bahūpi pariyāyasaddai vicāraiti vibhāvaiti yaujaitīti atthau| vaivacanau nāma sau hārauti tassa atthassa vuttappakārapariyāyasaddayaujanālakkhaṇau vaivacanahārau nāma| tasmā aikasmiṃ atthai anaikapariyāyasaddayaujanālakkhaṇau ‘‘vaivacanahārau’’ti vaiditabbaṃ|

11. dhammanti khandhādidhammaṃ| pañ‍ñattīhīti pañ‍ñāpanaihi pakāraihi ñāpanaihi, asaṅkaratau vā ṭhapanaihi| vividhāhīti nikkhaipappabhavādivasaina anaikavidhāhi| sau ākārauti yau aikassaivatthassa nikkhaipappabhavapañ‍ñattiādivasaina anaikāhi pañ‍ñattīhi pañ‍ñāpanākārau| ñaiyyau pañ‍ñatti nāma hārauti pañ‍ñattihārau nāmāti ñātabbau| tasmā aikaikassa dhammassa anaikāhi pañ‍ñattīhi pañ‍ñāpaitabbākāravibhāvanalakkhaṇau ‘‘pañ‍ñattihārau’’ti vaiditabbaṃ|

12. paṭic‍cuppādauti paṭic‍casamuppādau| indriyakhandhāti indriyāni ca khandhā ca| dhātuāyatanāti dhātuyau ca āyatanāni ca| aitaihīti yau dvādasapadikau pac‍cayākārau yāni ca dvāvīsatindriyāni yai ca pañ‍cakkhandhā yā ca aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau yāni ca dvādasāyatanāni, aitaihi suttai āgatapadatthamukhaina niddhāriyamānaihi| autarati yauti yau saṃvaṇṇanānayau augāhati, paṭic‍casamuppādādikai anupavisatīti atthau| autaraṇau nāma sau hārauti yau yathāvuttau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau, sau autaraṇahārau nāma| ca-saddaina caittha suñ‍ñatamukhādīnaṃ gāthāyaṃ avuttānampi saṅgahau daṭṭhabbau| aivaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādādimukhaihi suttatthassa autaraṇalakkhaṇau autaraṇau hārau nāmāti vaiditabbaṃ|

13. vissaj‍jitamhīti buddhādīhi byākatai| pañhaiti ñātuṃ icchitai atthai| gāthāyanti gāthāruḷhai| idañ‍ca pucchantā yaibhuyyaina gāthābandhavasaina pucchantīti katvā vuttaṃ| yamārabbhāti sā pana gāthā yaṃ atthaṃ ārabbha adhikic‍ca pucchitā, tassa atthassa| suddhāsuddhaparikkhāti padaṃ saudhitaṃ, ārambhau na saudhitau, padañ‍ca saudhitaṃ ārambhau ca saudhitauti aivaṃ padādīnaṃ saudhitāsaudhitabhāvavicārau| hārau sau saudhanau nāmāti yathāvuttavicārau saudhanau hārau nāma| aivaṃ suttai padapadatthapañhārambhānaṃ saudhanalakkhaṇau ‘‘saudhanau hārau’’ti vaiditabbaṃ|

14. aikattatāyāti  aikassa bhāvau aikattaṃ, aikattamaiva aikattatā, tāya aikattatāya| aika-saddau caittha samānasaddapariyāyau, tasmā sāmañ‍ñaināti atthau| visiṭṭhā mattā vimattā, vimattāva vaimattaṃ, tassa bhāvau vaimattatā, tāya vaimattatāya, visaisaināti atthau| tai na vikappayitabbāti yai dhammā ‘‘dukkhaṃ samudayau’’tiādinā sāmañ‍ñaina, ‘‘jāti jarā kāmataṇhā bhavataṇhā’’tiādinā visaisaina ca suttai daisitā, tai ‘‘kimaittha sāmañ‍ñaṃ, kau vā visaisau’’ti aivaṃ sāmañ‍ñavisaisavikappanavasaina na vikappayitabbā| kasmā? sāmañ‍ñavisaisakappanāya vauhārabhāvaina anavaṭṭhānatau kāladisāvisaisādīnaṃ viya apaikkhāsiddhitau ca| yathā hi ‘‘aj‍ja hiyyau svai’’ti vuc‍camānā kālavisaisā anavaṭṭhitasabhāvā ‘‘purimā disā pacchimā disā’’ti vuc‍camānā disāvisaisā ca, aivaṃ sāmañ‍ñavisaisāpi| tathā hi ‘‘idaṃ dukkha’’nti vuc‍camānaṃ jātiādiapaikkhāya sāmañ‍ñampi samānaṃ sac‍cāpaikkhāya visaisau hauti| aisa nayau samudayādīsupi| aisau hārau adhiṭṭhānauti aivaṃ suttāgatānaṃ dhammānaṃ avikappanavasaina sāmañ‍ñavisaisaniddhāraṇalakkhaṇau adhiṭṭhānau hārau nāmāti atthau|

15. yai dhammāti yai avij‍jādikā pac‍cayadhammā| yaṃ dhammanti yaṃ saṅkhārādikaṃ pac‍cayuppan‍nadhammaṃ| janayantīti nibbattainti| pac‍cayāti sahajātapac‍cayabhāvaina| paramparatauti paramparapac‍cayabhāvaina, anurūpasantānaghaṭanavasaina pac‍cayau hutvāti atthau| upanissayakauṭi hi idhādhippaitā| purimasmiṃ avasiṭṭhau pac‍cayabhāvau| haitumavakaḍḍhayitvāti taṃ yathāvuttapac‍cayasaṅkhātaṃ janakādibhaidabhin‍naṃ haituṃ ākaḍḍhitvā suttatau niddhāraitvā yau saṃvaṇṇanāsaṅkhātau, aisau hārau parikkhārauti aivaṃ suttai āgatadhammānaṃ parikkhārasaṅkhātai haitupac‍cayai niddhāraitvā saṃvaṇṇanalakkhaṇau parikkhārau hārauti atthau|

16. yai dhammāti yai sīlādidhammā| yaṃmūlāti yaisaṃ samādhiādīnaṃ mūlabhūtā, tai taisaṃ samādhiādīnaṃ padaṭṭhānabhāvaina samāraupayitabbāti sambandhau| yai caikatthā pakāsitā munināti yai ca rāgavirāgācaitauvimuttisaikkhaphalakāmadhātusamatik‍kamanādisaddā anāgāmiphalatthatāya aikatthā buddhamuninā paridīpitā, tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñavaivacanabhāvaina samāraupayitabbāti sambandhau| samāraupanañ‍caittha suttai yathārutavasaina  niddhāraṇavasaina vā gayhamānassa sikkhattayasaṅkhātassa  sīlādikkhandhattayassa pariyāyantaravibhāvanamukhaina bhāvanāpāripūrikathanaṃ, bhāvanāpāripūrī ca pahātabbassa pahānaināti pahānasamāraupanāpi atthatau dassitā aiva hauti| aisa samāraupanau hārauti aisa suttai āgatadhammānaṃ padaṭṭhānavaivacanabhāvanāpahānasamāraupanavicāraṇalakkhaṇau samāraupanau nāma hārauti atthau|

nayasaṅkhaipau

17. aivaṃ gāthābandhavasaina sauḷasapi hārai niddisitvā idāni nayai niddisituṃ ‘‘taṇhañ‍cā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha taṇhañ‍ca avij‍jampi cāti suttai āgataṃ atthatau niddhāraṇavasaina vā gahitaṃ taṇhaṃ avij‍jañ‍ca yau naitīti sambandhau| yau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau taṃ naiti saṃkilaisapakkhaṃ pāpaiti saṃkilaisavasaina suttatthaṃ yaujaitīti adhippāyau| samathaināti samādhinā| vipassanāyāti pañ‍ñāya, yau naiti vaudānapakkhaṃ pāpaiti, tathā suttatthaṃ yaujaitīti adhippāyau| sac‍caihi yaujayitvāti nayantau ca taṇhā ca avij‍jā ca bhavamūlakattā samudayasac‍caṃ, avasaisā taibhūmakadhammā dukkhasac‍caṃ, samathavipassanā maggasac‍caṃ, taina pattabbā asaṅkhatadhātu niraudhasac‍canti aivaṃ imaihi catūhi sac‍caihi yaujaitvā| ayaṃ nayau nandiyāvaṭṭauti yau taṇhāvij‍jāhi saṃkilaisapakkhassa suttatthassa samathavipassanāhi vaudānapakkhassa catusac‍cayaujanamukhaina nayanalakkhaṇau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau, ayaṃ nandiyāvaṭṭau nayau nāmāti atthau| aittha ca nayassa bhūmi gāthāyaṃ ‘‘nayau’’ti vuttā, tasmā saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisauti vuttaṃ| na hi atthanayau saṃvaṇṇanā, catusac‍capaṭivaidhassa anurūpau pubbabhāgai anugāhaṇanayau atthanayau| tassa pana yā ugghaṭitañ‍ñuādīnaṃ vasaina taṇhādimukhaina nayabhūmiracanā, tattha nayavauhārau|

18. akusalaiti dvādasacittuppādasaṅgahitai sabbaipi akusalai dhammai| samūlaihīti attanau mūlaihi, laubhadausamauhaihīti atthau| kusalaiti sabbaipi catubhūmakai kusalai dhammai| kusalamūlaihīti kusalaihi alaubhādimūlaihi yau naiti| nayantau ca kusalākusalaṃ māyāmarīciādayau viya abhūtaṃ na hautīti bhūtaṃ| paṭaghaṭādayau  viya na sammutisac‍camattanti tathaṃ| akusalassa iṭṭhavipākatābhāvatau kusalassa ca aniṭṭhavipākatābhāvatau vipākai sati avisaṃvādakattā avitathaṃ naiti| aivamaitaisaṃ tiṇṇampi padānaṃ kusalākusalavisaisanatā daṭṭhabbā| atha vā akusalamūlaihi akusalāni kusalamūlaihi ca kusalāni nayantau ayaṃ nayau bhūtaṃ tathaṃ avitathaṃ naiti, cattāri sac‍cāni niddhāraitvā yaujaitīti atthau| dukkhādīni hi bādhakādibhāvatau  añ‍ñathābhāvābhāvaina bhūtāni, sac‍casabhāvattā tathāni, avisaṃvādanatau avitathāni| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathānī’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1090)| tipukkhalaṃ taṃ nayaṃ āhūti yau akusalamūlaihi saṃkilaisapakkhassa kusalamūlaihi vaudānapakkhassa suttatthassa catusac‍cayaujanamukhaina nayanalakkhaṇau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau, taṃ tipukkhalaṃ nayanti vadantīti atthau|

19. vipal‍lāsaihīti asubhai subhantiādinayappavattaihi catūhi vipal‍lāsaihi| kilaisaiti kilissanti vibādhiyantīti kilaisā, saṃkiliṭṭhadhammā, saṃkilaisapakkhanti atthau| kaici ‘‘saṃkilaisai’’tipi paṭhanti, kilaisasahitaiti atthau| indriyaihīti saddhādīhi indriyaihi| saddhammaiti paṭipattipaṭivaidhasaddhammai, vaudānapakkhanti atthau| aitaṃ nayanti yau subhasañ‍ñādīhi vipal‍lāsaihi sakalassa saṃkilaisapakkhassa saddhindriyādīhi vaudānapakkhassa catusac‍cayaujanavasaina nayanalakkhaṇau saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisau, aitaṃ nayaṃ nayavidū saddhammanayakauvidā, atthanayakusalā aiva vā sīhavik‍kīḷitaṃ nayanti vadantīti atthau|

20. vaiyyākaraṇaisūti tassa tassa atthanayassa yaujanatthaṃ kataisu suttassa atthavissaj‍janaisūti atthau| tainaivāha ‘‘tahiṃ tahi’’nti| kusalākusalāti vaudāniyā saṃkilaisikā ca tassa tassa nayassa disābhūtadhammā| vuttāti suttatau niddhāraitvā kathitā| manasā vaulaukayataiti tai yathāvuttadhammai cittainaiva ‘‘ayaṃ paṭhamā disā ayaṃ dutiyā disā’’tiādinā tassa tassa nayassa disābhāvaina upaparikkhati, vicāraitīti atthau| ‘‘aulaukayatai tai abahī’’tipi  pāṭhau| tattha taiti tai yathāvuttadhammai| abahīti abbhantaraṃ, cittai aivāti atthau| taṃ khu disālaucanaṃ āhūti aulaukayataiti aittha yadaitaṃ aulaukanaṃ, taṃ disālaucanaṃ nāma nayaṃ vadanti| khu-ti ca nipātau avadhāraṇai| taina aulaukanamaiva ayaṃ nayau, na kauci atthavisaisauti dassaiti|

21. aulaukaitvāti paṭhamādidisābhāvaina upaparikkhitvā| disālaucanaināti disālaucananayaina karaṇabhūtaina| yaina hi vidhinā tassa tassa atthanayassa yaujanāya disā aulaukīyanti, sau vidhi disālaucananti aivaṃ vā aittha atthau daṭṭhabbau| ukkhipiyāti uddharitvā, disābhūtadhammai suttatau niddhāraitvāti atthau| ‘‘ukkhipiya yau samānaitī’’tipi paṭhanti, tassatthau – ‘‘yau taisaṃ disābhūtadhammānaṃ samānayanaṃ karautī’’ti| yanti vā kiriyāparāmasanaṃ| samānaitīti samaṃ, sammā vā ānaiti tassa tassa nayassa yaujanāvasaina| kai pana ānaiti? sabbai kusalākusalai taṃtaṃnayadisābhūtai| ayaṃ nayauti samānaitīti aittha yadaitaṃ taṃtaṃnayadisābhūtadhammānaṃ samānayanaṃ, ayaṃ aṅkusau nāma nayauti atthau| aitañ‍ca dvayaṃ ‘‘vauhāranayau, kammanayau’’ti ca vuc‍cati|

22. aivaṃ hārai nayai ca niddisitvā idāni naisaṃ yaujanak‍kamaṃ dassaintau ‘‘sauḷasa hārā paṭhama’’ntiādimāha| tattha paṭhamaṃ sauḷasa hārā ‘‘yaujaitabbā’’ti vacanasaisau| hārasaṃvaṇṇanā paṭhamaṃ kātabbā byañ‍janapariyaiṭṭhibhāvatauti adhippāyau| disalaucanatauti disālaucanaina, ayamaiva vā pāṭhau| aṅkusaina hīti hi-saddau nipātamattaṃ| saisaṃ uttānamaiva|

dvādasapadaṃ

23. idāni yaisaṃ byañ‍janapadānaṃ atthapadānañ‍ca vasaina dvādasa padāni suttanti vuttaṃ, tāni padāni niddisituṃ ‘‘akkharaṃ pada’’ntiādimāha| tattha apariyausitai padai vaṇṇau akkharaṃ pariyāyavasaina akkharaṇatau asañ‍caraṇatau| na hi vaṇṇassa pariyāyau vij‍jati, atha vaṇṇauti kainaṭṭhaina vaṇṇau? atthasaṃvaṇṇanaṭṭhaina| vaṇṇau aiva hi ittarakhaṇatāya aparāparabhāvaina pavattau padādibhāvaina gayhamānau yathāsambandhaṃ taṃ taṃ atthaṃ vadati| aikakkharaṃ vā padaṃ akkharaṃ, kaici pana ‘‘manasā daisanāvācāya akkharaṇatau akkhara’’nti vadanti|

padanti paj‍jati atthau aitaināti padaṃ, taṃ nāmapadaṃ ākhyātapadaṃ upasaggapadaṃ nipātapadanti catubbidhaṃ| tattha ‘‘phassau vaidanā citta’’nti aivamādikaṃ satvappadhānaṃ nāmapadaṃ| ‘‘phusati vaidayati vijānātī’’ti aivamādikaṃ kiriyāpadhānaṃ ākhyātapadaṃ| kiriyāvisaisaggahaṇanimittaṃ ‘‘pa’’ iti aivamādikaṃ upasaggapadaṃ| kiriyāya satvassa ca sarūpavisaisappakāsanahaitubhūtaṃ ‘‘aiva’’nti aivamādikaṃ nipātapadaṃ|

byañ‍jananti saṅkhaipatau vuttaṃ padābhihitaṃ atthaṃ byañ‍jayatīti byañ‍janaṃ, vākyaṃ| taṃ pana atthatau padasamudāyauti daṭṭhabbaṃ| padamattasavanaipi hi adhikārādivasaina labbhamānaihi padantaraihi anusandhānaṃ katvāva atthasampaṭipatti hautīti vākyamaiva atthaṃ byañ‍jayati| niruttīti ākārābhihitaṃ nibbacanaṃ nirutti|

niddaisauti  nibbacanavitthārau niravasaisadaisanattā niddaisau| padaihi vākyassa vibhāgau ākārau| yadi aivaṃ padatau ākārassa kau visaisauti? apariyausitai vākyai avibhaj‍jamānai vā tadavayavau padaṃ| uc‍cāraṇavasaina pariyausitai vākyai vibhaj‍jamānai vā tadavayavau ākārauti ayamaitaisaṃ visaisau| chaṭṭhaṃ vacanaṃ chaṭṭhavacanaṃ| ākārau chaṭṭhavacanaṃ aitassāti ākārachaṭṭhavacanaṃ, byañ‍janapadaṃ| aittha ca byañ‍jananti imassa padassa anantaraṃ vattabbaṃ ākārapadaṃ niddaisapadānantaraṃ vadantaina ‘‘ākārachaṭṭhavacana’’nti vuttaṃ, padānupubbikaṃ pana icchantaihi taṃ byañ‍janapadānantaramaiva kātabbaṃ| tathā hi vakkhati ‘‘aparimāṇā byañ‍janā aparimāṇā ākārāti, byañ‍janaihi vivarati ākāraihi vibhajatī’’ti ca| kaici pana ‘‘ākārapadabyañ‍jananiruttiyau ca niddaisau’’ti paṭhanti| aittāva byañ‍janaṃ sabbanti yānimāni akkharādīni niddiṭṭhāni, aittakamaiva sabbaṃ byañ‍janaṃ, aitaihi asaṅgahitaṃ byañ‍janaṃ nāma natthīti atthau|

24. saṅkāsanāti saṃkhittaina kāsanā| pakāsanāti paṭhamaṃ kāsanā, kāsīyati dīpīyatīti atthau| iminā hi atthapadadvayaina akkharapadaihi vibhāviyamānau atthākārau gahitau| yasmā akkharaihi suyyamānaihi suṇantānaṃ visaisavidhānassa katattā padapariyausānai  padatthasampaṭipatti hauti| tathā hi vakkhati ‘‘tattha bhagavā akkharaihi saṅkāsaiti padaihi pakāsaitīti, akkharaihi padaihi ca ugghāṭaitī’’ti ca|

vivaraṇāti vitthāraṇā| vibhajanā ca uttānīkammañ‍ca pañ‍ñatti ca vibhajanuttānīkammapañ‍ñatti| tattha vibhajanāti vibhāgakaraṇaṃ, ubhayaināpi niddisanamāha| idha purimanayainaiva byañ‍janākāraihi niddisiyamānau atthākārau dassitauti daṭṭhabbaṃ| uttānīkammaṃ pākaṭakaraṇaṃ| pakāraihi ñāpanaṃ pañ‍ñatti| dvayaināpi paṭiniddisanaṃ kathaiti| aitthāpi niruttiniddaisasaṅkhātaihi byañ‍janapadaihi niddisiyamānau atthākārau vuttau, yau paṭiniddisīyatīti vuc‍cati| aitaihīti aitaihi aiva saṅkāsanādivinimuttassa daisanātthassa abhāvatau| atthauti suttatthau| kammanti ugghaṭanādikammaṃ| suttatthaina hi daisanāya pavattiyamānaina ugghaṭitañ‍ñuādivainaiyyānaṃ cittasantānassa pabaudhanakiriyānibbatti| sau ca suttatthau saṅkāsanādiākārauti| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘atthau kammañ‍ca niddiṭṭha’’nti|

25. tīṇīti liṅgavipal‍lāsaina vuttaṃ, tayauti vuttaṃ hauti| navahi padaihīti navahi kauṭṭhāsaihi| atthau samāyuttauti atthau sammā yuttau na vinā vattati| sabbassa hi buddhavacanassa catusac‍cappakāsanatau atthanayānañ‍ca catusac‍cayaujanavasaina pavattanatau sabbau pāḷiatthau atthanayattayasaṅgahitau saṅkāsanādiākāravisaisavutti cāti|

26. idāni yathāniddiṭṭhai daisanāhārādikai naittippakaraṇassa padatthai sukhaggahaṇatthaṃ gaṇanavasaina paricchinditvā dassaintau ‘‘atthassā’’tiādimāha| tattha catubbīsāti sauḷasa hārā cha byañ‍janapadāni dvai kammanayāti aivaṃ catubbīsa| ubhayanti cha atthapadāni tayau atthanayāti idaṃ navavidhaṃ yathāvuttaṃ catubbīsavidhañ‍cāti aitaṃ ubhayaṃ| saṅkalayitvāti sampiṇḍaitvā| ‘‘saṅkhaipayatau’’tipi pāṭhau, aikatau karauntassāti atthau| aittikāti aitappamāṇā, itau vinimuttau kauci naittipadatthā natthīti atthau|

aivaṃ taittiṃsapadatthāya naittiyā suttassa atthapariyaisanāya yau ‘‘sauḷasa hārā paṭhama’’nti nayaihi paṭhamaṃ hārā saṃvaṇṇaitabbāti hāranayānaṃ saṃvaṇṇanāk‍kamau dassitau, svāyaṃ hāranayānaṃ daisanāk‍kamainaiva  siddhau| aivaṃ siddhai sati ayaṃ ārambhau imamatthaṃ dīpaiti – sabbaipimai hārā nayā ca iminā dassitak‍kamainaiva suttaisu saṃvaṇṇanāvasaina yaujaitabbā, na uppaṭipāṭiyāti|

kiṃ panaittha kāraṇaṃ, yadaitai hārā nayā ca imināva kamaina daisitāti? yadipi nāyamanuyaugau katthaci anuk‍kamai nivisati, api ca dhammadaisanāya nissayaphalatadupāyasarīrabhūtānaṃ assādādīnaṃ vibhāvanasabhāvattā pakatiyā sabbasuttānurūpāti suviñ‍ñaiyyabhāvatau paraisañ‍ca saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisānaṃ vicayahārādīnaṃ patiṭṭhābhāvatau paṭhamaṃ daisanāhārau dassitau|

padapucchāvissaj‍janapubbāparānugītīhi saddhiṃ daisanāhārapadatthānaṃ pavicayasabhāvatāya tassa anantaraṃ vicayau| tathā hi vakkhati ‘‘padaṃ vicinati…pai॰… āṇattiṃ vicinati anugītiṃ vicinatī’’ti|

vicayaina hāraina pavicitānaṃ atthānaṃ yuttāyuttivicāraṇā yuttāti yuttivicāraṇabhāvatau vicayānantaraṃ yuttihārau vuttau| tathā hi vakkhati – ‘‘vicayaina hāraina vicinitvā yuttihāraina yaujaitabba’’nti|

yuttāyuttānaṃyaiva  atthānaṃ upapattianurūpaṃ kāraṇaparamparāya niddhāraṇalakkhaṇaṃ padaṭṭhānacintanaṃ kattabbanti yuttihārānantaraṃ padaṭṭhānahārau dassitau| tathā hi vakkhati – ‘‘yau kauci upanissayau yau kauci pac‍cayau ca, sabbau sau padaṭṭhāna’’nti|

yuttāyuttānaṃ kāraṇaparamparāya pariggahitasabhāvānaṃyaiva ca dhammānaṃ avuttānampi aikalakkhaṇatāya gahaṇaṃ kātabbanti dassanatthaṃ padaṭṭhānānantaraṃ lakkhaṇau hārau vuttau| tathā hi lakkhaṇahāravibhaṅgai ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā’’tiādinā paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ dassaitvā ‘‘aivaṃ yai dhammā aikalakkhaṇā’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

atthatau niddhāritānampi dhammānaṃ nibbacanādīni vattabbāni, na suttai sarūpatau āgatānamaivāti dassanatthaṃ lakkhaṇānantaraṃ catubyūhau vuttau| aivañhi niravasaisatau atthāvabaudhau hauti, aivañ‍ca katvā ‘‘yadā hi bhikkhu atthassa ca nāmaṃ jānāti dhammassa ca nāmaṃ jānāti tathā tathā naṃ abhiniraupaitī’’ti anavasaisapariyādānaṃ vakkhati| tathā ‘‘punappunaṃ gabbhamupaitī’’ti aittha ‘‘yai jarāmaraṇaina aṭṭiyitukāmā bhavissanti, tai bhavissanti bhaujanai mattañ‍ñunau indriyaisu guttadvārā’’tiādinā sammāpaṭipattiṃ adhippāyabhāvaina vakkhati|

nibbacanādhippāyanidānavacanaihi saddhiṃ suttai padatthānaṃ suttantarasaṃsandanasaṅkhātai pubbāparavicārai dassitai taisaṃ sabhāgavisabhāgadhammantarāvaṭṭanaṃ sukhaina sak‍kā dassaitunti catubyūhānantaraṃ āvaṭṭau vuttau| tainaiva hi ‘‘ārambhatha nik‍kamathā’’ti gāthāyaṃ ārambhanik‍kamanabuddhasāsanayaugadhunanaihi vīriyasamādhipañ‍ñindriyāni niddhāraitvā tadanuyaugassa mūlaṃ ‘‘pamādau’’ti suttantarai dassitau pamādau āvaṭṭitau|

sabhāgavisabhāgadhammāvaṭṭanai niyaujitai sādhāraṇāsādhāraṇavasaina saṃkilaisavaudānadhammānaṃ padaṭṭhānatau bhūmitau ca vibhāgau sak‍kā sukhaina yaujitunti āvaṭṭānantaraṃ vibhattihārau vuttau| yatau vibhattihāravibhaṅgai ‘‘katamai dhammā sādhāraṇā? dvai dhammā sādhāraṇā, nāmasādhāraṇā vatthusādhāraṇā cā’’ti ārabhitvā ‘‘micchattaniyatānaṃ sattānaṃ aniyatānañ‍ca sattānaṃ dassanappahātabbā kilaisā sādhāraṇā, puthuj‍janassa sautāpan‍nassa ca kāmarāgabyāpādā sādhāraṇā’’tiādinā sabhāgavisabhāgapariyāyavantaiyaiva dhammai vibhajissati|

sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jadhammānaṃ  sappaṭibhāgābhāvatau taisaṃ vibhāgai katai suttāgatai dhammai akasiraina paṭipakkhatau parivattaituṃ sak‍kāti vibhattianantaraṃ parivattanahārau vuttau| tathā hi ‘‘sammādiṭṭhissa purisapuggalassa micchādiṭṭhi nij‍jiṇṇā bhavatī’’ti paṭivibhattasabhāvai aiva dhammai parivattanahāravibhaṅgai udāharissati|

paṭipakkhatau parivattitāpi dhammā pariyāyavacanaihi baudhaitabbā, na suttai āgatāyaivāti dassanatthaṃ parivattanānantaraṃ vaivacanahārau vuttau|

aivaṃ tai dhammā pariyāyasaddataupi vibhāvitā hauntīti pariyāyatau pakāsitānaṃ dhammānaṃ pabhaidatau pañ‍ñattivasaina vibhajanaṃ sukhaina sak‍kā ñātunti vaivacanahārānantaraṃ pañ‍ñattihārau vuttau| tathā hi suttai āgatadhammānaṃ pariyāyapañ‍ñattivibhāgaṃ subaudhanañ‍ca pañ‍ñattihāravibhaṅgai vakkhati|

pabhāvapariñ‍ñādipañ‍ñattivibhāgamukhaina  paṭic‍casamuppādasac‍cādidhammavibhāgai katai suttai āgatadhammānaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādādimukhaina avadhāraṇaṃ sak‍kā dassaitunti pañ‍ñattianantaraṃ autaraṇau hārau vuttau| tathā hi ‘‘uddhaṃ adhau’’ti gāthaṃ uddisitvā ‘‘vippamuttau’’ti padaina asaikkhaṃ vij‍jaṃ niddhāraitvā ‘‘vij‍juppādā avij‍jāniraudhau’’tiādinā paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ udāharissati|

dhātāyatanādīsu autāritānaṃ suttai padatthānaṃ pucchārambhavisaudhanaṃ sak‍kā sukhaina sampādaitunti autaraṇānantaraṃ saudhanau hārau vuttau| tathā hi vakkhati – ‘‘yattha aivaṃ suddhau ārambhau, sau pañhau vissaj‍jitau bhavatī’’tiādi|

visaudhitaisu suttai padapadatthaisu tattha labbhamānasāmañ‍ñavisaisabhāvau sukarau hautīti dassaituṃ saudhanānantaraṃ adhiṭṭhānau hārau dassitau| saudhanau hi adhiṭṭhānassa bahūpakārau, tatau aiva hi ‘‘yathā yathā vā pana pucchitaṃ, tathā tathā vissaj‍jayitabba’’nti vakkhati|

sāmañ‍ñavisaisabhūtaisu sādhāraṇāsādhāraṇaisu dhammaisu pavaiditaisu parikkhārasaṅkhātassa sādhāraṇāsādhāraṇarūpassa pac‍cayahaiturāsissa pabhaidau suviñ‍ñaiyyauti adhiṭṭhānānantaraṃ parikkhārau vuttau| tathā hi vakkhati ‘‘asādhāraṇalakkhaṇau haitu, sādhāraṇalakkhaṇau pac‍cayau| yathā kiṃ bhavai, yathā aṅkurassa nibbattiyā bījaṃ asādhāraṇaṃ, pathavī āpau ca sādhāraṇā’’tiādi|

asādhāraṇai sādhāraṇai ca kāraṇai dassitai tassa attanau phalaisu kāraṇākārau taisaṃ haituphalānaṃ pabhaidatau daisanākārau bhāvaitabbapahātabbadhammānaṃ bhāvanāpahānāni ca niddhāraitvā vuc‍camānāni sammā suttassa atthaṃ tathattāvabaudhāya saṃvattantīti parikkhārānantaraṃ samāraupanau hārau dassitauti| idaṃ hārānaṃ anuk‍kamakāraṇaṃ|

nayānaṃ pana vainaiyyattayappayaujitattā atthanayattayūpadaisassa tadanuk‍kamainaiva nandiyāvaṭṭādīnaṃ tiṇṇaṃ atthanayānaṃ kamau vaiditabbau| ugghaṭitañ‍ñuādayau hi tayau vainaiyyā nandiyāvaṭṭādayau payaujainti| tasmā tai uddaisaniddaisapaṭiniddaisā viya yathāk‍kamaṃ taisaṃ upakārāya  savaṃttantīti| tathā hi naisaṃ cattārau cha aṭṭha ca mūlapadā niddiṭṭhā| itarassa pana nayadvayassa atthanayattayassa bhūmiyā ālaucanaṃ tassa tattha samānayanañ‍cāti iminā kāraṇaina uddaisak‍kamau vaiditabbau| na hi sak‍kā anaulaukaitvā samānaitunti|

aitaparamatā ca hārānaṃ aittakaihi pakāravisaisaihi atthanayattayasahitaihi suttassa atthau niddhāriyamānau vainaiyyānaṃ alamanuttarāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā samadhigamāyāti vaiditabbau| dassanabhūmisamanuppattiatthā hi naittippakaraṇadaisanāti| atha vā aitadantaugadhattā sabbaisaṃ suttassa saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisānaṃ aittāvatā hārānaṃ daṭṭhabbā| yattakā hi suttassa saṃvaṇṇanāvisaisā, sabbai tai naittiupadaisāyattāti vuttauvāyamatthau|

tathā hi yai kaici suttassa saṃvaṇṇanāpakārā niddisīyanti| saiyyathidaṃ – suttassa samuṭṭhānaṃ vattabbaṃ, adhippāyau vibhāvaitabbau, anaikadhā padatthau saṃvaṇṇaitabbau, vidhi anuvādau ca vaiditabbau, viraudhau samādhātabbau, anusandhiyā anurūpaṃ nigamaitabbanti| tathā suttassa payaujanaṃ piṇḍatthau padatthau anusandhi caudanā parihārau ca atthaṃ vadantaina vattabbāti| tathā upaugghāṭapadaviggahapadatthacālanāpac‍cupaṭṭhānāni vattabbānīti|

tathā tissau kathā aikanāḷikā caturassā nisin‍navattikā| tattha pāḷiṃ vatvā aikaikapadassa atthakathanaṃ aikanāḷikā nāma|

paṭipakkhaṃ  dassaitvā paṭipakkhassa upamaṃ dassaitvā sapakkhaṃ dassaitvā sapakkhassa upamaṃ dassaitvā kathanaṃ caturassā nāma|

visabhāgadhammavasainaiva pariyausānaṃ gantvā puna sabhāgadhammavasainaiva pariyausānagamanaṃ nisin‍navattikā nāma|

bhaidakathāya tatvakathāya pariyāyavacanaihi ca suttaṃ saṃvaṇṇaitabbanti ca aivamādayau| taisampi aitthaiva avaraudhau, yasmā tai idha katipayahārasaṅgahitāti|

nayānaṃ pana yasmā ugghaṭitañ‍ñuādayau tayau aiva vainaiyyā sac‍cābhisamayabhāginau tadatthāya ca atthanayadaisanā, tasmā satipi saṃkilaisavaudānadhammānaṃ  yathāvuttamūlapadabhaidatau vaḍḍhaitvā vibhajitabbappakārai tathā mūlapadāni avaḍḍhaitvā vainaiyyattayavasainaiva aitaparamatā vuttā| navasu navasu aiva hi mūlapadaisu sabbaisaṃ saṃkilaisavaudānadhammānaṃ antaugadhabhāvatau na tāni vaḍḍhaitabbāni vainaiyyattayādhikāratau na hāpaitabbānīti nayānaṃ aitaparamatā daṭṭhabbā|

kammanayānaṃ pana ālaucanasamānayanatau añ‍ñassa pakārantarassa asambhavatau aitaparamatā| haitvādīti aittha ādisaddaina phalabhūmiupanisāsabhāgavisabhāgalakkhaṇanayādayau pariggahitā| taisu haitūti kāraṇaṃ, yau dhammautipi vuc‍cati, sau pana pac‍cayabhāvaina aikavidhau| kārakau sampāpakauti duvidhau| puna kārakau ñāpakau sampāpakauti tividhau| haituhaitu pac‍cayahaitu uttamahaitu sādhāraṇahaitūti catubbidhau| pac‍cayadhammau kusalau akusalau saddau ariyamaggauti pañ‍cavidhau| tathā sabhāgahaitu asabhāgahaitu ajjhattikahaitu bāhirahaitu janakahaitu pariggāhakahaitu sādhāraṇahaitu asādhāraṇahaitu samanantarahaitu paramparahaitu sahajātahaitu asahajātahaitu sāsavahaitu anāsavahaitūtiādinā anaikavidhau cāti vaiditabbau|

phalampi pac‍cayuppan‍nabhāvaina aikavidhaṃ| adhigantabbataupi sampāpakahaituvasaina phalapariyāyau labbhatīti nibbattaitabbaadhigantabbabhāvatau duvidhaṃ| ñāpaitabbanibbattaitabbapattabbatau tividhaṃ| pac‍cayuppan‍navipākakiriyāvacanatthanibbānavasaina pañ‍cavidhaṃ| sabhāgahaitunibbattaṃ asabhāgahaitunibbattanti aivamādivasaina anaikavidhañ‍cāti vaiditabbaṃ| tathā laukiyaṃ laukuttaranti| tattha laukuttaraṃ cattāri sāmañ‍ñaphalāni| laukiyaphalaṃ duvidhaṃ kāyikaṃ mānasañ‍ca| tattha kāyikaṃ pañ‍cadvārikaṃ, avasiṭṭhaṃ mānasaṃ| yañ‍ca tāya tāya suttadaisanāya sādhaitabbaṃ, tadapi phalanti|

bhūmīti sāsavabhūmi anāsavabhūmi saṅkhatabhūmi asaṅkhatabhūmi dassanabhūmi bhāvanābhūmi puthuj‍janabhūmi saikkhabhūmi asaikkhabhūmi sāvakabhūmi pac‍caikabuddhabhūmi sammāsambuddhabhūmi jhānabhūmi asamāhitabhūmi paṭipaj‍jamānabhūmi paṭipan‍nabhūmi paṭhamābhūmi yāva catutthībhūmi kāmāvacarabhūmi yāva laukuttarabhūmīti bahuvidhā| tattha sāsavabhūmi parittamahaggatā dhammā| anāsavabhūmi appamāṇā dhammā| saṅkhatabhūmi nibbānavaj‍jā sabbai sabhāvadhammā| asaṅkhatabhūmi appac‍cayā  dhammā| dassanabhūmi paṭhamamaggaphaladhammā| bhāvanābhūmi avasiṭṭhamaggaphaladhammā| puthuj‍janabhūmi hīnamajjhimā dhammā| saikkhabhūmi cattārau ariyamaggadhammā haiṭṭhimā ca tayau phaladhammā| asaikkhabhūmi aggaphaladhammā| sāvakapac‍caikabuddhabuddhadhammā sāvakādibhūmiyau| jhānabhūmi jhānadhammā| asamāhitabhūmi jhānavaj‍jitā dhammā| paṭipaj‍jamānabhūmi maggadhammā| paṭipan‍nabhūmi phaladhammā| paṭhamādibhūmiyau saha phalaina cattārau maggā apariyāpan‍nā dhammā ‘‘paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā’’tiādivacanatau| kāmāvacarādibhūmiyau kāmāvacarādidhammā| yai ca dhammā taisaṃ taisaṃ hāranayānaṃ patiṭṭhānabhāvaina suttaisu niddhārīyanti, taipi bhūmiyauti viñ‍ñātabbā|

upanisāti balavakāraṇaṃ, yau upanissayapac‍cayauti vuc‍cati| yañ‍ca sandhāya suttai ‘‘dukkhūpanisā saddhā saddhūpanisaṃ ‘sīla’nti yāva vimuttūpanisaṃ vimuttiñāṇadassana’’nti vuttaṃ| api ca upanisāti tasmiṃ tasmiṃ samayai siddhantai hadayabhūtaṃ abbhantaraṃ vuc‍cati| idhāpi naittihadayaṃ, yaṃ sammā pariggaṇhantā dhammakathikā tasmiṃ tasmiṃ suttai āgatadhammamukhaina sabbahāranayayaujanāya samatthā haunti| kiṃ panaitaṃ naittihadayaṃ? yadidaṃ aitassaiva taittiṃsavidhassa pakaraṇapadatthasauḷasassa aṭṭhavīsatividhapaṭṭhānavibhaṅgasahitassa visayau saha nimittavibhāgaina asaṅkaratau vavatthitau|

saiyyathidaṃ – daisanāhārassa assādādayau visayau, tassa assādādivibhāvanalakkhaṇattā| tassa assādau sukhaṃ saumanassanti aivamādivibhāgau, tassa nimittaṃ iṭṭhārammaṇādi, ayañ‍ca atthau daisanāhāravicayahāraniddaisavaṇṇanāyaṃ vitthāratau pakāsitau aiva| suttai āgatadhammassa sabhāgavisabhāgadhammāvaṭṭanavisayau āvaṭṭahārau, tadubhayaāvaṭṭanalakkhaṇattā| suttai āgatadhammānaṃ pac‍canīkadhammavisayau  parivattanahārau, paṭipakkhadhammaparivattanalakkhaṇattā| padaṭṭhānaparikkhāraisu āsan‍nakāraṇaṃ upanissayakāraṇañ‍ca padaṭṭhānaṃ, haitu parikkhārauti ayamaitaisaṃ visaisau|

sabhāgavisabhāgadhammā ca taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ anukūlapaṭikūladhammā yathāk‍kamaṃ vaiditabbā| yathā – sammādiṭṭhiyā sammāsaṅkappau sabhāgau, micchāsaṅkappau visabhāgauti iminā nayaina sabbaṃ sabhāgavisabhāgatau vaiditabbaṃ|

lakkhaṇanti  sabhāvau| sau hāranayānaṃ niddaisai vibhāvitau aiva|

yaṃ panaitaṃ haituādivisaisavinimuttaṃ hāranayānaṃ yaujanānibandhanaṃ, sau nayau| yathāha – lakkhaṇahārai ‘‘aivaṃ yai dhammā aikalakkhaṇā kic‍catau ca lakkhaṇatau ca sāmañ‍ñatau cā’’tiādi| tathā vicayaina hāraina vicinitvā yuttihāraina yaujaitabbāti| tathā saudhanahārādīsu suddhau ārambhau hauti, sau pañhau vissaj‍jitau bhavatīti aivamādi| aikattādayaupi nayā idha nayauti gahaitabbā|

aivaṃ haituphalādīni upadhāraitvā naisaṃ vasaina tattha tattha suttai labbhamānapadatthaniddhāraṇamukhaina yathālakkhaṇaṃ aitai hārā nayā ca yaujaitabbā| visaisatau pana padaṭṭhānaparikkhārā haituvasaina| daisanāvicayacatubyūhasamāraupanā haituphalavasaina| tathā vaivacanapañ‍ñattiautaraṇasaudhanā phalavasainaivāti kaici| vibhatti haitubhūmivasaina| parivattau visabhāgavasaina| āvaṭṭau sabhāgavisabhāgavasaina| lakkhaṇayuttiadhiṭṭhānā nayavasaina yaujaitabbāti| aittāvatā ca yaṃ vuttaṃ –

‘‘sāmañ‍ñatau visaisaina, padatthau lakkhaṇaṃ kamau|

aittāvatā ca haitvādī, vaiditabbā hi viñ‍ñunā’’ti||

ayaṃ gāthā vuttatthā hauti|

niddaisavāravaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

4. paṭiniddaisavāravaṇṇanā

1. daisanāhāravibhaṅgavaṇṇanā

5. aivaṃ  hārādayau sukhaggahaṇatthaṃ gāthābandhavasaina sarūpatau niddisitvā idāni taisu hārai tāva paṭiniddaisavasaina vibhajituṃ ‘‘tattha katamau daisanāhārau’’tiādi āraddhaṃ| tattha katamauti kathaitukamyatāpucchā| daisanāhārauti pucchitabbadhammanidassanaṃ| kiñ‍cāpi daisanāhārau niddaisavārai sarūpatau dassitau, paṭiniddaisassa pana visayaṃ  dassaintau ‘‘assādādīnavatā’’ti gāthaṃ aikadaisaina pac‍cāmasati| ayaṃ daisanāhārau pubbāparāpaikkhau| tattha pubbāpaikkhattai ‘‘katamau daisanāhārau’’ti pucchitvā ‘‘assādādīnavatā’’ti sarūpatau dassitassa nigamanaṃ hauti| parāpaikkhattai pana ‘‘ayaṃ daisanāhārau kiṃ daisayatī’’ti daisanākiriyāya kattuniddaisau hauti| taina daisanāhārassa anvatthasañ‍ñataṃ dassaiti| daisayatīti saṃvaṇṇaiti, vitthāraitīti atthau|

idāni anaina daisaitabbadhammai sarūpatau dassaintau ‘‘assāda’’ntiādimāha, taṃ pubbai vuttanayattā uttānamaiva| tasmā itau parampi avuttamaiva vaṇṇayissāma| ‘‘kattha pana āgatai assādādikai ayaṃ hārau saṃvaṇṇaitī’’ti anuyaugaṃ manasikatvā daisanāhāraina saṃvaṇṇaitabbadhammaṃ dassaintau ‘‘dhammaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmī’’tiādikaṃ sabbapariyattidhammasaṅgāhakaṃ bhagavatau chachak‍kadaisanaṃ aikadaisaina dassaiti|

tattha dhammanti ayaṃ dhamma-saddau pariyattisac‍casamādhipañ‍ñāpakatipuñ‍ñāpattiñaiyyādīsu bahūsu atthaisu diṭṭhappayaugau| tathā hi ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇātī’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 5.73) pariyattidhammai dissati| ‘‘diṭṭhadhammau pattadhammau’’tiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.299; mahāva॰ 18) sac‍cai| ‘‘aivaṃdhammā tai bhagavantau ahaisu’’ntiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 2.13, 145) samādhimhi| ‘‘sac‍caṃ dhammau dhiti cāgau’’ti aivamādīsu (jā॰ 1.1.57; 1.2.147-148) pañ‍ñāyaṃ| ‘‘jātidhammānaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattāna’’nti aivamādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 2.398; ma॰ ni॰ 1.131) pakatiyaṃ| ‘‘dhammau havai rakkhati dhammacāri’’ntiādīsu (jā॰ 1.10.102; 1.15.385) puñ‍ñai| ‘‘cattārau pārājikā dhammā’’ti aivamādīsu (pārā॰ 233) āpattiyaṃ| ‘‘kusalā dhammā akusalādhammā’’tiādīsu (dha॰ sa॰ tikamātikā 1) ñaiyyai| idha pana pariyattiyaṃ daṭṭhabbauti (ma॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.mūlapariyāyasuttavaṇṇanā; dha॰ sa॰ aṭṭha॰ cittuppādakaṇḍa 1; bu॰ vaṃ॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.1)|

vauti pana ayaṃ vau-saddau ‘‘handa dāni, bhikkhavai, pavāraimi vau’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.215) aittha upayaugatthai āgatau| ‘‘san‍nipatitānaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, dvayaṃ karaṇīya’’ntiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.273) karaṇatthai| ‘‘yai hi vau ariyā parisuddhakāyakammantā’’tiādīsu padapūraṇai| ‘‘āraucayāmi vau, bhikkhavai’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 7.72) sampadānatthai| idhāpi sampadānatthai aivāti daṭṭhabbau|
