upatthaddhanti aulubbhārammaṇabhūtāhi viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitīhi upatthambhitaṃ| tañ‍ca kammanti yaṃ ‘‘caitanā caitasika’’nti pubbai (naitti॰ 82) vuttaṃ| imai ca kilaisāti imai ca dasavatthukā kilaisā| saisaṃ suviñ‍ñaiyyamaiva|

85. idāni  āhārādayau nayānaṃ saṃkilaisapakkhai disābhāvaina vavatthapaituṃ ‘‘imā catassau disā’’tiādi āraddhaṃ, taṃ uttānamaiva| puna kabaḷīkārau āhārautiādi āhārādīsuyaiva yassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā, taṃ vibhajitvā dassaituṃ āraddhaṃ| tattha dasan‍naṃ suttānanti aikadaisaisu samudāyavauhāraina vuttaṃ| samudāyaisu hi pavattā samañ‍ñā avayavaisupi dissati, ‘‘yathā paṭau daḍḍhau, samuddau diṭṭhau’’ti ca| aikau atthauti aikassa atthassa nipphādanatau vuttaṃ| byañ‍janamaiva nānanti aittha byañ‍janaggahaṇaina byañ‍janatthaupi gahitauti daṭṭhabbaṃ| dasahipi suttapadaihi savatthukā taṇhā vuttā| taṇhā ca rāgacaritaṃ puggalaṃ khippaṃ dūsaitīti āha – ‘‘imai rāgacaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā’’ti| yathā ca paṭhamadisābhāvaina vuttadhammā rāgacaritassa upak‍kilaisā, aivaṃ dutiyadisābhāvaina vuttadhammā dausacaritassa| tatiyacatutthadisābhāvaina vuttadhammā yathāk‍kamaṃ diṭṭhicaritassa mandassa tikkhassa ca upak‍kilaisā vuttā| taisaṃ upak‍kilaisabhāvau vuttanayānusāraina vaiditabbau|

āhāravipal‍lāsādayau yadipi sabbaihi tīhi vimaukkhamukhaihi pubbabhāgai yathārahaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyā pahātabbā ca| yassa pana dukkhānupassanā purimai āhāradvayai dukkhākāraina bahulaṃ pavattati, tassa vasaina yau ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau, yau ca phassau āhārau, imai appaṇihitaina vimaukkhamukhaina pariñ‍ñaṃ gacchantīti vuttaṃ| aisa nayau saisaisu| aivañ‍caitaṃ, na añ‍ñathā| na hi ariyamaggānaṃ viya pahātabbaisu vimaukkhamukhānaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyapahātabbaisu kauci niyamau sambhavati| iti sabbai laukavaṭṭānusārinau dhammā niyyanti, tai laukā tīhi vimaukkhamukhaihīti nigamanaṃ| tassatthau – iti aivaṃ vuttappakārā sabbai āhārādayau laukasaṅkhātavaṭṭānusārinau dhammā tai laukabhūtā vaṭṭatau niyyanti anic‍cānupassanādīhi tīhi vimaukkhamukhaihīti|

86. aivaṃ saṃkilaisapakkhai disābhūtadhammai niddisitvā idāni vaudānapakkhai disābhūtadhammai dassaituṃ ‘‘catassau paṭipadā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha dibbabrahmaariyaānaiñ‍javihārauti cattārau vihārā| mānappahānaālayasamugghātaavij‍jāpahānabhavūpasamā cattārau acchariyā abbhutā dhammā| sac‍cādhiṭṭhānādīni cattāri adhiṭṭhānāni| chandasamādhibhāvanādayau catassau samādhibhāvanā| indriyasaṃvarau tapasaṅkhātau puñ‍ñadhammau  baujjhaṅgabhāvanā sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggasaṅkhātaṃ nibbānañ‍cāti cattārau sukhabhāgiyā dhammā vaiditabbāti|

paṭhamā paṭipadātiādi paṭipadāsatipaṭṭhānādīnaṃ abhaidasandassanaṃ| yadi aivaṃ kasmā visuṃ gahaṇaṃ katanti? dasavatthukassa kilaisapuñ‍jassa paṭipakkhabhāvadassanatthaṃ paṭipadādidasakaniddaisau| tathā hi vakkhati – ‘‘cattārau āhārā taisaṃ paṭipakkhau catassau paṭipadā’’tiādi  (naitti॰ 87)| kiñ‍cāpi catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu ‘‘idaṃ nāma satipaṭṭhānaṃ imāya aiva paṭipadāya ijjhatī’’ti niyamau natthi, tathāpi paṭhamāya paṭipadāya paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ sambhavatīti sambhavavasaina aivaṃ vuttaṃ – ‘‘paṭhamā paṭipadā, paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhāna’’nti| yasmā pana āhāravipal‍lāsādīnaṃ viya paṭipadāsatipaṭṭhānādīnaṃ atthatau nānattaṃ natthi| satipaṭṭhānāniyaiva hi tathā tathā paṭipaj‍jamānāni dukkhāpaṭipadādandhābhiñ‍ñādināmakāni haunti, tasmā yathā saṃkilaisapakkhai ‘‘paṭhamai āhārai paṭhamau vipal‍lāsau’’tiādinā adhikaraṇabhaidaina vuttaṃ, aivaṃ adhikaraṇabhaidaṃ akatvā ‘‘paṭhamā paṭipadā, paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhāna’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| saisaisupi aisaiva nayau|

andhassa pabbatārauhanaṃ viya kadācidaiva uppaj‍janakaṃ acchariyaṃ, accharāyauggaṃ acchariyanti paurāṇā| abhūtapubbaṃ bhūtanti abbhutaṃ| ubhayampaitaṃ vimhayāvahassa adhivacanaṃ| na hi mānappahānāditau añ‍ñaṃ durabhisambhavataraṃ vimhanīyañ‍ca upalabbhatīti adhitiṭṭhati aitaina, aittha vā adhiṭṭhānamattamaiva vā tanti adhiṭṭhānaṃ| sac‍cañ‍ca taṃ adhiṭṭhānañ‍ca, sac‍cassa vā adhiṭṭhānaṃ, sac‍caṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ aitassāti vā sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ| saisaisupi aisaiva nayau| samādhi aiva bhāvaitabbatāya samādhibhāvanā| sukhaṃ bhajatīti sukhabhāgiyau, sukhabhāgassa vā sukhakauṭṭhāsassa hitauti sukhabhāgiyau| aikassapi sattassa asubhabhāvanādayau viya aikadaisai avattitvā anavasaisapariyādānatau natthi aitissā pamāṇanti appamañ‍ñā|

paṭhamā paṭipadā bhāvitā bahulīkatā paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ paripūraitīti paṭhamāya paṭipadāya bhāvanābahulīkārau paṭhamassa satipaṭṭhānassa bhāvanāpāripūrīti atthau| saisapadaisupi aisaiva nayau| yathā hi ariyamaggai bhāvitai satipaṭṭhānādayau baudhipakkhiyadhammā sabbaipi bhāvitā aiva haunti, aivaṃsampadamidaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ|

kāyānupassanāya  kāmarāgassa ujuvipac‍canīkabhāvatau ‘‘paṭhamau satipaṭṭhānau bhāvitau bahulīkatau kāmapaṭipakkhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paripūraitī’’ti vuttaṃ| tathā pītipaṭisaṃvaidanādivasaina pavattamānaṃ dutiyaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ, sappītikassa dutiyajjhānassa cittassa abhippamaudanavasaina pavattamānaṃ tatiyaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ uk‍kaṃsagatasukhassa tatiyajjhānassa anic‍cavirāgādivasaina pavattiyā saṅkhāraisu upaikkhakaṃ catutthaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhibhāvatau catutthajjhānassa pāripūriyā saṃvattati|

yasmā  pana rūpāvacarapaṭhamajjhānaṃ rūpāvacarasamāpattīnaṃ, dutiyajjhānaṃ byāpādavitak‍kādidūrībhāvaina brahmavihārānaṃ, tatiyajjhānaṃ pītivirāgaina sukhaina vipassanāya adhiṭṭhānabhūtaṃ ariyavihārānaṃ, catutthajjhānaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiānaiñ‍jappattaṃ ānaiñ‍javihārānaṃ visaisatau pac‍cayau hauti, tasmā ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ paṭhamaṃ vihāraṃ paripūraitī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| iti yau yassa visaisapac‍cayau, sau taṃ paripūraitīti vuttauti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

87. idāni paṭipadādayau vaudānapakkhai disābhāvaina vavatthapaituṃ ‘‘tattha imā catassau disā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| taṃ suviñ‍ñaiyyamaiva| puna ‘‘paṭhamā paṭipadā’’tiādi paṭipadācatuk‍kādīsu yaina yassa puggalassa vaudānaṃ, taṃ vibhajitvā dassaituṃ āraddhaṃ| taṃ haiṭṭhā vuttanayamaiva| yadipi tīsu vimaukkhamukhaisu ‘‘idaṃ nāma vimaukkhamukhaṃ imāya aiva paṭipadāya ijjhatī’’ti niyamau natthi| yaisaṃ pana puggalānaṃ purimāhi dvīhi paṭipadāhi appaṇihitaina vimaukkhamukhaina ariyamaggādhigamau| tathā yassa tatiyāya paṭipadāya suñ‍ñatavimaukkhamukhaina, yassa ca catutthāya paṭipadāya animittavimaukkhamukhaina ariyamaggādhigamau, taisaṃ puggalānaṃ vasaina ayaṃ paṭipadāvimaukkhamukhasaṃsandanā| satipaṭṭhānādīhi vimaukkhamukhasaṃsandanāyapi aisaiva nayau|

taisaṃ vik‍kīḷitanti taisaṃ asantāsanajavaparak‍kamādivisaisayaugaina sīhānaṃ buddhānaṃ pac‍caikabuddhānaṃ buddhasāvakānañ‍ca vik‍kīḷitaṃ viharaṇaṃ| yadidaṃ āhārādikilaisavatthusamatik‍kamanamukhaina saparasantānai paṭipadādisampādanā| idāni āhārādīnaṃ paṭipadādīhi yaina samatik‍kamanaṃ, taṃ naisaṃ paṭipakkhabhāvaṃ dassaintau ‘‘cattārau āhārā taisaṃ paṭipakkhau catassau  paṭipadā’’tiādimāha| tattha taisaṃ paṭipakkhabhāvau pahātabbabhāvau pahāyakabhāvau ca āhāraviñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitīnañ‍caittha pahātabbabhāvau tappaṭibandhachandarāgavasaina daṭṭhabbau| tattha ‘‘vik‍kīḷitaṃ bhāvanā sacchikiriyā cā’’tiādi tassāyaṃ saṅkhaipatthau – taisaṃ vik‍kīḷitanti aittha yadaitaṃ vik‍kīḷitaṃ nāma bhāvaitabbānaṃ baudhipakkhiyadhammānaṃ bhāvanā, sacchikātabbānaṃ phalanibbānānaṃ sacchikiriyā ca| tathā pahātabbassa dasavatthukassa kilaisapuñ‍jassa tadaṅgādivasaina pahānaṃ byantīkiriyā anavasaisananti| idāni taṃ saṅkhaipaina dassaintau ‘‘indriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ vik‍kīḷitaṃ vipariyāsānadhiṭṭhāna’’nti āha|

indriyādhiṭṭhānanti indriyānaṃ pavattanaṃ bhāvanā sacchikiriyā ca| vipariyāsānadhiṭṭhānanti vipal‍lāsānaṃ apavattanaṃ pahānaṃ anuppādanaṃ| indriyāni saddhammagaucarauti indriyāni caittha saddhammassa  gaucarabhūtāni pavattihaitūti adhippaitāni saddhindriyādīnīti atthau| vipariyāsā kilaisagaucarauti vipal‍lāsā saṃkilaisapakkhassa pavattiṭṭhānaṃ pavattihaitūti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati sīhavik‍kīḷitassa nayassa bhūmīti yāyaṃ ‘‘cattārau āhārā’’tiādinā saṃkilaisapakkhai dasan‍naṃ catuk‍kānaṃ, ‘‘catassau paṭipadā’’tiādinā vaudānapakkhaipi dasan‍naṃ catuk‍kānaṃ taṇhācaritādīnaṃ upak‍kilaisavaudānavibhāvanāmukhaina niddhāraṇā, ayaṃ sīhavik‍kīḷitassa nayassa bhūmi nāma|

88. idāni ugghaṭitañ‍ñuādipuggalattayavasaina tipukkhalanayassa bhūmiṃ vibhāvaitukāmau yasmā pana nayānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñānuppavaisassa icchitattā sīhavik‍kīḷitanayatau tipukkhalanayau niggacchati, tasmā paṭipadāvibhāgatau cattārau puggalai sīhavik‍kīḷitanayassa bhūmiṃ niddisitvā tatau aiva ugghaṭitañ‍ñuādipuggalattayai niddhāraituṃ ‘‘tattha yai dukkhāya paṭipadāyā’’tiādi āraddhaṃ| tattha imai dvai puggalāti imai purimānaṃ dvin‍naṃ paṭipadānaṃ vasaina dvai puggalā| aisa nayau itaratthāpi| puna ‘‘tattha yai dukkhāya paṭipadāyā’’tiādi yathāvuttapuggalacatuk‍katau ugghaṭitañ‍ñuādipuggalattayaṃ niddhāraituṃ vuttaṃ| tattha yau sādhāraṇāyāti dukkhāpaṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya, sukhāpaṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyātīti sambandhau| kathaṃ pana paṭipadādvayaṃ aikassa sambhavatīti? nayidamaivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| aikassa puggalassa aikasmiṃ dvai paṭipadā sambhavantīti| yathāvuttāsu pana dvīsu  paṭipadāsu yau yāya kāyaci niyyāti, ayaṃ vipañ‍citañ‍ñūti ayamaittha adhippāyau| yasmā pana aṭṭhasāliniyaṃ (dha॰ sa॰ aṭṭha॰ 350) paṭipadā calati na calatīti vicāraṇāyaṃ ‘‘calatī’’ti vuttaṃ, tasmā aikassapi puggalassa jhānantaramaggantaraisu paṭipadābhaidau icchitauvāti|

‘‘tattha bhagavā’’tiādinā daisanāvibhāgaihipi tamaiva puggalavibhāgaṃ vibhāvaiti| taṃ haiṭṭhā vuttanayamaiva| tattha adhicittanti adhicittasikkhañ‍cāti ca-saddau luttaniddiṭṭhau| taina adhicittasikkhañ‍ca adhipañ‍ñāsikkhañ‍ca vipañ‍citañ‍ñussa pañ‍ñapaitīti atthau| adhisīlanti aitthāpi aisaiva nayau| adhisīlasikkhaṃ adhicittasikkhaṃ adhipañ‍ñāsikkhañ‍cāti yaujaitabbaṃ|

cattāri hutvā tīṇi bhavantīti liṅgavipal‍lāsaina vuttaṃ, cattārau puggalā hutvā tayau puggalā hauntīti atthau|

ayaṃ  saṃkilaisauti ayaṃ akusalamūlādidvādasattikasaṅgahau saṃkilissati aitaināti saṃkilaisauti katvā| idaṃ vaudānanti aitthāpi aisaiva nayau|

tīṇi hutvā dvai bhavantīti nandiyāvaṭṭanayassa disābhūtadhammadassanatthaṃ vuttaṃ| tainaivāha – ‘‘taṇhā ca avij‍jā cā’’tiādi, taṃ sabbaṃ suviñ‍ñaiyyamaiva|

kasmā panaittha nayānaṃ uddaisānuk‍kamaina niddaisau na katauti? nayānaṃ nayaihi sambhavadassanatthaṃ| paṭhamanayatau hi puggalādhiṭṭhānavasaina tatiyanayassa, tatiyanayatau ca dutiyanayassa sambhavauti imassa visaisassa dassanatthaṃ paṭhamanayānantaraṃ tatiyanayau, tatiyanayānantarañ‍ca dutiyanayau niddiṭṭhau| dhammādhiṭṭhānavasaina pana tatiyanayatau dutiyanayau, dutiyanayatau paṭhamanayaupi sambhavatīti imassa visaisassa dassanatthaṃ antai ‘‘taṇhā ca avij‍jā cā’’tiādinā paṭhamanayassa bhūmi dassitā| tainaiva hi ‘‘cattāri hutvā tīṇi bhavanti, tīṇi hutvā dvai bhavantī’’ti vuttaṃ| yadi aivaṃ ‘‘dvai hutvā cattāri bhavanti, dvai hutvā tīṇi bhavanti, tīṇi hutvā cattāri bhavantī’’ti ayampi nayau vattabbau siyāti? sac‍camaitaṃ, ayaṃ pana nayau atthatau dassitau aivāti  katvā na vuttau| yasmā tiṇṇaṃ atthanayānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ anuppavaisau icchitau, sati ca anuppavaisai tatau viniggamaupi sambhavati aivāti| ayañ‍ca atthau paiṭakaupadaisaina vibhāvaitabbau|

tatthāyaṃ āditau paṭṭhāya vibhāvanā – cattārau puggalā taṇhācaritau duvidhau mudindriyau tikkhindriyau ca| tathā diṭṭhicaritauti| tattha taṇhācaritau mudindriyau dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya niyyāti, tikkhindriyau dukkhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya niyyāti, diṭṭhicaritau pana mudindriyau sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya niyyāti, tikkhindriyau sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya niyyāti| iti imāsu paṭipadāsu yathārahaṃ ṭhitaihi taṇhācaritadiṭṭhicaritaihi cattārau āhārā tappaṭibandhachandarāgappahānaina pahātabbā| cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bhāvaitvā cattārau vipal‍lāsā daṭṭhabbāti sabbau yathāvuttanayau anugantabbau|

tatthāyaṃ pāḷi – tattha yai diṭṭhicaritā sattā, tai kāmaisu dausadiṭṭhī, na ca yai kāmaisu anusayā samūhatā, tai attakilamathānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti| taisaṃ satthā vā dhammaṃ daisaiti añ‍ñatarau vā garuṭṭhāniyau sabrahmacārī ‘‘kāmaihi natthi atthau’’ti, tai ca pubbaiyaiva kāmaihi anatthikā, iti kāmai appakasiraina paṭinissaj‍janti, tai caitasikaina dukkhaina anajjhausitā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘sukhā paṭipadā’’ti| yai pana taṇhācaritā sattā, tai kāmaisu ajjhausitā, taisaṃ satthā vā dhammaṃ daisaiti añ‍ñatarau vā bhikkhu ‘‘kāmaihi natthi atthau’’ti, tai piyarūpaṃ dukkhaina paṭinissaj‍janti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘dukkhā paṭipadā’’ti| iti imai sabbai sattā dvīsu paṭipadāsu samausaraṇaṃ gacchanti dukkhāyañ‍ca sukhāyañ‍ca|

tattha yai diṭṭhicaritā sattā, tai dvidhā tikkhindriyā ca mudindriyā ca| tattha yai diṭṭhicaritā sattā tikkhindriyā, tai sukhaina paṭinissaj‍janti, khippañ‍ca abhisamainti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā’’ti| tattha yai diṭṭhicaritā sattā mudindriyā paṭhamaṃ tikkhindriyaṃ upādāya dandhataraṃ abhisamainti, tai sukhaina paṭinissaj‍janti, dandhañ‍ca abhisamainti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā’’ti| tattha taṇhācaritā sattā duvidhā tikkhindriyā ca mudindriyā  ca| tattha yai taṇhācaritā sattā tikkhindriyā, tai dukkhaina paṭinissaj‍janti, khippañ‍ca abhisamainti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā’’ti| tattha yai taṇhācaritā sattā mudindriyā paṭhamaṃ tikkhindriyaṃ upādāya dandhataraṃ abhisamainti, tai dukkhaina paṭinissaj‍janti, dandhañ‍ca abhisamainti| taina vuc‍cati – ‘‘dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā’’ti| imā catassau paṭipadāyau apañ‍camā achaṭṭhā| yai hi kaici nibbutā nibbāyanti nibbāyissanti vā imāhi catūhi paṭipadāhi anañ‍ñāhi, ayaṃ paṭipadā catuk‍kamaggaina kilaisai niddisati| yā catuk‍kamaggaina ariyadhammaisu niddisitabbā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati sīhavik‍kīḷitau nāma nayau|

tatrimai cattārau āhārā, cattārau vipal‍lāsā upādānā yaugā ganthā āsavā aughā sal‍lā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau agatigamanānīti aivaṃ imāni sabbāni dasa padāni| ayaṃ suttassa saṃsandanā|

cattārau āhārā, tattha yau ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau, yau ca phassau āhārau, imai taṇhācaritaina pahātabbā| tattha yau ca manausañ‍caitanāhārau, yau ca viñ‍ñāṇāhārau, imai diṭṭhicaritaina pahātabbā|

tattha paṭhamau āhārau paṭhamau vipal‍lāsau, dutiyau āhārau dutiyau vipal‍lāsau, tatiyau āhārau  tatiyau vipal‍lāsau, catutthau āhārau catutthau vipal‍lāsau, imai cattārau vipal‍lāsā apañ‍camā achaṭṭhā| idañ‍ca pamāṇā cattārau āhārā|

tattha paṭhamai vipal‍lāsai ṭhitau kāmai upādiyati, idaṃ kāmupādānaṃ| dutiyai vipal‍lāsai ṭhitau anāgataṃ bhavaṃ upādiyati, idaṃ sīlabbatupādānaṃ| tatiyai vipal‍lāsai ṭhitau viparītadiṭṭhiṃ upādiyati, idaṃ diṭṭhupādānaṃ| catutthai vipal‍lāsai ṭhitau khandhai attatau upādiyati, idaṃ attavādupādānaṃ|

tattha kāmupādānai ṭhitau kāmai abhijjhāya ganthati, ayaṃ abhijjhākāyaganthau| sīlabbatupādānai ṭhitau byāpādaṃ ganthati, ayaṃ byāpādakāyaganthau| diṭṭhupādānai ṭhitau parāmāsaṃ ganthati, ayaṃ parāmāsakāyaganthau| attavādupādānai ṭhitau papañ‍caintau ganthati, ayaṃ idaṃsac‍cābhinivaisakāyaganthau|

tassa  ganthaganthitā kilaisā āsavanti| kiṃ pana vuc‍cati āsavantīti? vippaṭisārā| yai vippaṭisārā, tai anusayā| tattha abhijjhākāyaganthaina kāmāsavau, byāpādakāyaganthaina bhavāsavau, parāmāsakāyaganthaina diṭṭhāsavau, idaṃsac‍cābhinivaisakāyaganthaina avij‍jāsavau|

tai cattārau āsavā vaipul‍laṃ gatā aughā haunti, taina vuc‍canti ‘‘aughā’’ti| tattha kāmāsavau kāmaughau, bhavāsavau bhavaughau, avij‍jāsavau avij‍jaughau, diṭṭhāsavau diṭṭhaughau|

tai cattārau aughā āsayamanupaviṭṭhā anusayasahagatā vuc‍canti sal‍lāti hadayamāhac‍ca tiṭṭhanti| tattha kāmaughau rāgasal‍laṃ, bhavaughau dausasal‍laṃ, avij‍jaughau mauhasal‍laṃ, diṭṭhaughau diṭṭhisal‍laṃ|

imaihi catūhi sal‍laihi pariyādin‍naṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ catūsu dhammaisu tiṭṭhati rūpai vaidanāya sañ‍ñāya saṅkhāraisu| imā catassau viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau| tattha rāgasal‍laina nandūpasaicanaṃ rūpūpagaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhati| dausasal‍laina vaidanūpagaṃ| mauhasal‍laina sañ‍ñūpagaṃ| diṭṭhisal‍laina nandūpasaicanaṃ saṅkhārūpagaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhati|

catūhi  viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitīhi catubbidhaṃ agatiṃ gacchanti chandā dausā bhayā mauhā| rāgaina chandā agatiṃ gacchati, dausaina dausā agatiṃ gacchati, mauhaina mauhā agatiṃ gacchati, diṭṭhiyā bhayā agatiṃ gacchati, iti imāni ca kammāni imai ca kilaisā ayaṃ saṃsārahaitu|

tatthimā catassau disā kabaḷīkārau āhārau ‘‘asubhai subha’’nti vipal‍lāsau kāmupādānaṃ kāmayaugau abhijjhākāyaganthau kāmāsavau kāmaughau rāgasal‍laṃ rūpūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti chandā agatigamanaṃ, ayaṃ paṭhamā disā|

phassau āhārau ‘‘dukkhai sukha’’nti vipal‍lāsau sīlabbatupādānaṃ bhavayaugau byāpādakāyaganthau bhavāsavau bhavaughau dausasal‍laṃ vaidanūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti dausā agatigamanaṃ, ayaṃ dutiyā disā|

manausañ‍caitanāhārau ‘‘anattani attā’’ti vipal‍lāsau diṭṭhupādānaṃ diṭṭhiyaugau parāmāsakāyaganthau diṭṭhāsavau diṭṭhaughau diṭṭhisal‍laṃ sañ‍ñūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti bhayā agatigamanaṃ, ayaṃ tatiyā disā|

viñ‍ñāṇāhārau  ‘‘anic‍cai nic‍ca’’nti vipal‍lāsau attavādupādānaṃ avij‍jāyaugau idaṃsac‍cābhinivaisaukāyaganthau avij‍jāsavau avij‍jaughau mauhasal‍laṃ saṅkhārūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti mauhā agatigamanaṃ, ayaṃ catutthī disā| iti imaisaṃ dasan‍naṃ suttānaṃ paṭhamaina padaina paṭhamāya disāya ālaukanaṃ, dutiyaina padaina dutiyāya disāya, tatiyaina padaina tatiyāya disāya, catutthaina padaina catutthiyā disāya ālaukanaṃ, ayaṃ vuc‍cati disā ālaukanā| iminā nayaina sabbai kilaisā catūsu padaisu pakkhipitabbā| ayaṃ akusalapakkhau|

catassau paṭipadā, cattāri jhānāni, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā, cattārau vihārā dibbau brahmā ariyau ānaiñ‍jau, cattārau sammappadhānā, cattārau acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, cattārau adhiṭṭhānā, cattārau samādhayau chandasamādhi vīriyasamādhi cittasamādhi vīmaṃsāsamādhi, cattārau dhammā sukhabhāgiyā nāñ‍ñatra baujjhaṅgā nāñ‍ñatra tapasā nāñ‍ñatra indriyasaṃvarā nāñ‍ñatra sabbanissaggā, cattāri appamāṇāni|

tattha  dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā bhāviyamānā bahulīkariyamānā paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paripūraiti, paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ paripūraiti, paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ paṭhamaṃ vihāraṃ paripūraiti, paṭhamau vihārau paripuṇṇau paṭhamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ paripūraiti, paṭhamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ paṭhamaṃ acchariyaṃ abbhutaṃ dhammaṃ paripūraiti, paṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau paripuṇṇau paṭhamaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ paripūraiti, paṭhamaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ chandasamādhiṃ paripūraiti, chandasamādhi paripuṇṇau indriyasaṃvaraṃ paripūraiti, indriyasaṃvarau paripuṇṇau paṭhamaṃ appamāṇaṃ paripūraiti| aivaṃ yāva sabbanissaggā catutthaṃ appamāṇaṃ paripūraiti|

tattha paṭhamā ca paṭipadā paṭhamañ‍ca jhānaṃ paṭhamañ‍ca satipaṭṭhānaṃ dibbau ca vihārau paṭhamañ‍ca sammappadhānaṃ paṭhamau ca acchariyau abbhutau dhammau sac‍cādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca chandasamādhi ca indriyasaṃvarau ca maittā ca appamāṇaṃ| ayaṃ paṭhamā disā|

dutiyā ca paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā dutiyañ‍ca jhānaṃ dutiyañ‍ca satipaṭṭhānaṃ brahmā ca vihārau dutiyañ‍ca sammappadhānaṃ dutiyau ca acchariyau abbhutau dhammau cāgādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca  cittasamādhi ca tapau ca karuṇā ca appamāṇaṃ| ayaṃ dutiyā disā|

tatiyā ca paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā tatiyañ‍ca jhānaṃ tatiyañ‍ca satipaṭṭhānaṃ ariyau ca vihārau tatiyañ‍ca sammappadhānaṃ tatiyau ca acchariyau abbhutau dhammau sac‍cādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca vīriyasamādhi ca baujjhaṅgā ca muditā ca appamāṇaṃ| ayaṃ tatiyā disā|

catutthī ca paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā catutthañ‍ca jhānaṃ catutthañ‍ca satipaṭṭhānaṃ ānaiñ‍jau ca vihārau catutthañ‍ca sammappadhānaṃ catutthau ca acchariyau abbhutau dhammau upasamādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca vīmaṃsāsamādhi ca sabbanissaggau ca upaikkhā ca appamāṇaṃ| ayaṃ catutthī disā| imāsaṃ catun‍naṃ disānaṃ yā ālaukanā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati disālaucanau nāma nayau|

tatthāyaṃ yaujanā – cattārau ca āhārā, catassau ca paṭipadā, cattārau ca vipal‍lāsā, cattārau ca satipaṭṭhānā, cattāri ca upādānāni, cattāri ca jhānāni, cattārau ca yaugā, cattārau ca vihārā, cattārau ca ganthā, cattārau ca sammappadhānā, cattārau ca āsavā, cattārau ca acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, cattārau ca aughā, cattāri ca adhiṭṭhānāni, cattāri ca  sal‍lāni, cattārau ca samādhayau, catassau ca viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau, cattārau ca sukhabhāgiyā dhammā, cattāri ca agatigamanāni cattāri ca appamāṇāni| iti kusalākusalānaṃ pakkhapaṭipakkhavasaina yaujanā| ayaṃ sīhavik‍kīḷitai disālaucanau nayau|

tassa cattāri sāmañ‍ñaphalāni pariyausānaṃ, tattha paṭhamāya disāya sautāpattiphalaṃ pariyausānaṃ, dutiyāya sakadāgāmiphalaṃ, tatiyāya anāgāmiphalaṃ, catutthiyā arahattaphalaṃ pariyausānanti|

tattha katamau tipukkhalanayau? paṭipadāvibhāgaina catūsu puggalaisu yau sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya niyyāti, ayaṃ ugghaṭitañ‍ñū| yau sukhāya vā paṭipadāya, dandhābhiñ‍ñāya, dukkhāya vā paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya niyyāti, ayaṃ vipañ‍citañ‍ñū| yau dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya niyyāti, ayaṃ naiyyau| iti cattārau hutvā tayau haunti| tattha ugghaṭitañ‍ñussa samathapubbaṅgamā vipassanā sappāyā| naiyyassa vipassanāpubbaṅgamau samathau, vipañ‍citañ‍ñussa samathavipassanā yuganaddhā| ugghaṭitañ‍ñussa mudukā daisanā, naiyyassa tikkhā daisanā, vipañ‍citañ‍ñussa tikkhamudukā daisanā|

ugghaṭitañ‍ñussa adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā, vipañ‍citañ‍ñussa adhicittasikkhā ca adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā ca, naiyyassa adhisīlasikkhā ca adhicittasikkhā ca adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā ca| iti imaisaṃ puggalānaṃ catūhi paṭipadāhi niyyānaṃ|

tatthāyaṃ saṃkilaisapakkhau, tīṇi akusalamūlāni, tayau phassā, tissau vaidanā, tayau upavicārā, tayau kilaisā, tayau vitak‍kā, tayau pariḷāhā, tīṇi saṅkhatalakkhaṇāni, tissau dukkhatā|

tīṇi akusalamūlānīti laubhau akusalamūlaṃ, dausau akusalamūlaṃ, mauhau akusalamūlaṃ| tayau phassāti sukhavaidanīyau phassau, dukkhavaidanīyau phassau, adukkhamasukhavaidanīyau phassau| tissau vaidanāti sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| tayau upavicārāti saumanassūpavicārau, daumanassūpavicārau, upaikkhūpavicārau| tayau kilaisāti laubhau, dausau, mauhau| tayau vitak‍kāti kāmavitak‍kau, byāpādavitak‍kau, vihiṃsāvitak‍kau| tayau pariḷāhāti  rāgajau, dausajau, mauhajau| tīṇi saṅkhatalakkhaṇānīti uppādau, ṭhiti, vayau| tissau dukkhatāti dukkhadukkhatā, vipariṇāmadukkhatā, saṅkhāradukkhatā|

tattha laubhau akusalamūlaṃ manāpikaina ārammaṇaina samuṭṭhahati| tadaiva manāpikārammaṇaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhavaidanīyau phassau, sukhavaidanīyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhā vaidanā, sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saumanassūpavicārau, saumanassūpavicāraṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati rāgau, rāgaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati kāmavitak‍kau, kāmavitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati rāgajau pariḷāhau, rāgajaṃ pariḷāhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati uppādau saṅkhatalakkhaṇaṃ, uppādaṃ saṅkhatalakkhaṇaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vipariṇāmadukkhatā|

dausau akusalamūlaṃ amanāpikaina ārammaṇaina samuṭṭhahati| tadaiva amanāpikārammaṇaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dukkhavaidanīyau phassau, dukkhavaidanīyaṃ  phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dukkhā vaidanā, dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati daumanassūpavicārau, daumanassūpavicāraṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dausau, dausaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati byāpādavitak‍kau, byāpādavitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dausajau pariḷāhau, dausajaṃ pariḷāhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ saṅkhatalakkhaṇaṃ, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ saṅkhatalakkhaṇaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dukkhadukkhatā|

mauhau akusalamūlaṃ upaikkhāṭhāniyaṃ ārammaṇaina samuṭṭhahati| tadaiva upaikkhāṭhāniyaṃ ārammaṇaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati adukkhamasukhavaidanīyau phassau, adukkhamasukhavaidanīyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati adukkhamasukhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati upaikkhūpavicārau, upaikkhūpavicāraṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati mauhau, mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vihiṃsāvitak‍kau, vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati mauhajau pariḷāhau, mauhajaṃ pariḷāhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vayau saṅkhatalakkhaṇaṃ, vayaṃ saṅkhatalakkhaṇaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṅkhāradukkhatā| iti ayaṃ tīhi ākāraihi kilaisānaṃ niddaisau| yau kauci akusalapakkhau, sabbau sau tīsu akusalamūlaisu samausaratīti|

tattha katamau kusalapakkhau? tīṇi kusalamūlāni alaubhau, adausau, amauhau| tissau pañ‍ñā sutamayī, cintāmayī, bhāvanāmayī| tayau samādhī savitak‍kasavicārau, avitak‍kavicāramattau, avitak‍kaavicārau| tissau sikkhā adhisīlasikkhā, adhicittasikkhā, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā| tīṇi nimittāni samathanimittaṃ, paggahanimittaṃ, upaikkhānimittaṃ| tayau vitak‍kā naikkhammavitak‍kau, abyāpādavitak‍kau, avihiṃsāvitak‍kau| tīṇi indriyāni anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ, añ‍ñindriyaṃ, añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ| tayau upavicārā naikkhammūpavicārau, abyāpādūpavicārau, avihiṃsūpavicārau tissau aisanā kāmaisanā, bhavaisanā, brahmacariyaisanā| tayau khandhā sīlakkhandhau, samādhikkhandhau, pañ‍ñākkhandhau|

tattha alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ sutamayipañ‍ñaṃ paripūraiti| sutamayi pañ‍ñā paripuṇṇā savitak‍kasavicāraṃ samādhiṃ paripūraiti, savitak‍kasavicārau samādhi paripuṇṇau adhisīlasikkhaṃ paripūraiti, adhisīlasikkhā paripuṇṇā  samathanimittaṃ paripūraiti, samathanimittaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ naikkhammavitak‍kaṃ paripūraiti, naikkhammavitak‍kau paripuṇṇau anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ paripūraiti, anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ naikkhammasitūpavicāraṃ paripūraiti, naikkhammūpavicāraṃ paripuṇṇau kāmaisanaṃ pajahati| kāmaisanappahānaṃ sīlakkhandhaṃ paripūraiti|

adausau kusalamūlaṃ cintāmayipañ‍ñaṃ paripūraiti, cintāmayipañ‍ñā paripuṇṇā avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ samādhiṃ paripūraiti, avitak‍kavicāramattau samādhi paripuṇṇau adhicittasikkhaṃ paripūraiti, adhicittasikkhā paripuṇṇā upaikkhānimittaṃ paripūraiti, upaikkhānimittaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ abyāpādavitak‍kaṃ paripūraiti, abyāpādavitak‍kau paripuṇṇau añ‍ñindriyaṃ paripūraiti, añ‍ñindriyaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ abyāpādūpavicāraṃ paripūraiti, abyāpādūpavicārau paripuṇṇau bhavaisanaṃ pajahati, bhavaisanappahānaṃ samādhikkhandhaṃ paripūraiti|

amauhau kusalamūlaṃ bhāvanāmayipañ‍ñaṃ paripūraiti, bhāvanāmayipañ‍ñā paripuṇṇā avitak‍kaavicāraṃ samādhiṃ paripūraiti, avitak‍kaavicārau samādhi paripuṇṇau adhipañ‍ñāsikkhaṃ paripūraiti, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā paripuṇṇā paggahanimittaṃ paripūraiti, paggahanimittaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ avihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ paripūraiti, avihiṃsāvitak‍kau paripuṇṇau añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ paripūraiti, añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ avihiṃsūpavicāraṃ paripūraiti, avihiṃsūpavicārau paripuṇṇau brahmacariyaisanaṃ paripūraiti, brahmacariyaisanā paripuṇṇā pañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ paripūraiti|

iti imai tayau dhammā akusalapakkhikā kusalapakkhikā ca tikaniddaisaihi niddiṭṭhā tipukkhalanayassa  disā nāma| tassa pariyausānaṃ tayau vimaukkhā appaṇihitau suñ‍ñatau animittau, ayaṃ tipukkhalau nāma dutiyau nayau|

tattha yai imai tayau puggalā ugghaṭitañ‍ñū vipañ‍citañ‍ñū naiyyauti imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ dvai puggalā sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya, sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyanti, dvaiyaiva puggalā dukkhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya, dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyanti, imai cattārau| tai visaisaina dvai haunti diṭṭhicaritau ca taṇhācaritau ca| imai  cattārau hutvā tayau haunti, tayau hutvā dvai haunti| imaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ ayaṃ saṃkilaisau – avij‍jā ca taṇhā ca ahirīkañ‍ca anauttappañ‍ca asati ca asampajañ‍ñañ‍ca nīvaraṇāni ca saṃyaujanāni ca ajjhausānañ‍ca abhinivaisau ca ahaṃkārau ca mamaṃkārau ca assaddhiyañ‍ca dauvacassatā ca kausaj‍jañ‍ca ayaunisaumanasikārau ca vicikicchā ca avij‍jā ca asaddhammassavanañ‍ca asamāpatti ca|

tattha avij‍jā ca ahirīkañ‍ca asati ca nīvaraṇāni ca ajjhausānañ‍ca ahaṃkārau ca assaddhiyañ‍ca kausaj‍jañ‍ca vicikicchā ca asaddhammassavanañ‍ca, ayaṃ aikā disā|

taṇhā ca anauttappañ‍ca asampajañ‍ñañ‍ca saṃyaujanāni ca abhinivaisau ca mamaṃkārau ca dauvacassatā ca ayaunisaumanasikārau ca avij‍jā ca asamāpatti ca, ayaṃ dutiyā disā| dasan‍naṃ dukānaṃ dasa padāni paṭhamā disāti kātabbāni| saṃkhittaina atthaṃ ñāpainti paṭipakkhai kaṇhapakkhassa dasan‍naṃ dukānaṃ dasa padāni dutiyakāni, ayaṃ dutiyā disā|

tattha katamau kusalapakkhau? samathau ca vipassanā ca vij‍jā ca caraṇañ‍ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca hirī ca auttappañ‍ca ahaṃkārappahānañ‍ca mamaṃkārappahānañ‍ca sammāvāyāmau ca yaunisaumanasikārau ca sammāsati ca sammāsamādhi ca pañ‍ñā ca nibbidā ca samāpatti ca saddhammassavanañ‍ca saumanassañ‍ca dhammānudhammapaṭipatti ca|

dasan‍naṃ dukānaṃ samathādīni saumanassapariyausānāni paṭhamāni dasa padāni paṭhamā disā, vipassanādīni dhammānudhammapaṭipattipariyausānāni dutiyāni dasa padāni dutiyā disā| iti akusalapakkhai kusalapakkhai ca nandiyāvaṭṭassa nayassa catassau disā|

tāsu  kusalapakkhai samathādīhi akusalapakkhai taṇhādayau pahānaṃ gacchanti, taisaṃ pahānā rāgavirāgā caitauvimutti, kusalapakkhai vipassanādīhi akusalapakkhai avij‍jādayau pahānaṃ gacchanti, taisaṃ pahānā avij‍jāvirāgā pañ‍ñāvimutti| iti imā dvai vimuttiyau nandiyāvaṭṭanayai pariyausānaṃ|

tattha  taṇhā avij‍jā samathau vipassanāti cattāri padāni, taisu aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni samausaranti| kathaṃ? samathau ca alaubhau ca adausau ca asubhasañ‍ñā ca dukkhasañ‍ñā cāti imāni pañ‍ca padāni samathaṃ bhajanti, vipassanā ca amauhau ca anic‍casañ‍ñā ca anattasañ‍ñā cāti imāni cattāri padāni vipassanaṃ bhajanti| aivaṃ nava padāni kusalāni dvīsu padaisu samausaranti| taṇhā ca laubhau ca dausau ca subhasañ‍ñā ca sukhasañ‍ñā cāti imāni pañ‍ca padāni taṇhaṃ bhajanti, avij‍jā ca mauhau ca nic‍casañ‍ñā ca attasañ‍ñā cāti imāni cattāri padāni avij‍jaṃ bhajanti| aivaṃ nava padāni akusalāni dvīsu padaisu samausaranti| iti tipukkhalau ca sīhavik‍kīḷitau ca nandiyāvaṭṭanayaṃ anuppavisanti|

kathaṃ tipukkhalai nayai itarai dvai nayā anuppavisanti? vipassanā ca amauhau ca anic‍casañ‍ñā ca anattasañ‍ñā cāti imāni cattāri padāni amauhau, samathau ca alaubhau ca asubhasañ‍ñā ca dukkhasañ‍ñā cāti imāni cattāri padāni alaubhau, adausau adausau aiva| aivaṃ nava padāni kusalāni tīsu padaisu samausaranti| taṇhā ca laubhau ca subhasañ‍ñā ca sukhasañ‍ñā cāti imāni cattāri padāni laubhau, avij‍jā ca mauhau ca nic‍casañ‍ñā ca attasañ‍ñā cāti imāni cattāri padāni mauhau, dausau dausau aiva| aivaṃ nava padāni akusalāni tīsu padaisu samausaranti| iti tipukkhalai nayai itarai dvai nayā anuppavisanti|

kathaṃ catūsu padaisu aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni samausaranti? taṇhā ca subhasañ‍ñā ca, ayaṃ paṭhamau vipal‍lāsau| laubhau ca sukhasañ‍ñā ca, ayaṃ dutiyau vipal‍lāsau| avij‍jā ca nic‍casañ‍ñā ca, ayaṃ tatiyau vipal‍lāsau| mauhau ca attasañ‍ñā ca, ayaṃ catutthau vipal‍lāsau| iti nava padāni akusalāni catūsu padaisu samausaranti| samathau ca asubhasañ‍ñā ca paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ, alaubhau ca dukkhasañ‍ñā ca dutiyaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ, vipassanā ca anic‍casañ‍ñā ca tatiyaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ, amauhau ca anattasañ‍ñā ca catutthaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ| iti nava padāni kusalāni catūsu padaisu samausaranti| aivaṃ sīhavik‍kīḷitanayai  itarai dvai nayā anuppavisanti| tiṇṇañhi nayānaṃ yā bhūmiyau gaucarau, sau aikaikaṃ nayaṃ  anuppavisati| tasmā aikaikassa nayassa akusalai vā dhammai viñ‍ñātai kusalai vā paṭipakkhau anvaisitabbau| paṭipakkhaṃ anvaisitvā sau nayau niddisitabbau| tamhi nayai niddiṭṭhai yathā aikamhi nayai itaraisaṃ nayānaṃ mūlapadāni anuppaviṭṭhāni, tatau tatau nīharitvā niddisitabbāni| aikaikasmiñhi nayai aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni anuppaviṭṭhāni|

tattha aikaikasmiṃ dhammai viñ‍ñātai sabbai dhammā viñ‍ñātā haunti| imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ nayānaṃ sīhavik‍kīḷitassa nayassa cattāri phalāni pariyausānaṃ paṭhamāya disāya paṭhamaṃ phalaṃ, dutiyāya disāya dutiyaṃ phalaṃ, tatiyāya disāya tatiyaṃ phalaṃ, catutthāya disāya catutthaṃ phalaṃ pariyausānaṃ|

tipukkhalassa nayassa tayau vimaukkhā pariyausānaṃ paṭhamāya disāya appaṇihitau, dutiyāya suñ‍ñatau, tatiyāya animittau vimaukkhau pariyausānaṃ|

nandiyāvaṭṭassa nayassa dvai vimuttiyau pariyausānaṃ paṭhamāya disāya taṇhāvirāgā caitauvimutti, dutiyāya disāya avij‍jāvirāgā pañ‍ñāvimutti pariyausānaṃ| imaisu tīsu nayaisu yā aṭṭhārasan‍naṃ padānaṃ ālaucanā, ayaṃ disālaucanau nayau| yā ālaukaitvā kusalapakkhai akusalapakkhai ca ‘‘ayaṃ dhammau imaṃ dhammaṃ bhajatī’’ti jānantaina sammā yaujanā, ayaṃ aṅkusau nayauti imai pañ‍ca nayā|

nayasamuṭṭhānavāravaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

sāsanapaṭṭhānavāravaṇṇanā

89. aivaṃ sabbathā nayasamuṭṭhānaṃ vibhajitvā idāni sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ vibhajantau yasmā saṅgahavārādīsu mūlapadaihaiva paṭṭhānaṃ saṅgahaitvā sarūpatau na dassitaṃ, tasmā yathā mūlapadaihi paṭṭhānaṃ niddhāraitabbaṃ, aivaṃ paṭṭhānataupi mūlapadāni niddhāraitabbānīti dassanatthaṃ ‘‘aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā kuhiṃ daṭṭhabbā? sāsanapaṭṭhānai’’ti āha| mūlapadasāsanapaṭṭhānānañhi añ‍ñamañ‍ñasaṅgahau pubbai dassitau aivāti| atha sāsanapaṭṭhānanti kau vacanatthau? sāsanassa paṭṭhānanti sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ, sāsanaṃ  daisanā, tassā vainaiyyajjhāsayānurūpaṃ taisaṃ hitasukhanipphādanatthaṃ pakāraihi ṭhānaṃ pavatti sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ| idha pana tassa tathābhāvadīpanaṃ ‘‘sāsanapaṭṭhāna’’nti vaiditabbaṃ| atha vā sāsanaṃ adhisīlasikkhādayau| taisaṃ pavattanupāyabhāvatau patiṭṭhahanti aitaihīti paṭṭhānāni, saṃkilaisādidhammā| taisaṃ pavaidanatau tadupacāraina suttāni paṭṭhānāni| taisaṃ pana samūhabhāvatau ayaṃ pakaraṇappadaisau paṭṭhānaṃ nāma|

aparau nayau – kainaṭṭhaina paṭṭhānaṃ? paṭṭhitaṭṭhaina gamanaṭṭhaināti atthau| ‘‘yai tai gauṭṭhā paṭṭhitagāvau’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.156) āgataṭṭhānasmiñhi yaina paṭṭhānaina tai ‘‘gauṭṭhā paṭṭhitagāvau’’ti vuttā, taṃ atthatau gamanaṃ hauti| iti nātivitthāritanayaisu hāranayaisu anissaṅgagamanassa daisanāñāṇassa saṃkilaisabhāgiyādilaukiyādibhaidaisu tadubhayavaumissakabhaidaisu ca vitthāritanayalābhatau nissaṅgavasaina pavattagamanattā tai saṃkilaisabhāgiyādayau laukiyādayau ca visuṃ visuṃ vaumissā ca adhikaraṇavasaina paṭṭhānaṃ nāma| taisaṃ pakāsanatau ayaṃ pakaraṇappadaisau paṭṭhānanti vaiditabbaṃ|

‘‘saṃkilaisabhāgiya’’ntiādīsu saṃkilissati aitaināti saṃkilaisau| saṃkilaisabhāgai saṃkilaisakauṭṭhāsai pavattaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ| vāsanā puñ‍ñabhāvanā, vāsanābhāgai pavattaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ, vāsanaṃ bhajāpaitīti vā vāsanābhāgiyaṃ| nibbijjhanaṃ laubhakkhandhādīnaṃ padālanaṃ nibbaidhau| nibbaidhabhāgai pavattaṃ, nibbaidhaṃ bhajāpaitīti vā nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ| pariniṭṭhitasikkhādhammā asaikkhā, asaikkhabhāvai pavattaṃ, asaikkhai bhajāpaitīti vā asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ| taisu yattha taṇhādisaṃkilaisau vibhattau, idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ| yattha dānādipuñ‍ñakiriyavatthu vibhattaṃ, idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ| yattha saikkhā sīlakkhandhādayau vibhattā, idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ| yattha pana asaikkhā sīlakkhandhādayau vibhattā, idaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ| itarāni taisaṃ vaumissakanayavasaina vuttāni|

tāni pana cha dukā cattārau tikā aikaṃ catuk‍kaṃ aparampi aikaṃ catuk‍kanti dvādasa haunti| taisu cattārau dukā dvai ca tikā uddhaṭā, itarai  na uddhaṭā, anuddharaṇai kāraṇaṃ natthi| iminā nayaina taipi gahaituṃ sak‍kāti pāḷiyaṃ saṃkhittāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| tathā hi vakkhati – ‘‘imāni cattāri suttāni, sādhāraṇāni katāni aṭṭha bhavantī’’tiādi| tattha yasmā katthaci suttai taṇhāsaṃkilaisauva niddisīyati, katthaci diṭṭhisaṃkilaisauva, katthaci duc‍caritasaṃkilaisauva  niddisīyati, tasmā saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ tidhā vibhajitvā uddiṭṭhaṃ ‘‘taṇhāsaṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ sutta’’ntiādinā| tathā vaudānaṃ nāma saṃkilaisai sati hautīti vaudānabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ saṃkilaisavibhāgaina tidhāva uddiṭṭhaṃ ‘‘taṇhāvaudānabhāgiyaṃ sutta’’ntiādinā| taṃ pana atthatau vāsanābhāgiyādi aiva hauti| ayañ‍ca nayau kaisuci pautthakaisu natthi|

‘‘tattha saṃkilaisau tividhau’’tiādi saṃkilaisapaṭipakkhatau samathādiniddhāraṇavasaina vāsanābhāgiyādisuttānaṃ visayadassanatthaṃ āraddhaṃ| tattha yadi āsatti uppaj‍jati bhavaisūti bhavaisu chandarāgaṃ pajahituṃ asak‍kauntassa yadi bhavapatthanā uppaj‍jati| aivaṃ sāyanti aivamassa puggalassa ayaṃ samathavipassanābhāvanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu bhavati puj‍jabhavaphalanibbattanatau| tatrūpapattiyā saṃvattatīti tatra tatra bhavai upapattiyā saṃvattati| imāni cattāri suttānīti imāni saṃkilaisabhāgiyādīni cattāri suttāni| sādhāraṇāni katānīti saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca, saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca, saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca, vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍cāti aivaṃ padantarasaṃyaujanavasaina missitāni katāni| aṭṭha bhavantīti purimāni cattāri imāni cattārīti aivaṃ aṭṭha bhavanti|

tāniyaiva aṭṭha suttāni sādhāraṇāni katāni sauḷasa bhavantīti tāniyaiva yathāvuttāni aṭṭha suttāni vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca, asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca, nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca, asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca, asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca, asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca, nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca naivasaṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca, navāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nanibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca na  asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍cāti aivaṃ sādhāraṇāni katāni purimāni aṭṭha imāni aṭṭhāti sauḷasa bhavanti| taisu cattārau aikakā, cha dukā, cattārau tikā, aikau catuk‍kau, aparaupi aikau catuk‍kauti ayampi vibhāgau vaiditabbau| tatthāpi dvai dukā, dvai tikā, dvai catuk‍kā ca pāḷiyaṃ anāgatāti vaiditabbā|

idāni imassa paṭṭhānassa sakalasāsanasaṅgahitabhāvaṃ vibhāvaituṃ ‘‘imaihi sauḷasahi suttaihi bhin‍naihi navavidhaṃ suttaṃ bhin‍naṃ bhavatī’’ti vuttaṃ| tassatthau – imaihi saṃkilaisabhāgiyādīhi sauḷasahi suttaihi paṭṭhānanayaina vibhattaihi suttagaiyyādinavavidhaṃ pariyattisāsanasaṅkhātaṃ suttaṃ bhin‍naṃ sauḷasadhā  vibhattaṃ hauti| iminā sauḷasavidhaina paṭṭhānaina asaṅgahitau pariyattisāsanassa padaisau natthīti adhippāyau| kathaṃ pana saṃkilaisabhāgiyādibhāvau gahaitabbauti? āha ‘‘gāthāya gāthā anuminitabbā’’tiādi| tattha gāthāya gāthā anuminitabbāti ayaṃ gāthā viya gāthā saṃkilaisabhāgiyāti vā vāsanābhāgiyāti vā nibbaidhabhāgiyāti vā asaikkhabhāgiyāti vā anuminitabbā, anu anu minitvā tak‍kaitvā jānitabbāti atthau| saisapadaisupi aisaiva nayau| aittha ca gāthāvaiyyākaraṇavinimuttā sabbā pariyatti ‘‘suttainā’’tipadaina saṅgahitāti daṭṭhabbā|

90. idāni saṃkilaisabhāgiyādīni suttāni yathāniddiṭṭhāni udāharaṇavasaina vibhāvaituṃ ‘‘tattha katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ sutta’’ntiādi āraddhaṃ| tattha ‘‘kāmandhā jālasañchan‍nā’’ti gāthāya atthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| yathā imassa, aivaṃ itau parānampi haiṭṭhā vuttatthānaṃ uttānapadānañ‍ca atthaṃ na vaṇṇayissāma|

agatigamanānīti kāyādīhi ayuttagamanāni, akattabbakaraṇānīti atthau| chandāti chandahaitu icchāpac‍cayā| agatiṃ gacchatīti agantabbaṃ gatiṃ gacchati, akattabbaṃ karautīti atthau| dhammanti sādhūnaṃ ariyānaṃ dhammaṃ| ativattatīti atimadditvā vītik‍kamati| nihīyatīti hāyati| yasauti kitti ca parivārau ca|

‘‘manaupubbaṅgamā dhammā’’ti gāthāyaṃ manauti yadipi kāmāvacarakusalādibhaidaṃ sabbampi catubhūmakacittaṃ manau, imasmiṃ pana ṭhānai cakkhupālatthairassa  (dha॰ pa॰ 1-2; thairagā॰ 95) purimajātiyaṃ vaij‍jabhūtassa uppan‍navasaina niyamiyamānaṃ paṭighasampayuttacittamaiva labbhati| sau manau pubbaṅgamau aitaisanti manaupubbaṅgamā, manasā paṭhamagāminā saman‍nāgatāti atthau| dhammāti nissattanij‍jīvaṭṭhaina dhammā, tai pana vaidanādayau tayau arūpinau khandhā| aitai hi manaupubbaṅgamā| kathaṃ panaitaihi saddhiṃ aikasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ aikasmiñ‍ca ārammaṇai aikakkhaṇai uppaj‍jamānau manau pubbaṅgamau nāma hautīti? uppādapac‍cayaṭṭhaina| yathā hi bahūsu aikatau gāmaghātādikammāni karauntaisu ‘‘kau aitaisaṃ pubbaṅgamau’’ti vuttai yau taisaṃ pac‍cayau hauti, yaṃ yaṃ nissāya tai taṃ kammaṃ karaunti, sau dattau vā mittau vā taisaṃ pubbaṅgamauti vuc‍cati, aivaṃsampadamidaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| iti uppādapac‍cayaṭṭhaina manau pubbaṅgamau aitaisanti manaupubbaṅgamā| na hi  tai manai anuppaj‍jantai uppaj‍jituṃ sak‍kuṇanti, manau pana aikac‍caisu caitasikaisu anuppaj‍jantaisupi uppaj‍jati aiva|

adhipativasaina manau saiṭṭhau aitaisanti manausaiṭṭhā| yathā hi caurādīnaṃ caurajaiṭṭhakādayau adhipatinau saiṭṭhā, tathā taisampi manau saiṭṭhau| yathā pana dāruādīhi nipphan‍nāni bhaṇḍāni dārumayādīni nāma haunti, tathā aitaipi manatau nipphan‍nattā manaumayā nāma| paduṭṭhaināti abhijjhādīhi dausaihi paduṭṭhaina dūsitaina bhāsati vā karauti vā| sau hi bhāsantau catubbidhaṃ vacīduc‍caritamaiva bhāsati, karauntaupi tividhaṃ kāyaduc‍caritamaiva karauti, abhāsantau akarauntau taihi abhijjhādīhi paduṭṭhamanatāya tividhaṃ manauduc‍caritaṃ pūraiti| aivamassa dasa akusalakammapathā pāripūriṃ gacchanti| tatau naṃ dukkhamanvaitīti tatau tividhaduc‍caritatau taṃ puggalaṃ dukkhaṃ anvaiti duc‍caritānubhāvaina catūsu apāyaisu dukkhaṃ anugacchati| yathā kiṃ? cak‍kaṃva vahatau padanti, yathā nāma sakaṭaṃ vahatau balībaddassa padaṃ paharantaṃ cak‍kaṃ anugacchati, aivaṃ naṃ puggalaṃ dukkhamanugacchatīti|

‘‘middhī yadā hautī’’ti gāthāyaṃ middhīti thinamiddhābhibhūtau| mahagghasauti mahābhaujanau āharahatthakaalaṃsāṭakatatravaṭṭakakākamāsakabhuttavamitakānaṃ añ‍ñatarau viya| niddāyitāti supanasīlau| samparivattasāyīti saiyyasukhapassasukhānaṃ anuyuñ‍janavasaina samparivattakasayanasīlau| nivāpapuṭṭhauti kuṇḍakādinā sūkarabhattaina puṭṭhau| gharasūkarau hi bālakālatau paṭṭhāya pausiyamānau thūlasarīrakālai gaihatau bahi nikkhamituṃ alabhantau haiṭṭhāmañ‍cādīsu samparivattitvā samparivattitvā assasantau passasantau sayataiva| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yadā purisau middhī ca hauti mahagghasau ca, nivāpapuṭṭhau mahāvarāhau viya añ‍ñaina iriyāpathaina yāpaituṃ asak‍kauntau niddāsīlau saṃparivattasāyī, tadā sau ‘‘anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ anattā’’ti tīṇi lakkhaṇāni manasi kātuṃ na sak‍kauti| taisaṃ amanasikārā mandapañ‍ñau punappunaṃ gabbhaṃ upaiti, gabbhavāsatau na parimuc‍catīti|

‘‘ayasāva mala’’nti gāthāyaṃ ayasāti ayatau| samuṭṭhitanti jātaṃ| tatuṭṭhāyāti tatau uṭṭhahitvā| atidhaunacārinanti dhaunā vuc‍cati cattārau pac‍cayai idamatthitāya alamaitaināti pac‍cavaikkhitvā paribhuñ‍janapañ‍ñā, taṃ atik‍kamitvā carantau atidhaunacārī nāma| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yathā ayatau malaṃ samuṭṭhāya yatau taṃ samuṭṭhitaṃ, tamaiva khādati vināsaiti, aivamaivaṃ cattārau  pac‍cayai appac‍cavaikkhitvā paribhuñ‍jantaṃ atidhaunacārinaṃ sāni kammāni attanau santānai uṭṭhitattā attanau santakānaiva tāni kammāni duggatiṃ nayantīti|

‘‘caurau yathā’’ti gāthāyaṃ caurau yathā sandhimukhai gahītauti yathā caurau gharasandhiṃ chinditvā gaihaṃ pavisantau gharasandhimukhai aiva rājapurisaihi gahitau| sakammunā hañ‍ñati bajjhatai cāti taina attanā katakammaina kasābhitāḷanādinā hañ‍ñati caiva addubandhanādinā bajjhati ca| aivaṃ ayaṃ paic‍ca pajā paratthāti aivampi ayaṃ pāpakārinī pajā itau cavitvā paralaukai| sakammunā hañ‍ñati bajjhatai cāti attanāva kataina pāpakammaina nirayādīsu nānappakāraihi kammakāraṇādīhi hañ‍ñati caiva paribajjhati cāti|

‘‘sukhakāmānī’’ti gāthāyaṃ yau daṇḍaina vihiṃsatīti yau puggalau daṇḍaina vā laiḍḍuādīhi vā vibādhati| paic‍ca sau na labhai sukhanti sau puggalau paralaukai manussasukhaṃ vā dibbasukhaṃ vā na labhati, nibbānasukhai pana vattabbamaiva natthi|

gun‍naṃ  cai taramānānanti gāvīsu mahaughaṃ tarantīsu| jimhaṃ gacchati puṅgavauti yadi yūthapati usabhau kuṭilaṃ gacchati| sabbā tā jimhaṃ gacchantīti sabbāpi tā gāviyau kuṭilamaiva gacchanti| kasmā? naittai jimhaṃ gatai satīti naittari kuṭilaṃ gatai sati, naittassa kuṭilaṃ gatattāti atthau| sau hi tāsaṃ pac‍cayikau upaddavaharau ca|

‘‘aivamaiva’’nti gāthāyaṃ yathā caitaṃ, aivamaivaṃ yau manussaisu padhānasammatau, yadi sau adhammacārī siyā| yai tassa anujīvinau, sabbaipi adhammikāva haunti| sāmisampadā hi pakatisampadaṃ sampādaiti| yasmā ca aitadaiva, tasmā sabbaṃ raṭṭhaṃ dukkhaṃ saiti, rājā cai hauti adhammikau| sukiccharūpā vatāti suṭṭhu kicchāpan‍narūpā vata| upadhīsūti kāmaguṇūpadhīsu| rattāti rāgābhibhūtā| kaṭukanti dukkhaṃ|

kuk‍kuc‍cajanakainaiva pattavaṭṭippabhavassa upacchin‍nattā phaluppatti kadaliyā parābhavāya hautīti āha – ‘‘phalaṃ vai kadaliṃ hantī’’ti| tathā phalapariyausānattā ausadhīnaṃ ‘‘phalaṃ vaiḷuṃ phalaṃ naḷa’’nti vuttaṃ| vaḷavāya kucchismiṃ gadrabhassa jātā assatarī nāma, sā gabbhaṃ gaṇhitvā kālai sampattai vijāyituṃ na sak‍kauti| pādaihi bhūmiṃ paharantī tiṭṭhati, athassa cattārau  pādai catūsu khāṇukaisu bandhitvā kucchiṃ phālaitvā pautakaṃ nīharanti, sā tatthaiva marati| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘gabbhau assatariṃ yathā’’ti| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yathā attanau phalaṃ kadalivaiḷunaḷaipi vināsaiti, gabbhau ca assatariṃ, aivaṃ attanau kammaphalabhūtau sak‍kārau asappurisaṃ vināsaitīti|

kaudhamakkhagarūti kujjhanalakkhaṇaṃ kaudhaṃ, paraguṇamakkhanalakkhaṇaṃ makkhañ‍ca garuṃ katvā uddhaṃ katvā ukkhipitvā carantau| sukhaittaiti sukhaittaipi| pūtibījaṃvāti pūtibhāvaṃ gataṃ bījaṃ viya| chakaṇarasādiparibhāvanasukkhāpanasukhasayādīni akaraṇaina bījadausaduṭṭhanti atthau|

91. caitasāti attanau cittaina| caitauti tassa puggalassa cittaṃ| paric‍cāti paricchinditvā| iriyatīti pavattati| yathābhatanti yathā kiñ‍ci āharitvā ṭhapitaṃ|

mākatthāti mā akattha| na pamutyatthīti pamaukkhau natthi| upaic‍cāpīti sañ‍cic‍cāpi, buddhipubbaināpīti atthau|

‘‘adhammainā’’ti  vatvāpi ‘‘musāvādainā’’ti vacanaṃ musāvādassa mahāsāvaj‍jabhāvadassanatthaṃ| tainaivāha – ‘‘aikaṃ dhammaṃ atītassā’’tiādi (dha॰ pa॰ 176), tathā ‘‘aivaṃ parittaṃ khau, rāhula, taisaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ, yaisaṃ natthi sampajānamusāvādai laj‍jā’’tiādi (ma॰ ni॰ 2.108)| taṃ kathaṃ nu bhavissatīti taṃ dhanaṃ kaina nu pakāraina taisaṃ bhavissati| adhammaina taisaṃ sambhatattā taisu naciraṭṭhitikaṃ hautīti atthau| antarāyā su bhavissantīti adhammiyavauhārāditau rājantarāyādayau bhavissanti| sūti nipātamattaṃ| sambhatassa vinassatīti imassa sambhataṃ saj‍jitaṃ vinassati| sagganti sugatiṃ| sā hi rūpādīhi saubhanaihi aggauti saggauti adhippaitā| aittāvatāti diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikānaṃ atthānaṃ hāniyā| hatāti vinaṭṭhā|

vivaṭṭataiti nivaṭṭati| laubhā khaṇati attānanti laubhahaitu apuñ‍ñāni karauntau kāyavisamādiyaugaina attānaṃ khaṇati nāma| mittaihi jīratīti mittabhāvaihi hāyati|

carantīti catūhi iriyāpathaihi akusalamaiva karauntā vicaranti| bālāti idhalaukatthaṃ paralaukatthañ‍ca  ajānantā idha bālā nāma| dummaidhāti nippañ‍ñā| na hi pañ‍ñāya duṭṭhattaṃ nāma atthi| amittainaivāti amittabhūtaina viya vairinā viya hutvā| kaṭukapphalanti tikhiṇaphalaṃ, dukkhaphalanti atthau| na taṃ kammaṃ kataṃ sādhu, yaṃ katvā anutappatīti yaṃ kammaṃ nirayādīsu nibbattanasamatthaṃ dukkhudayaṃ katvā anussaritānussaritakkhaṇai anutappati anusaucati, taṃ kataṃ na sādhu na sundaraṃ na bhaddakaṃ| yassa assumukhauti yassa assūhi tintamukhau raudantau vipākaṃ paṭisaivati anubhauti|

duk‍karanti vattapaṭivattapūraṇādivasaina ābhisamācārikasīlassa kātuṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyatāya duk‍karaṃ| samādānatau paṭṭhāya khaṇḍaṃ akatvā visaisatau ādibrahmacariyakassa carimakacittaṃ pāpaitabbatāya duttitikkhaṃ, sīlasaṃvarādayau vā aparikkhatai katvā sampādaituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyatāya duk‍karaṃ| adhivāsaitabbānaṃ pana dussahanatau khantisaṃvaraṃvasaina duttitikkhaṃ| abyattaināti mandapañ‍ñaina| sāmañ‍ñanti samaṇabhāvau| tatthāti tassa sāmañ‍ñassa| sambādhāti dun‍nivatthaduppārutamātugāmādisammaddā| yatthāti sīlasaṃvarādīnaṃ paribandhabhūtaisu sambādhasaṅkhātaisu visabhāgārammaṇādīsu| atha vā duk‍karantipadassa atthaṃ dassaituṃ duttitikkhanti vuttaṃ| duttitikkhanti dukkhamaṃ duradhivāsiyaṃ| abyattaināti bālaina| sāmañ‍ñanti samaṇadhammau| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yaṃ paṇḍitā kulaputtā dasapi vassāni vīsatipi…pai॰… saṭṭhipi vassāni dantaibhi dantamādhāya jivhāya tāluṃ āhac‍ca caitasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇhitvā aikāsanaṃ aikabhattaṃ paṭisaivamānā āpāṇakauṭikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carantā sāmañ‍ñaṃ karaunti, taṃ bālā abyattā kātuṃ na sak‍kauntīti| bahūhi tattha sambādhāti tasmiṃ sāmañ‍ñasaṅkhātai ariyamaggai bahū sambādhā, maggādhigamāya paṭipan‍nassa bahū parissayāti atthau|

appamaiyyaṃ paminantauti appamaiyyaṃ khīṇāsavapuggalaṃ ‘‘aittakasīlau ayaṃ aittakasamādhi aittakapañ‍ñau’’ti aivaṃ minantau| kaudha vidvā vikappayaiti kau idha vidvā maidhāvī vikappaiyya, khīṇāsavauva khīṇāsavaṃ minantau vikappaiyyāti dīpaiti| nivutaṃ mañ‍ñaiti yau pana puthuj‍janau minaituṃ ārabhati, taṃ nivutaṃ avakuj‍japañ‍ñaṃ mañ‍ñāmi| akissavanti kissavā vuc‍cati pañ‍ñā, nippañ‍ñanti atthau|

kuṭhārīti attacchaidakaṭṭhaina kuṭhārisadisī pharusavācā| chindatīti kusalamūlasaṅkhātai mūlaiyaiva nikantati| visaṃ halāhalaṃ ivāti halāhalasaṅkhātaṃ visaṃ iva| aivaṃ viraddhaṃ pātaitīti viraddhaṃ  aparaddhaṃ khalitapuggalaṃ aivaṃ apāyaisu vinipātaiti| vācā dubbhāsitā yathāti yathā vācā ariyūpavādanavasaina dubbhāsitā|

92. nindiyanti nindanīyaṃ| taṃ vā nindati yau pasaṃsiyauti yau guṇavisiṭṭhatāya pasaṃsārahau puggalau, taṃ vā sau pāpicchatādīni āraupaitvā garahati| vicinātīti upacināti| kalinti aparādhaṃ| ayaṃ kalīti ayaṃ aparādhau| akkhaisūti jūtakīḷanakkhaisu| sabbassāpi sahāpi attanāti sabbaina attanau dhanaināpi attanāpi saddhiṃ| sugataisūti saubhanagamanattā, sundaraṃ ṭhānaṃ gatattā, sammā gatattā, sammā ca gadattā sugatasaṅkhātaisu buddhādīsu| manaṃ padausayaiti yau manaṃ padausaiyya, tassa ayaṃ manaupadausau aiva mahattarau kalīti vuttaṃ hauti| kasmā? yasmā sataṃ sahassānaṃ…pai॰… pāpakanti| tattha sataṃ sahassānanti nirabbudagaṇanāya satasahassaṃ| chattiṃsatīti aparāni chattiṃsati  nirabbudāni| pañ‍ca cāti abbudagaṇanāya pañ‍ca ca abbudāni| tasmā vassagaṇanāya aittakau sau kālau, yaṃ kālaṃ ariyagarahivācaṃ manañ‍ca paṇidhāya pāpakaṃ nirayaṃ upaiti, tattha pac‍catīti vuttaṃ hauti| idañ‍ca saṅkhaipaina padumanirayai āyuppamāṇaṃ, vitthāraina pana paratau āgamissati|

laubhaguṇaiti ‘‘guṇau’’ti bālaihi diṭṭhattā, anaikakkhattuṃ pavattitattā ca laubhauyaiva laubhaguṇau, tasmiṃ laubhaguṇai, taṇhāyāti atthau| anuyuttauti anu anu yuttau| avadañ‍ñūti avacanañ‍ñū, buddhānampi auvādassa aggahaṇatau| maccharīti pañ‍cavidhamacchariyaina maccharī| paisuṇiyaṃ anuyuttauti paisuṇiyasmiṃ anuyuttau aggasāvakānaṃ bhaidanaina| kaukālikañhi mīyamānaṃ auvadantaina āyasmatā mahāmauggal‍lānaina bhāsitā imā gāthāti| mukhaduggāti mukhavisama| vibhūtāti vigatabhūta alikavādi| anariyāti asappurisa| bhūnahūti bhūtihanaka attanau buddhivināsaka| purisantāti purisādhama| kalīti alakkhipurisa| avajātakaputtāti buddhassa bhagavatau avajātaputta| mā bahubhāṇidha nairayikausīti idāni bahubhāṇī mā hauhi, nairayikau asi jātau| rajamākirasīti kilaisarajaṃ attani pakkhipasi| santaiti samitakilaisai khīṇāsavai| kibbisakārīti pāpakāri| papatanti narakaṃ|

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyanti idaṃ taṇhādīnaṃ sabhāvabhaidatau avatthābhaidatau ca anaikabhaidakaṃ dassaituṃ anaikaihi suttapadaihi udāharaṇavasaina dassitaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttanti vaiditabbaṃ|

pasan‍naināti  kammakammaphalādīni saddahantaina|

93. iddhanti hatthūpagasīsūpagādialaṅkāraihi maṇikanakādīhi ca samiddhaṃ| phītanti tailamadhuphāṇitādīhi ca dhanadhañ‍ñādīhi ca vipulaṃ| ākiṇṇamanussanti nirantaramanussaṃ| sambādhabyūhanti byūhā vuc‍canti anibbiddharacchāyau| yaisu paviṭṭhamaggainaiva niggacchanti, tai sambādhā byūhakā aitthāti sambādhabyūhaṃ| imināpi tassa nagarassa ghanavāsamaiva dīpaiti| bhantaināti damathaṃ anupagataina, itau citau ca paribbhamantaina vā| apāpakanti alāmakaṃ| avaic‍cappasādaināti acalappasādaina, sac‍cappaṭivaidhatau āgataina pasādaina|

paic‍ca  sau labhataiti yau bhūtai daṇḍaina na hiṃsati, sau puggalau paralaukai manussabhūtau manussasukhaṃ daivabhūtau dibbasukhaṃ ubhayaṃ atik‍kantau nibbānasukhaṃ labhatīti atthau|

94. cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatīti janapadacārikaṃ gamissati| kasmā pana bhagavā janapadacārikaṃ caratīti? sattahi kāraṇaihi buddhā bhagavantau janapadacārikaṃ caranti – daisantaragatānaṃ vainaiyyānaṃ vinayanattaṃ, tatra ṭhitānaṃ ussuk‍kasamuppādanaṃ, bhāvakānaṃ aikasmiṃ ṭhānai nibaddhavāsapariharaṇaṃ attanau ca tattha anāsaṅgadassanaṃ, sambuddhavasitaṭṭhānatāya daisānaṃ caitiyabhāvasampādanaṃ, bahūnaṃ sattānaṃ dassanūpasaṅkamanādīhi puñ‍ñaughappasavanaṃ, avuṭṭhiādiupaddavūpasamanañ‍cāti imaihi sattahi kāraṇaihi buddhā bhagavantau janapadacārikaṃ carantīti vaiditabbaṃ|

isidattapurāṇāti isidattau ca purāṇau ca, taisu isidattau sakadāgāmī| purāṇau sautāpan‍nau| sākaitaiti ‘‘sākaitau’’ti laddhanāmai attanau bhaugagāmakai| maggai purisaṃ ṭhapaisunti taisaṃ kira gāmadvāraina bhagavatau gamanamaggau, tasmā ‘‘sacai bhagavā amhākaṃ suttānaṃ vā pamattānaṃ vā gacchaiyya, atha passituṃ na labhaiyyāmā’’ti maggamajjhai purisaṃ ṭhapaisuṃ| anubandhiṃsūti na dūratauva, piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhiṃsu| bhagavā hi sakaṭamaggassa majjhai jaṅghamaggaina agamāsi, itarai ubhausu passaisu anugacchantā agamaṃsu| maggā auk‍kammāti buddhā hi kainaci saddhiṃ gacchantāva paṭisanthāraṃ karaunti kainaci saddhiṃ ṭhitā kainaci saddhiṃ divasabhāgampi nisin‍nā, tasmā bhagavā cintaisi – ‘‘imai mayhaṃ sāsanai val‍labhā āgataphalā, imaihi saddhiṃ nisīditvā divasabhāgaṃ paṭisanthāraṃ karissāmī’’ti| maggatau auk‍kamitvā yainañ‍ñataraṃ  rukkhamūlaṃ tainupasaṅkami| pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdīti tai kira chattupāhanakattaradaṇḍapādabbhañ‍janatailāni caiva aṭṭhavidhañ‍ca pānakaṃ sarabhapādapal‍laṅkañ‍ca gāhāpaitvā āgamaṃsu| atha naṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā adaṃsu| satthā tattha nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsūti ‘‘chattupāhanādīni bhikkhusaṅghassa daithā’’ti vatvā bhagavantaṃ vanditvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu|

sāvatthiyā  kausalaisu cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatītiādi sabbaṃ majjhimadaisavasainaiva vuttaṃ| kasmā? niyatāciṇṇattā| bhagavatau hi cārikacaraṇaṃ majjhimadaisaiyaiva| sacaipi pac‍cantadaisai gacchati, majjhimadaisaiyaiva aruṇaṃ uṭṭhāpaitīti niyatāciṇṇaṃ, tasmā majjhimadaisavasainaiva vuttaṃ| kāsīsūti kāsiraṭṭhatau| tathā magadhaisūti magadharaṭṭhatau| āsan‍nai nau bhagavā bhavissatīti aittha na kaivalaṃ āsan‍nattā aiva taisaṃ saumanassaṃ hauti, atha khau ‘‘idāni dānaṃ dātuṃ gandhamālādipūjaṃ kātuṃ dhammaṃ sautuṃ pañhaṃ pucchituṃ labhissāmā’’ti naisaṃ saumanassaṃ hauti|

tasmātiha thapatayau sambādhau gharāvāsauti thapatayau yasmā tumhākaṃ mayi dūrībhūtai anappakaṃ daumanassaṃ āsan‍nai anappakaṃ saumanassaṃ hauti, tasmāpi vaiditabbamaitaṃ ‘‘sambādhau gharāvāsau’’ti| gharāvāsassa hi dausaina tumhākaṃ aivaṃ hauti| sacai pana gharāvāsaṃ pahāya pabbajitā assatha, aivaṃ vau mayā saddhiṃyaiva gacchantānañ‍ca āgacchantānañ‍ca taṃ na bhavaiyyāti imamatthaṃ dīpaintau aivamāha| tattha sakiñ‍canasapalibaudhaṭṭhaina sambādhatā vaiditabbā| mahāgharai vasantassāpi hi sakiñ‍canasapalibaudhaṭṭhaina gharāvāsau sambādhauva| rajaupathauti rāgādirajānaṃ āgamanapathau, āgamanaṭṭhānanti atthau| abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jāti pabbaj‍jā pana akiñ‍canaapalibaudhaṭṭhaina abbhaukāsau| caturatanikaipi hi gabbhai dvin‍naṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pal‍laṅkaina pal‍laṅkaṃ ghaṭṭaitvā nisin‍nānampi akiñ‍canāpalibaudhaṭṭhaina pabbaj‍jā abbhaukāsau nāma hauti| alañ‍ca pana vau thapatayau appamādāyāti aivaṃ sambādhagharāvāsai vasantānaṃ tumhākaṃ appamādamaiva kātuṃ yuttanti atthau|

nāgāti hatthinau| aupavayhāti rañ‍ñau ārauhanayauggā| aikaṃ puratau aikaṃ pacchatau nisīdāpaimāti tai kira dvaipi janā sabbālaṅkārapaṭimaṇḍitā dvīsu nāgaisu tā itthiyau aivaṃ nisīdāpaitvā rañ‍ñau nāgaṃ majjhai katvā ubhausu passaisu gacchanti, tasmā aivamāhaṃsu| nāgaupi rakkhitabbauti yathā kiñ‍ci visaisitaṃ na karauti, aivaṃ rakkhitabbau hauti| tāpi bhaginiyauti yathā pamādaṃ nāpaj‍janti, aivaṃ rakkhitabbā haunti| attāpīti sitakathitavikkhaipitādīni  akarauntaihi attāpi rakkhitabbau hauti| aivaṃ karauntau hi ‘‘sāmidubbhakau aisau’’ti niggahaitabbau hauti|

tasmātiha thapatayauti yasmā tumhai rājā nic‍caṃ rājabhaṇḍaṃ paṭicchāpaiti, tasmāpi sambādhau gharāvāsau rajaupathau| yasmā pana paṃsukūlikaṃ bhikkhuṃ aivaṃ paṭicchāpaintau natthi, tasmā abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā, aivaṃ sabbatthāpi| alañ‍ca khau thapatayau appamādāya, appamādamaiva karauthāti dassaiti|

muttacāgauti vissaṭṭhacāgau| payatapāṇīti āgatāgatānaṃ dānatthāya dhautahatthau| vausaggaratauti vausaggasaṅkhātai cāgai ratau| yācayaugauti yācitabbayuttau| ‘‘yājayaugau’’tipi pāṭhau, dānayuttauti atthau| dānasaṃvibhāgaratauti aitaina appamattakampi kiñ‍ci labhitvā tataupi saṃvibhāgai ratau| appaṭivibhattanti ‘‘idaṃ amhākaṃ bhavissati, idaṃ ayyāna’’nti aivaṃ akatavibhāgaṃ, sabbaṃ dātabbamaiva hutvā ṭhitanti atthau| imaihi khau thapatayau catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hautīti sautāpan‍nau imaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau hautīti atthau| aitaina sautāpan‍naina imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ dhammānaṃ aikantatau labbhamānataṃ dassaiti|

aivaṃ taisaṃ thapatīnaṃ imaihi catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ pariyāyaina dassaitvā idāni nippariyāyaina taṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘tumhai khau thapatayau’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

95. sahassaṃ kappakauṭiyauti sahassaṃ attabhāvā ahaisunti atthau| ‘‘asīti kappakauṭiyau’’tipi pāṭhau, asītiāyukappakauṭiyau ahaisunti atthau| kattha pana tai ahaisunti? āha ‘‘daivai caiva manussai cā’’ti, daivaisu caiva manussaisu cāti atthau| saṃviruḷhamhīti samantatau pal‍lavaggahaṇaina viruḷhai| alabhiṃhanti alabhiṃ ahaṃ| aj‍ja tiṃsaṃ tatau kappāti tatau kappatau aj‍ja sampati ayaṃ kappau tiṃsatimau| tassā sañ‍ñāya vāsanāti tassa buddhagatāya sañ‍ñāya vāsanatau|

taṇhānighātakauti  taṇhāya samucchaidakau| vaṭaṃsakauti pupphamayakaṇṇikau| sabbapupphaihilaṅkatauti nānāpupphaihi alaṅkatau| lapanantarāti uttarādharauṭṭhānaṃ antaratau| auk‍kāti pabhā| muddhanantaradhāyathāti muddhani antaradhāyatha| kaṅkhaṃ vitarāti vimatiṃ vinaudaihi| yassa taṃ sabbadhammaisu, sadā ñāṇaṃ pavattatīti tanti nipātamattaṃ| yassa sabbadhammaisu ākaṅkhappaṭibaddhattā sadā ñāṇaṃ pavattati| sau sabbañ‍ñū bhagavā thairaṃ ānandaṃ aitadabravīti sambandhau| rājā raṭṭhai bhavissatīti sabbasmiṃ raṭṭhai rājā bhavissati| carimanti carimabhavaṃ| sacchikatvāti pac‍cakkhaṃ katvā| dhammatanti catusac‍cadhammaṃ, pac‍caikabaudhiṃ vā|

96. suvaṇṇacchadanaṃ nāvanti ubhausu passaisu suvaṇṇālaṅkāraihi paṭimaṇḍitavasaina chāditaṃ suvaṇṇanāvaṃ| pañhaṃ puṭṭhā viyākāsi, sak‍kassa iti mai sutanti yathā sā daivatā pañhaṃ puṭṭhā sak‍kassa byākāsi, aivaṃ mayāpi sutanti āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau attanā yathāsutaṃ taṃ bhagavatau vadati|

paṃsuthūpaisūti sarīradhātuṃ abbhantarai ṭhapaitvā paṃsūhi katathūpaisu| aivañhi tai bhagavantaṃ uddissakatā nāma haunti, tainaivāha – ‘‘uddissakataisu dasabaladharāna’’nti|

97. daivaputtasarīravaṇṇāti daivaputtasarīrasadisavaṇṇā| subhagasaṇṭhitīti saubhaggayuttasaṇṭhānā| uḷāraṃ vata taṃ āsīti taṃ mayā kataṃ puñ‍ñaṃ uḷāraṃ vata ahausi| yāhanti yā ahaṃ| satasahassaṃ kappai, muditau thūpaṃ apūjaisīti thūpaṃ pūjaitvā satasahassaṃ āyukappai ahaṃ muditauti atthau| anāgantuna vinipātanti apāyupapattiṃ anupagantvā| yaṃ cakkhunti yaṃ pañ‍ñācakkhuṃ| paṇihitanti ṭhapitaṃ| vimuttacittamhīti vimuttacittau amhi| vidhūtalatauti vidhūtataṇhālatau, samucchin‍nataṇhauti atthau|

98. sāmākapatthaudanamattanti sāmākatiṇānaṃ nāḷikaudanamattaṃ| akhilaiti pañ‍can‍naṃ caitaukhilānaṃ abhāvaina akhilai| tasmiñ‍ca aukappayi dhammamuttamanti tasmiṃ pac‍caikabuddhai uttamadhammaṃ pac‍caikabaudhiṃ ‘‘uttamadhammaina nāma imasmiṃ  bhavitabba’’nti saddahiṃ| ‘‘tasmiñ‍ca dhammai paṇidhaisiṃ mānasa’’nti iminā paṭiladdhadhammaṃ ahampi sacchikaraiyyanti cittaṃ paṇidahiṃ| bhavai kudāsupi ca mā apaikkhavāti katthaci bhavai apaikkhavā mā bhavaiyyanti ca paṇidhaisiṃ mānasanti sambandhau|

kurūsūti uttarakurūsu| dīghāyukaisūti taisaṃ vassasahassāyukatāya vuttaṃ| amamaisūti apariggahaisu| pāṇīsūti sattaisu| ahīnagāmīsūti yathāladdhasampattīhi yāvatāyukaṃ aparihīnasabhāvaisu| tidasaupapaj‍jathāti tāvatiṃsau hutvā upapaj‍jiṃ, tidasai vā tāvatiṃsabhavanai upapaj‍jiṃ| visiṭṭhakāyūpagatauti visiṭṭhakāyaisu nānāvaṇṇakāyaisu upagatau| yasassisūti parivāravantaisu| hitāhitāsihīti kusalākusalai vītivattīhi| pac‍cakkhaṃ khvimanti pac‍cakkhaṃ khau imaṃ vacananti adhippāyau|

sakāsīti sau akāsi| balimābhihārīti pūjābaliṃ abhihari| patitassa aikanti tassa hatthatau aikapupphaṃ patitaṃ|

upariṭṭhanti upari vaihāsai ṭhitaṃ| ariṭṭhanti ariṭṭhaṃ nāma pac‍caikasambuddhaṃ| ajjhattañ‍ca bahiddhā cāti ajjhattavisayā ca bahiddhavisayā ca| yai mai vij‍jiṃsūti yai mai pubbai vij‍jamānā ahaisuṃ| jātimaraṇasaṃsārau, natthi dāni punabbhavauti punappunaṃ jāyanamīyanabhūtau saṃsārau punabbhavauti ca vuc‍cati, sau ca dāni natthīti atthau|

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttanti idaṃ vāsanābhāgapuñ‍ñavibhāvanānaṃ nānāsuttapadānaṃ udāharaṇavasaina dassitaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttanti vaiditabbaṃ|

99. ‘‘uddhaṃ adhau…pai॰… apunabbhavāyā’’ti idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttanti vuttaṃ aughataraṇassa ariyamaggakic‍cattā| na caitanā karaṇīyāti na cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ| dhammatāti dhammasabhāvau|

100. yadā havaiti yasmiṃ havai kālai| pātubhavantīti uppaj‍janti| dhammāti anulaumapac‍cayākārapaṭivaidhasādhakā baudhipakkhiyadhammā| pātubhavantīti vā pakāsainti, abhisamayavasaina pākaṭā haunti| dhammāti catuariyasac‍cadhammā| ātāpau vuc‍cati kilaisasantāpanaṭṭhaina vīriyaṃ| ātāpinauti  sammappadhānavīriyavatau| jhāyatauti ārammaṇūpanijjhānalakkhaṇaina lakkhaṇūpanijjhānalakkhaṇaina ca jhānaina jhāyantassa| brāhmaṇassāti bāhitapāpassa khīṇāsavassa| athassa kaṅkhā vapayanti sabbāti athassa aivaṃ pātubhūtadhammassa yā tā ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, phusatīti? nau kal‍lau pañhauti bhagavā avaucā’’tiādinā (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.12) nayaina ‘‘katamaṃ nu khau, bhantai, jarāmaraṇaṃ, kassa panidaṃ jarāmaraṇanti? nau kal‍lau pañhauti bhagavā avaucā’’tiādinā (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.35) ca nayaina pac‍cayākārakaṅkhā vuttā| yā ca  pac‍cayākārassaiva appaṭividdhattā ‘‘ahausiṃ nu khau ahamatītamaddhāna’’ntiādikā (ma॰ ni॰ 1.18; saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.20) sauḷasakaṅkhā ‘‘buddhai kaṅkhati dhammai kaṅkhatī’’tiādikā (dha॰ sa॰ 1008) aṭṭha ca kaṅkhā āgatā, tā sabbā vapayanti apagacchanti nirujjhanti, kasmā? yatau pajānāti sahaitudhammaṃ, yasmā avij‍jādikaina haitunā sahaitukaṃ imaṃ saṅkhārādiṃ kaivalaṃ dukkhakkhandhadhammaṃ pajānāti añ‍ñāsi paṭivijjhati|

yatau khayaṃ pac‍cayānaṃ avaidīhi yasmā pac‍cayānaṃ khayasaṅkhātaṃ nibbānaṃ avaidi añ‍ñāsi paṭivijjhi, tasmā yadāssa ātāpinau jhāyatau brāhmaṇassa vuttappakārā dhammā pātubhavanti| athassa yā nibbānassa aviditattā kaṅkhā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ, sabbāpi tā kaṅkhā vapayantīti|

ārañ‍ñanti ārañ‍ñakaṃ| añ‍ñātuñchaina yāpaintanti kulaisu añ‍ñātau nic‍canavauyaiva hutvā uñchaina piṇḍacariyāya yāpaintaṃ| atha vā abhilakkhitaisu issarajanagaihaisu kaṭukabhaṇḍasambhāraṃ sugandhabhaujanaṃ pariyaisantassa uñchanaṃ ñātuñchanaṃ nāma, gharapaṭipāṭiyā pana dvārai ṭhitaina laddhamissakabhaujanaṃ añ‍ñātuñchanaṃ nāma| idaṃ idha adhippaitaṃ| taina yāpaintaṃ| kāmaisu anapaikkhinanti vatthukāmakilaisakāmaisu nirapaikkhaṃ|

chaitvāti vadhitvā| sukhaṃ saitīti kaudhapariḷāhaina aparidayhamānattā sukhaṃ sayati| na saucatīti kaudhavināsaina vinaṭṭhadaumanassattā na saucati| visamūlassāti dukkhavipākassa| madhuraggassāti yaṃ ak‍kuṭṭhassa pac‍cak‍kausitvā pahaṭassa paṭippaharitvā sukhaṃ uppaj‍jati, taṃ sandhāya sau ‘‘madhuraggau’’ti vuttau| imasmiñhi ṭhānai pariyausānaṃ ‘‘agga’’nti vuttaṃ| ariyāti buddhādayau|

hanaiti  hanaiyya| uppatitanti asamugghāṭitaṃ avikkhambhituppan‍navasaina samudācāruppan‍navasaina samudācarantaṃ| vinaudayaiti attanau santānatau nīharaiyya|

101. sattiyāti daisanāsīsamaitaṃ, aikataudhārādinā satthaināti atthau| aumaṭṭhauti pahaṭau| cattārau hi pahārā aumaṭṭhau ummaṭṭhau maṭṭhau vimaṭṭhauti| tattha upari ṭhatvā adhaumukhaṃ din‍nappahārau aumaṭṭhau nāma, adhau ṭhatvā uddhaṃ mukhaṃ din‍nappahārau ummaṭṭhau nāma, aggaḷasūci viya vinivijjhitvā katau maṭṭhau nāma, saisau sabbaupi vimaṭṭhau nāma| imasmiṃ pana ṭhānai aumaṭṭhau  gahitau| sau hi sabbadāruṇau duruddharaṇasal‍lau duttikicchau antaudausau antaupubbalauhitau ca hauti| pubbalauhitaṃ anikkhamitvā vaṇamukhaṃ pariyaunandhitvā tiṭṭhati| pubbalauhitaṃ nīharitukāmaihi mañ‍caina saddhiṃ bandhitvā adhausirau kātabbau hauti, maraṇaṃ vā maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ pāpuṇāti| paribbajaiti viharaiyya|

imāya gāthāya kiṃ kathitaṃ? yathā sattiyā aumaṭṭhapurisau sal‍lubbāhanavaṇatikicchanānaṃ atthāya vīriyaṃ ārabhati payaugaṃ karauti parak‍kamati| yathā ca dayhamānai matthakai ādittasirau tassa nibbāpanatthāya vīriyaṃ ārabhati payaugaṃ karauti parak‍kamati, aivamaivaṃ bhikkhu kāmarāgappahānāya satau appamattau hutvā viharaiyya bhagavāti kathaisi|

aivaṃ daivatāya kathitai atha bhagavā cintaisi – ‘‘imāya daivatāya upamā daḷhaṃ katvā ānītā, atthaṃ pana parittakaṃ gahaitvā ṭhitā| punappunaṃ kathaintīpi hi aisā kāmarāgassa vikkhambhanappahānamaiva kathaiyya, yāva ca kāmarāgau maggaina na samugghāṭiyyati, tāva anubandhauva hautī’’ti tamaiva upamaṃ gahaitvā paṭhamamaggavasaina daivatāya vinivaṭṭaitvā dassaintau ‘‘sattiyā viya aumaṭṭhau’’ti dutiyagāthamāha| tassatthau purimanayānusāraina vaiditabbau|

laukāmisanti kāmaguṇau| santipaikkhauti sabbasaṅkhārūpasamaṃ nibbānaṃ apaikkhamānau| pañ‍ñavāti pañ‍ñavantau| pahitattauti nibbānaṃ patipaisitacittau| viratau kāmasañ‍ñāyāti yāya kāyaci sabbatau kāmasañ‍ñāya  catutthamaggasampayuttāya samucchaidaviratiyā viratau| ‘‘virattau’’tipi pāṭhau| kāmasañ‍ñāyāti pana bhummavacanaṃ hauti| sagāthāvaggai (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.96) ‘‘kāmasañ‍ñāsū’’ti pāṭhau| catūhi maggaihi dasan‍nampi saṃyaujanānaṃ atītattā sabbasaṃyaujanātītau| catutthamaggainaiva vā uddhambhāgiyasaṃyaujanātītau tatra tatrābhinandanatau nandisaṅkhātāya taṇhāya tiṇṇañ‍ca bhavānaṃ parikkhīṇattā nandibhavaparikkhīṇau| sau tādisau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu gambhīrai saṃsāraṇṇavai na sīdati|

saddahānauti yaina pubbabhāgai kāyasucaritādibhaidaina, aparabhāgai ca sattattiṃsabaudhipakkhiyabhaidaina dhammaina arahantau buddhapac‍caikabuddhabuddhasāvakā nibbānaṃ pattā| taṃ saddahānau arahataṃ dhammaṃ nibbānappattiyā laukiyalaukuttarapañ‍ñaṃ labhati, tañ‍ca khau na saddhāmattakainaiva| yasmā pana saddhājātau upasaṅkamati, upasaṅkamantau payirupāsati, payirupāsantau  sautaṃ audahati, audahitasautau dhammaṃ suṇāti, tasmā upasaṅkamanatau paṭṭhāya yāva dhammassavanaina sussūsaṃ labhatai pañ‍ñaṃ|

kiṃ vuttaṃ hauti? taṃ dhammaṃ saddahitvāpi ācariyupajjhāyai kālaina kālaṃ upasaṅkamitvāpi vattakaraṇaina payirupāsitvā yadā payirupāsanāya ārādhitacittā kiñ‍ci vattukāmā haunti| atha adhigatāya sautukāmatāya sautaṃ audahitvā suṇantau labhatīti aivaṃ sussūsampi ca satiavippavāsaina appamattau subhāsitadubbhāsitañ‍ñutāya vicakkhaṇau aiva labhati, na itarau| taināha – ‘‘appamattau vicakkhaṇau’’ti|

patirūpakārīti daisakālādīni ahāpaitvā laukiyassa laukuttarassa dhammassa patirūpaṃ adhigamūpāyaṃ karautīti patirūpakārī| dhuravāti caitasikavīriyavasaina anikkhittadhurau| uṭṭhātāti kāyikavīriyavasaina uṭṭhānasampan‍nau asithilaparak‍kamau| vindatai dhananti laukiyalaukuttaradhanaṃ adhigacchati| sac‍caināti vacīsac‍caina paramatthasac‍caina ca| buddhapac‍caikabuddhaariyasāvakā nibbutiṃ pāpuṇantā kittimpi pāpuṇantiyaiva| dadanti paraisaṃ yaṃ kiñ‍ci icchitaṃ patthitaṃ daintau mittāni ganthati sampādaiti karautīti  atthau| duddadaṃ vā dadantau ganthati, dānamukhaina cattāripi saṅgahavatthūni gahitānīti vaiditabbāni| taihi mittāni karaunti| asmā laukā paraṃ laukaṃ, sa vai paic‍ca na saucatīti yassa puggalassa imai saddhādayau dhammā vij‍janti, sau imasmā laukā paraṃ laukaṃ gantvā na saucati, saukakāraṇaṃ tassa natthīti atthau|

‘‘yassaitai caturau dhammā, saddhassa gharamaisinau|

sac‍caṃ dhammau dhiti cāgau, sa vai paic‍ca na saucatī’’ti|| (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.246; su॰ ni॰ 190) –

gāthaṃ avasaisaṃ katvā udāhaṭaṃ| āḷavakasuttai hi imā gāthā āḷavakaina ‘‘kathaṃ su labhatai pañ‍ña’’ntiādinā (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.246; su॰ ni॰ 187) puṭṭhaina bhagavatā bhāsitāti|

yaina kainaci vaṇṇaināti yaina kainaci kāraṇaina, pakāraina vā| saṃvāsauti aikasmiṃ ṭhānai sahavāsau samāgamau| tanti tathā samāgataṃ anukampitabbaṃ purisaṃ| manasā cai pasan‍naināti karuṇāsamussāhitaina  pasādaina pasan‍naina manasā| na taina hauti saṃyuttauti taina yathāvuttaina anusāsanaina kāmacchandādīnaṃ saṃyaujanavasaina saṃyuttau nāma na hauti| yānukampā anuddayāti yā ariyamaggasampāpanavasaina karuṇāyanā, maittāyanā cāti atthau|

102. rāgau ca dausau cāti rāgadausā haiṭṭhā vuttanayāva| kutaunidānāti kiṃnidānā kiṃhaitukā| pac‍cattavacanassa hi ayaṃ tau-ādaisau, samāsai cassa laupābhāvau vaiditabbau| aratī ratī laumahaṃsau kutaujāti yāyaṃ pantaisu saināsanaisu, adhikusalaisu ca dhammaisu arati uk‍kaṇṭhitā, yā ca pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu rati abhirati āsatti kīḷanādi, yau ca laumahaṃsasamuṭṭhānatau laumahaṃsasaṅkhātau cittutrāsau, imai tayau dhammā kutau jātā kutau nibbattāti pucchā| kutau samuṭṭhāyāti kutau uppaj‍jitvā| manauti kusalacittaṃ| vitak‍kāti kāmavitak‍kādayau| kumārakā dhaṅkamivausajantīti yathā kumārakā kīḷantā kākaṃ suttaina pādai bandhitvā ausajanti khipanti, aivaṃ kusalamanaṃ akusalavitak‍kā kutau samuṭṭhāya ausajantīti pucchā|

rāgau  cāti dutiyagāthā tassā vissaj‍janaṃ| tattha itauti attabhāvaṃ sandhāyāha| attabhāvanidānā hi rāgadausā, arati rati laumahaṃsā ca attabhāvatau jātā| kāmavitak‍kādayau attabhāvatau aiva samuṭṭhāya kusalamanaṃ ausajanti| taina tadañ‍ñaṃ pakatiādikāraṇaṃ paṭikkhipantau āha – ‘‘itaunidānā itau samuṭṭhāyā’’ti| purimagāthāya vuttanayainaittha saddasiddhi vaiditabbā|

idāni yvāyaṃ ‘‘itaunidānā’’tiādīsu attabhāvanidānā attabhāvatau jātā attabhāvatau samuṭṭhāyāti atthau vuttau, taṃ sādhaintau āha – ‘‘snaihajā attasambhūtā’’ti| aitai hi rāgādayau vitak‍kapariyausānā taṇhāsnaihaina jātā| tathā jāyantā ca pañ‍cupādānakkhandhabhaidai attabhāvasaṅkhātai attani sambhūtā| taināha – ‘‘snaihajā attasambhūtā’’ti| idāni tadatthajautikaṃ upamaṃ dassaiti ‘‘nigraudhassaiva khandhajā’’ti| tattha khandhajāti khandhaisu jātā pārauhā| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yathā nigraudhassa khandhajasaṅkhātā pārauhā āpaurasasaṅkhātai snaihai sati jāyanti, jāyantā ca tasmiṃyaiva nigraudhai taisu taisu sākhappadaisaisu sambhavanti, aivaṃ aitai rāgādayau ajjhattaṃ taṇhāsnaihai sati jāyanti, jāyantā ca tasmiṃyaiva attabhāvai taisu taisu cakkhādippadaisaisu iṭṭhārammaṇaisu sambhavanti| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘snaihajā attasambhūtā’’ti| puthu visattā kāmaisūti yasmā  rāgaupi pañ‍cakāmaguṇikādivasaina, dausaupi āghātavatthuādivasaina aratiādayaupi tassa tassa bhaidassa vasaināti sabbathā sabbaipimai kilaisā puthu anaikappakārā hutvā vatthudvārārammaṇādivasaina taisu taisu kāmaisu tathā tathā visattā laggā saṃsibbitvā ṭhitā| kimiva? māluvāva vitatā vanai yathā vanai vitatā māluvā taisu taisu rukkhasākhappasākhādibhaidaisu visattā hauti laggā saṃsibbitvā ṭhitā, aivaṃ aitai kilaisā dhammā, tasmā aittha puthupabhaidaisu vatthukāmaisu visattaṃ kilaisagahanaṃ|

yai naṃ pajānanti yataunidānaṃ, tai naṃ vinaudainti suṇauhi yakkha| tassatthau – yai sattā naṃ kilaisagahanaṃ ‘‘itaunidānaṃ aisa uppaj‍jatī’’ti jānanti, tai naṃ taṇhāsinaihasinaihitai attabhāvai uppaj‍jatīti  ñatvā taṃ taṇhāsinaihaṃ ādīnavānupassanādibhāvanāñāṇagginā visausaintā vinaudainti pajahanti, aivaṃ amhākaṃ bhāsitaṃ suṇauhi yakkhāti| tai duttaraṃ aughamimaṃ taranti, atiṇṇapubbaṃ apunabbhavāyāti yai hi saṃkilaisagahanaṃ vinaudainti, tai aikantaina maggaṃ bhāvainti| na hi maggabhāvanaṃ vinā kilaisavinaudanaṃ atthi| aivaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintā tai pakatiñāṇaina duttaraṃ kāmaughādiṃ catubbidhaṃ aughaṃ iminā dīghaina addhunā supinantainapi atiṇṇapubbaṃ anatik‍kantapubbaṃ apunabbhavāya nibbānāya taranti|

duk‍karaṃ bhagavāti aikau kira daivaputtau pubbayaugāvacarau bahalakilaisatāya sappayaugaina kilaisai vikkhambhaintau samaṇadhammaṃ katvā pubbahaitumandatāya ariyabhūmiṃ appatvāva kālaṃ katvā daivalaukai nibbattau, sau tathāgataṃ upasaṅkamitvā duk‍karabhāvaṃ āraucaintau aivamāha| tattha duk‍karanti dasapi vassāni…pai॰… saṭṭhipi vassāni aikantaparisuddhassa samaṇadhammassa karaṇaṃ nāmaitaṃ duk‍karaṃ| saikkhāti satta saikkhā| sīlasamāhitāti sīlaina samāhitā samupaitā| ṭhitattāti patiṭṭhitasabhāvā| aivaṃ pucchitapañhaṃ vissaj‍jitvā uparipañhaṃ samuṭṭhāpanatthaṃ ‘‘anagāriyupaitassā’’tiādimāha| tattha anagāriyupaitassāti anagāriyaṃ niggaihabhāvaṃ upagatassa, pabbajitassāti atthau| tuṭṭhīti catupac‍cayasantausau|

bhāvanāyāti cittavūpasamabhāvanāya| tai chaitvā mac‍cunau jālanti yai rattindivaṃ indriyūpasamai ratā, tai dussamādahaṃ cittaṃ samādahanti| yai samāhitacittā, tai catupac‍cayasantausaṃ pūraintā na kilamanti| yai santuṭṭhā, tai sīlaṃ pūraintā na kilamanti| yai sīlai patiṭṭhitā satta saikkhā, tai ariyā mac‍cunau jālasaṅkhātaṃ kilaisajālaṃ chinditvā gacchanti|

duggamauti  sac‍camaitaṃ, bhantai, yai indriyūpasamai ratā, tai dussamādahaṃ cittaṃ samādahanti| yai samāhitacittā, tai catupac‍cayasantausaṃ pūraintā na kilamanti| yai santuṭṭhā, tai sīlaṃ pūraintā na kilamanti| yai sīlai paramaggāhinau satta saikkhā, tai ariyā mac‍cunau jālasaṅkhātaṃ kilaisajālaṃ chinditvā gacchanti| kiṃ na gamissanti, ayaṃ pana duggamau ‘‘bhagavā  visamau maggau’’ti āha| tattha kiñ‍cāpi ariyamaggau naiva duggamau na visamau, pubbabhāgapaṭipadāya panassa bahū parissayā haunti, tasmā aivaṃ vuttau| avaṃsirāti ñāṇasiraina adhausirā hutvā papatanti| ariyamaggaṃ ārauhituṃ asamatthatāya aiva tai maggai papatantīti vuc‍canti| ariyānaṃ samau maggauti svaiva maggau ariyānaṃ samau hauti| visamai samāti visamaipi sattakāyai samā aiva|

103. idañhi taṃ jaitavananti anāthapiṇḍikau daivaputtau jaitavanassa caiva buddhādīnañ‍ca vaṇṇabhaṇanatthaṃ āgantvā aivamāha| isisaṅghanisaivitanti bhikkhusaṅghanivaisitaṃ| aivaṃ paṭhamagāthāya jaitavanassa vaṇṇaṃ kathaitvā idāni ariyamaggassa vaṇṇaṃ kathaintau ‘‘kammaṃ vij‍jā’’tiādimāha| tattha kammanti maggacaitanā| vij‍jāti maggapañ‍ñā| dhammauti samādhi, samādhipakkhikā vā dhammā| sīlaṃ jīvitamuttamanti sīlai patiṭṭhitassa jīvitañ‍ca uttamanti dassaiti| atha vā vij‍jāti diṭṭhisaṅkappā| dhammauti vāyāmasatisamādhayau| sīlanti vācākammantājīvā| jīvitamuttamanti aitasmiṃ sīlai patiṭṭhitassa jīvitaṃ nāma uttamanti| ‘‘aitaina mac‍cā sujjhantī’’ti aitaina aṭṭhaṅgikaina maggaina sattā visujjhanti|

tasmāti yasmā maggaina sujjhanti, na gauttadhanaihi, tasmā| yaunisau vicinai dhammanti upāyaina baudhipakkhiyadhammaṃ vicinaiyya| aivaṃ tattha visujjhatīti aivaṃ tasmiṃ ariyamaggai visujjhati| atha vā yaunisau vicinai dhammanti upāyaina ariyasac‍cadhammaṃ vicinaiyya| aivaṃ tattha visujjhatīti aivaṃ taisu catūsu ariyasac‍caisu visujjhati| idāni sāriputtatthairassa vaṇṇaṃ kathaintau ‘‘sāriputtauvā’’tiādimāha| tattha sāriputtauvāti avadhāraṇavacanaṃ, aitaihi pañ‍ñādīhi sāriputtauva saiyyauti vadati| upasamaināti kilaisavūpasamaina| pāraṅgatauti nibbānaṃ gatau, yau kauci nibbānapattau bhikkhu, na tādisau| aitāvaparamau siyā, na thairā uttaritarau nāma sāvakau atthīti vadati|

atītanti atītai pañ‍cakkhandhai| nānvāgamaiyyāti taṇhādiṭṭhīhi nānugacchaiyya| nappaṭikaṅkhaiti taṇhādiṭṭhīhi na patthaiyya| yadatītanti idamaittha kāraṇavacanaṃ| yasmā yaṃ atītaṃ, taṃ  pahīnaṃ niruddhaṃ atthaṅgataṃ, tasmā  taṃ nānugacchaiyya| yasmā ca yaṃ tattha anāgataṃ, taṃ appattaṃ ajātaṃ anibbattaṃ, tasmā tampi na patthaiyya| tattha tatthāti pac‍cuppan‍nampi dhammaṃ yattha yatthaiva sau uppan‍nau, tattha tatthaiva naṃ anic‍cānupassanādīhi sattahi anupassanāhi vipassati, arañ‍ñādīsu vā tattha tattha vipassati| asaṃhīraṃ asaṃkuppanti idaṃ vipassanāpaṭivipassanādassanatthaṃ vuttaṃ| vipassanā hi rāgādīhi na saṃhirati na kuppatīti asaṃhīrā asaṃkuppā, taṃ anubrūhayai vaḍḍhaiyya paṭivipassaiyyāti vuttaṃ hauti| atha vā nibbānaṃ rāgādīhi na saṃhirati na kuppatīti asaṃhīraṃ asaṃkuppaṃ, taṃ vidvā paṇḍitau bhikkhu anubrūhayai, punappunaṃ tadārammaṇaṃ phalasamāpattiṃ appaintau vaḍḍhaiyyāti atthau|

tassa pana anubrūhanassa atthāya aj‍jaiva kic‍camātappanti kilaisānaṃ ātāpanaparitāpanaina ‘‘ātappa’’nti laddhanāmaṃ vīriyaṃ aj‍jaiva kātabbaṃ| kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvaiti svai jīvitaṃ vā maraṇaṃ vā kau jānāti| aj‍jaiva dānaṃ dassāmi, sīlaṃ vā rakkhissāmi, añ‍ñataraṃ vā pana kusalaṃ karissāmi, ‘‘aj‍ja tāva papañ‍cau atthi, svai vā punadivasai vā jānissāmī’’ti cittaṃ anuppādaitvā ‘‘aj‍jaiva karissāmī’’ti aivaṃ vīriyaṃ kātabbanti dassaiti| mahāsainaināti ahivicchikavisasatthādīni hi anaikāni maraṇakāraṇāni tassa saināti tāya mahatiyā saināya vasaina mahāsainaina aivarūpaina mac‍cunā saddhiṃ ‘‘katipāhaṃ tāva āgamaihi, yāvāhaṃ buddhapūjādiṃ attanau avassayaṃ kammaṃ karaumī’’ti aivaṃ mittasanthavākārasaṅkhātau vā ‘‘idaṃ sataṃ vā sahassaṃ vā gahaitvā katipāhaṃ āgamaihī’’ti aivaṃ lañ‍jānuppadānasaṅkhātau vā ‘‘iminā balarāsinā paṭibāhissāmī’’ti aivaṃ balarāsisaṅkhātau vā saṅgarau natthi| saṅgarauti hi mittakaraṇalañ‍jadānabalarāsisaṅkaḍḍhanānaṃ nāmaṃ, tasmā ayamatthau vuttau| atanditanti analasaṃ uṭṭhāhakaṃ| aivaṃ paṭipan‍nattā bhaddau aikarattau assāti bhaddaikarattau| itīti aivaṃ paṭipan‍naṃ puggalaṃ ‘‘bhaddaikarattau aya’’nti rāgādisantatāya santau buddhamuni ācikkhati|

cakkhunā pañ‍ñāya cāti cakkhunā ca pañ‍ñāya ca| cakkhubhūtāya vā pañ‍ñāya| satiyā pañ‍ñāya cāti satiyā ca pañ‍ñāya ca, sativisiṭṭhāya vā pañ‍ñāya| kāyaināti nāmakāyaina|

dibbacakkhu  suvisuddhanti dibbaṃ cakkhu suvisuddhaṃ, yaṃ sacchikarautīti adhippāyau| pubbainivāsāti purimāsu jātīsu nivutthakkhandhā| iddhividhāti iddhikauṭṭhāsā| niraudhauti nibbānaṃ| saisaṃ suviñ‍ñaiyyamaiva|

104. yassa  sailūpamaṃ cittanti aikaghanaṃ sailaṃ viya pakativātaihi laukadhammavātaihi akampanīyatau yassa cittaṃ sailūpamaṃ| taināha – ‘‘ṭhitaṃ nānupakampatī’’ti| rajanīyaisūti lābhādīsu| kaupanaiyyaiti alābhādikai| kutau naṃ dukkhamaissatīti taṃ aivaṃ bhāvitacittaṃ vītik‍kantalaukadhammaṃ uttamapurisaṃ laukadhammahaitukaṃ dukkhaṃ nānugamissati|

yau brāhmaṇauti bāhitapāpadhammatāya brāhmaṇau, na diṭṭhamaṅgalikatāya huṃhuṅkārakasāvādipāpadhammayuttau hutvā kaivalaṃ jātimattaina brāhmaṇauti paṭijānāti| sau brāhmaṇau bāhitapāpadhammattā huṃhuṅkārappahānaina nihuṃhuṅkau| rāgādikasāvābhāvaina nik‍kabhāvau| sīlasaṃvaraina saṃyatacittatāya yatattau| catumaggañāṇasaṅkhātaihi vaidaihi antaṃ nibbānaṃ, vaidānaṃ vā antaṃ gatattā vaidantagū| maggabrahmacariyassa vusitattā vūsitabrahmacariyau| dhammaina sau brahmavādaṃ vadaiyyāti sau ‘‘brāhmaṇau aha’’nti aitaṃ vādaṃ vadaiyya| yassa sakalalaukasan‍nivāsai kuhiñ‍ci aikārammaṇaipi rāgussadau dausussadau mauhussadau mānussadau diṭṭhussadauti imai ussadā natthīti atthau|

na gādhatīti na patiṭṭhahati| suk‍kāti suk‍kasaṅkhātā gahā| yadi candimasūriyādīnaṃ pabhā tattha natthi, tamau aiva ca siyāti āsaṅkamānai sandhāyāha ‘‘tamau tattha na vij‍jatī’’ti| yadā ca attanāvaidītiādīsu aivaṃvidhaṃ nibbānaṃ attapac‍cakkhaina ñāṇaina yadā vindati, atha rūpārūpadhammatau sukhadukkhatau ca vippamuttau hautīti|

sakaisu dhammaisūti sakaattabhāvasaṅkhātaisu upādānakkhandhaisu| yaibhuyyaina hi ajjhattaṃ vipassanābhinivaisau hautīti| aitaṃ pisācanti ajakalāpaka, aitaṃ tayā vuttaṃ pisācaṃ kilaisapisācañ‍ca| pak‍kulanti tayā kataṃ ak‍kulaṃ pak‍kulakaraṇañ‍ca| ativattatīti atik‍kamati|

nābhinandati  āyantinti purāṇadutiyikaṃ āgacchantiṃ añ‍ñaṃ vā na abhinandati cittaina na sampaṭicchati| tamaiva pak‍kamantiṃ na saucati| saṅgā saṅgāmajiṃ muttanti pañ‍cavidhāpi saṅgatau muttaṃ saṅgāmajiṃ bhikkhuṃ|

bahvaitthāti bahu aittha nhāyati janau, na taina sau suddhau nāma hautīti adhippāyau|

jātibalaṃ  nisaidhanti jātibalassa nisaidhakaṃ| sahāyā vatāti samathavipassanābhāvanāya saha ayanavasaina sahāyā vata| kālaina kālaṃ sappāyadhammassa savanavasaina cirarattaṃ samaiti samāgamau aitaisanti cirarattasamaitikā| sithilamārabbhāti sithilaṃ vīriyaṃ katvā|

105. tatra khau, bhikkhavai, kau visaisauti satthu sāvakassa ca pañ‍casvaiva upādānakkhandhaisu nibbidādayauti pubbabhāgapaṭipattiyaṃ anupādāvimuttiyañ‍ca haiṭṭhā upari ca visaisābhāvaṃ dassaiti| vuttañhaitaṃ – ‘‘natthi vimuttiyā nānatta’’nti (a॰ ni॰ 5.31; kathā॰ 355 atthatau samānaṃ)| tattha visaisābhāvaṃ pac‍cāmasati ‘‘tatra kau visaisau’’ti| adhippayāsauti adhikapayaugau| nānākaraṇanti ca visaisauyaiva vuttau|

ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, visaisauti bhikkhavai, yadipi sāvakassa satthu ca vimuttiyaṃ visaisau natthi, sayambhuñāṇaina pana savāsanasabbakilaisai khaipaitvā sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhitvā anuppan‍nassa ariyamaggassa parasantānai uppādanādisakalasabbañ‍ñuguṇasamāyaugau| ayaṃ sammāsambuddhassa pañ‍ñāvimuttatau visaisauti| tattha anuppan‍nassāti avattamānassa| ariyamaggañhi kassapasammāsambuddhau uppādaisi| antarā añ‍ñau satthā uppādaitā nāma nāhausi, tasmā ayaṃ bhagavā anuppan‍nassa maggassa uppādaitā nāma| asañ‍jātassāti tassaiva vaivacanaṃ| anakkhātassāti akathitassa| maggaṃ jānātīti maggañ‍ñū| maggaṃ viditaṃ pākaṭaṃ akāsīti maggavidū| maggai ca amaggai ca kauvidauti maggakauvidau| maggānugāti maggaṃ anugacchantā| pacchāsaman‍nāgatāti ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ saman‍nāgatau, sāvakā pacchā saman‍nāgatā|

106. ‘‘nīcai  kulai pac‍cājātau’’tiādinā (a॰ ni॰ 4.85; pu॰ pa॰ 168) tamaina yuttauti tamau| kāyaduc‍caritādīhi puna nirayatamupagamanatau tamaparāyaṇau| iti ubhayainapi khandhatamauva kathitau hauti| ‘‘aḍḍhai kulai pac‍cājātau’’tiādinā (a॰ ni॰ 4.85; pu॰ pa॰ 168) jautinā yuttauti jauti, ālaukabhūtauti vuttaṃ hauti| kāyasucaritādīhi puna saggūpapattibhavūpagamanatau jautiparāyaṇau| iminā nayaina itaraipi dvai vaiditabbā|

na taṃ daḷhaṃ bandhanamāhu dhīrāti aittha dhīrāti buddhādayau paṇḍitapurisā| yaṃ saṅkhalikasaṅkhātaṃ  ayaina nibbattaṃ āyasaṃ addubandhanasaṅkhātaṃ dārumayañ‍ca pabbajatiṇaihi raj‍juṃ katvā kataraj‍jubandhanañ‍ca, taṃ asiādīhi chindituṃ sak‍kuṇaiyyatāya ‘‘thira’’nti na vadantīti atthau| sārattarattāti rattā hutvā rattā| balavarāgarattāti atthau| maṇikuṇḍalaisūti maṇīsu ca kuṇḍalaisu ca, maṇicittaisu vā kuṇḍalaisu| aitaṃ daḷhanti yai maṇikuṇḍalaisu sārattarattā, taisu yau rāgau, yā ca puttadāraisu apaikkhā taṇhā, aitaṃ kilaisamayaṃ bandhanaṃ paṇḍitapurisā ‘‘daḷha’’nti vadanti|

auhārinanti ākaḍḍhitvā catūsu apāyaisu pātanatau avaharati haiṭṭhā haratīti auhārinaṃ| sithilanti bandhanaṭṭhānai chaviādīni akaupaitvā bandhanabhāvampi ajānāpaitvā jalapathathalapathādīsu kammaṃ kātuṃ daitīti sithilaṃ| duppamuñ‍canti laubhavasaina hi aikavārampi uppan‍naṃ kilaisabandhanaṃ daṭṭhaṭṭhānatau kacchapau viya dummaucayaṃ hautīti duppamuñ‍caṃ| aitampi chaitvānāti aitaṃ daḷhampi kilaisabandhanaṃ ñāṇakhaggaina chinditvā anapaikkhinau hutvā kāmasukhaṃ pahāya paribbajanti pak‍kamanti pabbajanti cāti atthau|
